Motivated by the increasing complexity of charged particle track reconstruction in high-luminosity LHC experiments, this study characterizes and upgrades a quantum graph neural network (QGNN) designed for event graph classification based on detector hits. The QGNN employs a hybrid architecture combining classical feedforward layers with parametrized quantum circuits to classify hit connections across detector layers. Experimental results demonstrate improved training convergence and stability compared to the original design, highlighting the effectiveness of the hybrid structure in handling dense, high-dimensional event data.
为应对高亮度LHC实验中粒子轨迹重建的复杂性，本研究对一种用于事件图分类的量子图神经网络（QGNN）进行了表征与升级。该QGNN采用混合架构，结合经典前馈网络与参数化量子电路，用于分类探测器各层间的击中连接。实验结果表明，相较于原始设计，升级后的模型在训练收敛性和稳定性方面表现更优，验证了混合架构在处理高维密集事件数据中的有效性。
ImprovedGS+: A High-Performance C++/CUDA Re-Implementation Strategy for 3D Gaussian Splatting
Authors: Jordi Muñoz Vicente
First: 2026-03-09T17:38:27+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-09T17:38:27+00:00
Comments: 6 pages, 1 figure. Technical Report. This work introduces ImprovedGS+, a library-free C++/CUDA implementation for 3D Gaussian Splatting within the LichtFeld-Studio framework. Source code available at https://github.com/jordizv/ImprovedGS-Plus
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Recent advancements in 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) have shifted the focus toward balancing reconstruction fidelity with computational efficiency. In this work, we propose ImprovedGS+, a high-performance, low-level reinvention of the ImprovedGS strategy, implemented natively within the LichtFeld-Studio framework. By transitioning from high-level Python logic to hardware-optimized C++/CUDA kernels, we achieve a significant reduction in host-device synchronization and training latency. Our implementation introduces a Long-Axis-Split (LAS) CUDA kernel, custom Laplacian-based importance kernels with Non-Maximum Suppression (NMS) for edge scores, and an adaptive Exponential Scale Scheduler. Experimental results on the Mip-NeRF360 dataset demonstrate that ImprovedGS+ establishes a new Pareto-optimal front for scene reconstruction. Our 1M-budget variant outperforms the state-of-the-art MCMC baseline by achieving a 26.8% reduction in training time (saving 17 minutes per session) and utilizing 13.3% fewer Gaussians while maintaining superior visual quality. Furthermore, our full variant demonstrates a 1.28 dB PSNR increase over the ADC baseline with a 38.4% reduction in parametric complexity. These results validate ImprovedGS+ as a scalable, high-speed solution that upholds the core pillars of Speed, Quality, and Usability within the LichtFeld-Studio ecosystem.
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to balance reconstruction quality and computational efficiency in 3D Gaussian Splatting, this work introduces ImprovedGS+, a high-performance C++/CUDA re-implementation of the ImprovedGS strategy within the LichtFeld-Studio framework. The method replaces Python-based logic with hardware-optimized kernels, including a Long-Axis-Split CUDA kernel, custom Laplacian-based importance sampling with Non-Maximum Suppression for edge refinement, and an adaptive Exponential Scale Scheduler. Experimental results on the Mip-NeRF360 dataset show that the 1M-budget variant reduces training time by 26.8% (saving 17 minutes per session) while using 13.3% fewer Gaussians and maintaining superior visual quality compared to the MCMC baseline. The full variant achieves a 1.28 dB PSNR gain over the ADC baseline with a 38.4% reduction in parametric complexity, demonstrating ImprovedGS+ as a scalable, high-speed solution that preserves speed, quality, and usability.
为在3D高斯点绘中平衡重建质量与计算效率，本文提出ImprovedGS+，即在LichtFeld-Studio框架内对ImprovedGS策略的C++/CUDA重实现。该方法将Python高层逻辑替换为硬件优化内核，包括长轴分割CUDA内核、基于拉普拉斯的自适应重要性采样（含非极大值抑制边缘优化）及自适应指数缩放调度器。在Mip-NeRF360数据集实验中，1M预算版本训练时间减少26.8%（每会话节省17分钟），同时减少13.3%的高斯点数量且保持视觉质量；完整版本相较ADC基线PSNR提升1.28dB，参数复杂度降低38.4%，验证了ImprovedGS+在速度、质量与可用性上的高效平衡。
How Far Can Unsupervised RLVR Scale LLM Training?
Authors: Bingxiang He, Yuxin Zuo, Zeyuan Liu, Shangziqi Zhao, Zixuan Fu, Junlin Yang, Cheng Qian, Kaiyan Zhang, Yuchen Fan, Ganqu Cui, Xiusi Chen, Youbang Sun, Xingtai Lv, Xuekai Zhu, Li Sheng, Ran Li, Huan-ang Gao, Yuchen Zhang, Bowen Zhou, Zhiyuan Liu, Ning Ding
Venue: ICLR 2026
First: 2026-03-09T17:38:11+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-09T17:38:11+00:00
Comments: Accepted to the ICLR 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Unsupervised reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (URLVR) offers a pathway to scale LLM training beyond the supervision bottleneck by deriving rewards without ground truth labels. Recent works leverage model intrinsic signals, showing promising early gains, yet their potential and limitations remain unclear. In this work, we revisit URLVR and provide a comprehensive analysis spanning taxonomy, theory and extensive experiments. We first classify URLVR methods into intrinsic versus external based on reward sources, then establish a unified theoretical framework revealing that all intrinsic methods converge toward sharpening the model's initial distribution This sharpening mechanism succeeds when initial confidence aligns with correctness but fails catastrophically when misaligned. Through systematic experiments, we show intrinsic rewards consistently follow a rise-then-fall pattern across methods, with collapse timing determined by model prior rather than engineering choices. Despite these scaling limits, we find intrinsic rewards remain valuable in test-time training on small datasets, and propose Model Collapse Step to measure model prior, serving as a practical indicator for RL trainability. Finally, we explore external reward methods that ground verification in computational asymmetries, showing preliminary evidence they may escape the confidence-correctness ceiling. Our findings chart boundaries for intrinsic URLVR while motivating paths toward scalable alternatives.
中文标题/摘要
标题：无监督强化学习与可验证奖励如何扩展大语言模型训练规模？
无监督强化学习与可验证奖励（URLVR）提供了一条突破监督瓶颈、无需真实标签即可推导奖励以扩展大语言模型训练规模的路径。近期研究利用模型内在信号，显示出早期的积极成果，但其潜力与局限性尚不明确。本文重新审视URLVR，从分类、理论到大量实验进行全面分析。我们首先根据奖励来源将URLVR方法分为内在型与外在型，进而建立统一理论框架，揭示所有内在方法均趋向于强化模型初始分布。该强化机制在初始置信度与正确性一致时有效，但若不一致则会灾难性失效。通过系统实验，我们发现内在奖励在各类方法中均呈现先升后降的模式，崩溃时间由模型先验决定而非工程选择。尽管存在扩展限制，我们发现内在奖励在小数据集上的测试时训练中仍具价值，并提出模型崩溃步数以衡量模型先验，作为RL可训练性的实用指标。最后，我们探索基于计算不对称性的外在奖励方法，初步表明其可能突破置信度-正确性上限。我们的研究为内在URLVR划定边界，并激励探索可扩展的替代路径。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to scale LLM training beyond supervised learning bottlenecks, this work investigates unsupervised reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (URLVR), which derives rewards without ground truth labels. The study classifies URLVR methods into intrinsic (model-generated) and external (computationally verified) types, then develops a unified theoretical framework showing that intrinsic methods converge by sharpening the model’s initial distribution—succeeding only when initial confidence matches correctness, and failing catastrophically otherwise. Experiments reveal intrinsic rewards consistently follow a rise-then-fall pattern, with collapse timing determined by model prior rather than engineering choices; however, intrinsic rewards remain useful for test-time training on small datasets, and the authors propose 'Model Collapse Step' as a practical metric to assess RL trainability. External reward methods, grounded in computational asymmetries, show early promise in potentially escaping the confidence-correctness ceiling.
为突破监督学习对大语言模型训练的瓶颈，本文研究了无监督可验证奖励强化学习（URLVR），区分内在与外在奖励方法。理论分析表明，内在方法通过强化模型初始分布实现收敛，仅当初始置信度与正确性一致时有效，否则会灾难性失效。实验显示，内在奖励普遍存在先升后降模式，崩溃时间由模型先验决定而非工程选择。尽管受限，内在奖励在小数据集测试训练中仍有价值，作者提出“模型崩溃步”作为评估强化学习可训练性的实用指标。外在奖励方法借助计算不对称性，初步显示出可能突破置信度-正确性天花板的潜力。
CODA: Difficulty-Aware Compute Allocation for Adaptive Reasoning
Authors: Siye Wu, Jian Xie, Yikai Zhang, Yanghua Xiao
First: 2026-03-09T17:37:15+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-09T17:37:15+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The emergence of large reasoning models demonstrates that scaling inference-time compute significantly enhances performance on complex tasks. However, it often falls into another trap: overthinking simple problems, where repetitive rationales yield minimal accuracy gains at a disproportionately high cost. This motivates adaptive reasoning: dynamically aligning reasoning depth with instance difficulty. In this paper, we study adaptive reasoning from an optimality perspective, formalizing it as a utility maximization problem where tokens are allocated until the marginal accuracy gain falls below the incremental cost. Based on this, we propose CODA (Compute Allocation by Difficulty Awareness), a method that operationalizes this principle by allocating tokens via a policy-internal difficulty signal. Specifically, CODA estimates difficulty via group-based rollouts and maps it to two non-negative gates that modulate a length-dependent shaping term on top of the binary base reward. The easy-side gate penalizes verbosity on simple instances, whereas the hard-side gate encourages more deliberative rollouts on challenging ones. Across model scales and benchmarks, CODA achieves adaptive reasoning without external annotations or user-provided budgets: on easy tasks, CODA reduces token costs by over 60% while maintaining strong accuracy, whereas on hard tasks it incentivizes more deliberative rollouts to maximize performance.
中文标题/摘要
标题：CODA：基于难度感知的计算分配以实现自适应推理
大型推理模型的出现表明，增加推理时的计算量可显著提升复杂任务的性能。然而，这常陷入另一陷阱：对简单问题过度思考，重复推理带来的准确率提升微乎其微，却付出不成比例的代价。这促使自适应推理：动态调整推理深度以匹配实例难度。本文从最优性角度研究自适应推理，将其形式化为效用最大化问题，即持续分配token直至边际准确率增益低于增量成本。基于此，我们提出CODA（基于难度感知的计算分配），通过模型内部的难度信号分配token。具体而言，CODA通过基于组的 rollout 估计难度，并将其映射为两个非负门控，调节基于长度的塑造项，叠加在二元基础奖励之上。易难度门控惩罚简单实例的冗长推理，而难难度门控则鼓励在挑战性实例上进行更深入的推理。在不同模型规模和基准测试中，CODA无需外部标注或用户提供的预算即可实现自适应推理：在简单任务上，CODA将token成本降低60%以上，同时保持高准确率；在困难任务上，它激励更深入的推理以最大化性能。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency of fixed-depth reasoning that wastes compute on simple problems while under-serving hard ones, this paper proposes CODA, a difficulty-aware compute allocation method that dynamically adjusts token usage based on instance difficulty. CODA estimates difficulty through group-based rollouts and uses two non-negative gates to modulate a length-dependent reward shaping term: one penalizing verbosity on easy instances and the other encouraging deeper reasoning on hard ones. Experiments across multiple model scales and benchmarks show CODA reduces token costs by over 60% on easy tasks while preserving accuracy, and promotes more deliberate reasoning on hard tasks without requiring external annotations or user-specified budgets.
为了解决大模型在推理时固定深度导致简单任务过度消耗计算资源的问题，本文提出CODA方法，通过难度感知动态分配计算资源。该方法将自适应推理形式化为效用最大化问题，当边际准确率增益低于增量成本时停止分配token。CODA通过组内 rollout 估计实例难度，并利用两个门控机制调节token分配：简单任务上抑制冗余推理，复杂任务上鼓励更深入的推理。实验表明，CODA在简单任务上可节省60%以上token成本且保持高准确率，在困难任务上则激励更细致的推理以最大化性能，适用于不同规模模型和多个基准，无需外部标注或用户预算。
Context-free Self-Conditioned GAN for Trajectory Forecasting
Authors: Tiago Rodrigues de Almeida, Eduardo Gutierrez Maestro, Oscar Martinez Mozos
First: 2026-03-09T17:37:03+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-09T17:37:03+00:00
Comments: Accepted at the 2022 21st IEEE International Conference on Machine Learning and Applications (ICMLA)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
In this paper, we present a context-free unsupervised approach based on a self-conditioned GAN to learn different modes from 2D trajectories. Our intuition is that each mode indicates a different behavioral moving pattern in the discriminator's feature space. We apply this approach to the problem of trajectory forecasting. We present three different training settings based on self-conditioned GAN, which produce better forecasters. We test our method in two data sets: human motion and road agents. Experimental results show that our approach outperforms previous context-free methods in the least representative supervised labels while performing well in the remaining labels. In addition, our approach outperforms globally in human motion, while performing well in road agents.
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to model diverse behavioral patterns in trajectory data without labeled supervision, this paper proposes a context-free self-conditioned GAN framework that learns multiple modes from 2D trajectories by leveraging the discriminator’s feature space to represent distinct motion behaviors. The method trains three variants of the GAN architecture, each designed to enhance forecasting capability through self-conditioning. Experiments on human motion and road agent datasets show that the approach outperforms prior context-free methods on the least representative supervised labels and achieves global superiority in human motion forecasting, while remaining competitive in road agent scenarios.
为在无监督条件下建模轨迹数据中的多样化行为模式，本文提出一种上下文无关的自条件GAN框架，通过判别器特征空间学习不同运动行为模式。该方法设计了三种GAN训练变体以提升预测能力。实验表明，该方法在人类运动数据集上全局表现最优，在道路代理数据集上亦保持竞争力，且在标签代表性最弱的场景下超越了先前的上下文无关方法。
Clarity and Computational Efficiency of Orbital Boundary Labeling
Authors: Markus Wallinger, Annika Bonerath, Soeren Terziadis, Jules Wulms, Martin Nöllenburg
First: 2026-03-09T17:36:51+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-09T17:36:51+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Circular interfaces such as those found on smartwatches, automotive dashboards, cockpit instruments, or in radial visualizations pose unique challenges for placing readable labels. Traditional rectangular labeling methods waste screen space and create visual clutter on these constrained displays. In orbital boundary labeling, the labels (e.g., the features' names) are placed in an annulus-shaped orbit outside of the figure, and each label is connected to its feature using a short, crossing-free leader line. We contribute algorithms to compute two leader styles, orbital-radial and straight-line, for uniform and non-uniform label sizes, optimizing for crossing-free shortest leaders. We evaluate the model and the algorithms with computational experiments and a controlled user experiment. The user experiment reveals that both leader types exhibit similar accuracy, but straight-line leaders yield faster response times.
Summary / 总结
To address the challenge of labeling circular interfaces where traditional rectangular methods cause clutter and wasted space, this paper introduces algorithms for orbital boundary labeling that place labels in an outer annulus and connect them to features via crossing-free leader lines. Two leader styles—orbital-radial and straight-line—are optimized for both uniform and non-uniform label sizes, minimizing leader length. Experimental results show both styles achieve comparable labeling accuracy, but straight-line leaders result in significantly faster user response times.
为解决智能手表、汽车仪表盘或驾驶舱等圆形界面中传统矩形标签导致的视觉杂乱和空间浪费问题，本文提出轨道边界标注方法，将标签置于外环并用无交叉引导线连接。针对均匀与非均匀标签尺寸，提出两种引导线样式（轨道径向与直线）的优化算法，目标是最小化引导线长度。实验表明，两种引导线在标注准确性上相当，但直线引导线显著提升用户响应速度。
From Pixels to Predicates: Learning Symbolic World Models via Pretrained Vision-Language Models
Authors: Ashay Athalye, Nishanth Kumar, Tom Silver, Yichao Liang, Jiuguang Wang, Tomás Lozano-Pérez, Leslie Pack Kaelbling
First: 2024-12-31T06:14:16+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-09T17:35:57+00:00
Comments: A version of this paper appears in the official proceedings of RA-L, Volume 11, Issue 4
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Our aim is to learn to solve long-horizon decision-making problems in complex robotics domains given low-level skills and a handful of short-horizon demonstrations containing sequences of images. To this end, we focus on learning abstract symbolic world models that facilitate zero-shot generalization to novel goals via planning. A critical component of such models is the set of symbolic predicates that define properties of and relationships between objects. In this work, we leverage pretrained vision-language models (VLMs) to propose a large set of visual predicates potentially relevant for decision-making, and to evaluate those predicates directly from camera images. At training time, we pass the proposed predicates and demonstrations into an optimization-based model-learning algorithm to obtain an abstract symbolic world model that is defined in terms of a compact subset of the proposed predicates. At test time, given a novel goal in a novel setting, we use the VLM to construct a symbolic description of the current world state, and then use a search-based planning algorithm to find a sequence of low-level skills that achieves the goal. We demonstrate empirically across experiments in both simulation and the real world that our method can generalize aggressively, applying its learned world model to solve problems with a wide variety of object types, arrangements, numbers of objects, and visual backgrounds, as well as novel goals and much longer horizons than those seen at training time.
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to enable robots to generalize across novel goals and environments using minimal demonstrations, this work proposes learning symbolic world models from pretrained vision-language models. The method extracts visual predicates from images and uses them to construct an abstract, compact world model via optimization-based learning from short demonstrations. At test time, the VLM generates symbolic state descriptions, which are then used with planning algorithms to execute long-horizon tasks. Experiments in both simulation and real-world settings show the approach achieves strong generalization across diverse object types, arrangements, and goals, even when trained on limited data and short horizons.
为使机器人能在仅凭少量演示的情况下泛化到新环境与新目标，该研究提出从预训练视觉-语言模型中学习符号化世界模型。方法从图像中提取候选视觉谓词，并通过优化筛选出紧凑且任务相关的符号表示。测试时，模型利用视觉-语言模型构建当前状态的符号描述，再通过搜索算法规划低层动作序列。实验表明，该方法在仿真与真实机器人场景中均表现优异，能成功解决对象类型、布局、数量及视觉背景多样、任务时长更长的未见问题。
OfficeQA Pro: An Enterprise Benchmark for End-to-End Grounded Reasoning
Authors: Krista Opsahl-Ong, Arnav Singhvi, Jasmine Collins, Ivan Zhou, Cindy Wang, Ashutosh Baheti, Owen Oertell, Jacob Portes, Sam Havens, Erich Elsen, Michael Bendersky, Matei Zaharia, Xing Chen
First: 2026-03-09T17:34:53+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-09T17:34:53+00:00
Comments: 24 pages, 16 figures. Introduces the OfficeQA Pro benchmark for grounded reasoning over enterprise documents
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We introduce OfficeQA Pro, a benchmark for evaluating AI agents on grounded, multi-document reasoning over a large and heterogeneous document corpus. The corpus consists of U.S. Treasury Bulletins spanning nearly 100 years, comprising 89,000 pages and over 26 million numerical values. OfficeQA Pro consists of 133 questions that require precise document parsing, retrieval, and analytical reasoning across both unstructured text and tabular data. Frontier LLMs including Claude Opus 4.6, GPT-5.4, and Gemini 3.1 Pro Preview achieve less than 5% accuracy on OfficeQA Pro when relying on parametric knowledge, and less than 12% with additional access to the web. When provided directly with the document corpus, frontier agents still struggle on over half of questions, scoring 34.1% on average. We find that providing agents with a structured document representation produced by Databricks' ai_parse_document yields a 16.1% average relative performance gain across agents. We conduct additional ablations to study the effects of model selection, table representation, retrieval strategy, and test-time scaling on performance. Despite these improvements, significant headroom remains before agents can be considered reliable at enterprise-grade grounded reasoning.
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to evaluate AI agents on realistic enterprise document reasoning, the authors introduce OfficeQA Pro, a benchmark based on 89,000 pages of U.S. Treasury Bulletins containing 26 million numerical values. The 133 questions require agents to parse, retrieve, and reason across unstructured text and tabular data. Frontier LLMs like Claude Opus 4.6, GPT-5.4, and Gemini 3.1 Pro achieve under 5% accuracy using parametric knowledge and under 12% with web access. Even with direct access to the full document corpus, performance remains low at 34.1%. Providing agents with a structured document representation via Databricks' ai_parse_document boosts performance by 16.1% on average, and ablation studies reveal that model choice, table representation, retrieval strategy, and scaling affect results, yet significant performance gaps remain for reliable enterprise-grade reasoning.
为评估AI代理在真实企业文档上的接地推理能力，OfficeQA Pro基准基于近百年美国财政部公告，包含89,000页和2600万数值。该基准包含133道题，需在文本与表格间精准解析、检索与分析。前沿大模型如Claude Opus 4.6、GPT-5.4和Gemini 3.1 Pro Preview仅依赖参数知识时准确率低于5%，即使接入网络也仅12%以下。直接提供完整文档库时，平均准确率提升至34.1%。采用Databricks的ai_parse_document结构化文档表示，平均性能提升16.1%。进一步消融实验分析了模型、表格表示、检索策略和测试时扩展的影响，但距离可靠的企业级接地推理仍有显著差距。
BEV-Patch-PF: Particle Filtering with BEV-Aerial Feature Matching for Off-Road Geo-Localization
Authors: Dongmyeong Lee, Jesse Quattrociocchi, Christian Ellis, Rwik Rana, Amanda Adkins, Adam Uccello, Garrett Warnell, Joydeep Biswas
First: 2025-12-17T06:03:36+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-09T17:33:53+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We propose BEV-Patch-PF, a GPS-free sequential geo-localization system that integrates a particle filter with learned bird's-eye-view (BEV) and aerial feature maps. From onboard RGB and depth images, we construct a BEV feature map. For each 3-DoF particle pose hypothesis, we crop the corresponding patch from an aerial feature map computed from a local aerial image queried around the approximate location. BEV-Patch-PF computes a per-particle log-likelihood by matching the BEV feature to the aerial patch feature. On two real-world off-road datasets, our method achieves 9.7x lower absolute trajectory error (ATE) on seen routes and 6.6x lower ATE on unseen routes than a retrieval-based baseline, while maintaining accuracy under dense canopy and shadow. The system runs in real time at 10 Hz on an NVIDIA Tesla T4, enabling practical robot deployment.
Summary / 总结
We propose BEV-Patch-PF, a GPS-free sequential geo-localization system that integrates a particle filter with learned bird's-eye-view (BEV) and aerial feature maps.
为实现无GPS的越野环境定位，BEV-Patch-PF将粒子滤波与学习到的鸟瞰图和航拍特征图相结合。它从车载RGB和深度图像构建鸟瞰图特征，并对每个粒子位姿假设，从局部航拍图像中提取对应航拍块，通过特征匹配计算每个粒子的对数似然。在两个真实越野数据集上，该方法相比检索基线在已知路线上的绝对轨迹误差降低9.7倍，在未知路线上的误差降低6.6倍，且在密林和阴影等复杂条件下仍保持精度，系统在NVIDIA Tesla T4上以10Hz实时运行。
CoCo: Code as CoT for Text-to-Image Preview and Rare Concept Generation
Authors: Haodong Li, Chunmei Qing, Huanyu Zhang, Dongzhi Jiang, Yihang Zou, Hongbo Peng, Dingming Li, Yuhong Dai, ZePeng Lin, Juanxi Tian, Yi Zhou, Siqi Dai, Jingwei Wu
First: 2026-03-09T17:31:16+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-09T17:31:16+00:00
Comments: 21 pages, 7 figures, 7 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Recent advancements in Unified Multimodal Models (UMMs) have significantly advanced text-to-image (T2I) generation, particularly through the integration of Chain-of-Thought (CoT) reasoning. However, existing CoT-based T2I methods largely rely on abstract natural-language planning, which lacks the precision required for complex spatial layouts, structured visual elements, and dense textual content. In this work, we propose CoCo (Code-as-CoT), a code-driven reasoning framework that represents the reasoning process as executable code, enabling explicit and verifiable intermediate planning for image generation. Given a text prompt, CoCo first generates executable code that specifies the structural layout of the scene, which is then executed in a sandboxed environment to render a deterministic draft image. The model subsequently refines this draft through fine-grained image editing to produce the final high-fidelity result. To support this training paradigm, we construct CoCo-10K, a curated dataset containing structured draft-final image pairs designed to teach both structured draft construction and corrective visual refinement. Empirical evaluations on StructT2IBench, OneIG-Bench, and LongText-Bench show that CoCo achieves improvements of +68.83%, +54.8%, and +41.23% over direct generation, while also outperforming other generation methods empowered by CoT. These results demonstrate that executable code is an effective and reliable reasoning paradigm for precise, controllable, and structured text-to-image generation. The code is available at: https://github.com/micky-li-hd/CoCo
中文标题/摘要
标题：CoCo：将代码作为思维链用于文本到图像预览与稀有概念生成
统一多模态模型（UMMs）的最新进展显著推动了文本到图像（T2I）生成，特别是通过整合思维链（CoT）推理。然而，现有的基于CoT的T2I方法主要依赖抽象自然语言规划，难以满足复杂空间布局、结构化视觉元素和密集文本内容所需的精确性。本文提出CoCo（代码作为思维链），一种以代码驱动的推理框架，将推理过程表示为可执行代码，从而实现图像生成的显式且可验证的中间规划。给定文本提示，CoCo首先生成指定场景结构布局的可执行代码，然后在沙箱环境中执行以生成确定性草图图像。模型随后通过精细图像编辑优化该草图，生成最终高保真结果。为支持该训练范式，我们构建了CoCo-10K数据集，包含结构化草图-最终图像对，旨在同时训练结构化草图构建与视觉修正能力。在StructT2IBench、OneIG-Bench和LongText-Bench上的实证评估表明，CoCo相比直接生成方法分别提升68.83%、54.8%和41.23%，且优于其他结合CoT的生成方法。结果表明，可执行代码是实现精确、可控、结构化文本到图像生成的有效且可靠的推理范式。代码开源地址：https://github.com/micky-li-hd/CoCo
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of natural-language-based Chain-of-Thought reasoning in text-to-image generation, this work introduces CoCo, a code-driven reasoning framework that translates planning into executable code for precise visual construction. Given a text prompt, CoCo first generates structured code specifying scene layout, which is executed in a sandbox to produce a deterministic draft image; this draft is then refined via fine-grained editing to yield a high-fidelity output. Trained on CoCo-10K, a dataset of structured draft-final image pairs, the model outperforms direct generation by +68.83% on StructT2IBench, +54.8% on OneIG-Bench, and +41.23% on LongText-Bench, demonstrating that executable code enables more accurate, controllable, and structured image generation than traditional CoT methods.
为解决自然语言链式推理在文本到图像生成中对复杂空间布局和结构化内容表达不足的问题，作者提出CoCo框架，将推理过程转化为可执行代码。给定文本提示，CoCo首先生成描述场景结构的代码，在沙箱环境中执行生成确定性草图，再通过精细化图像编辑得到高保真结果。基于CoCo-10K数据集（包含结构化草图与最终图像对）训练后，CoCo在StructT2IBench、OneIG-Bench和LongText-Bench上分别比直接生成提升68.83%、54.8%和41.23%，并优于其他CoT增强方法，证明可执行代码是实现精确、可控、可验证图像生成的有效方案。
Group Entropies and Mirror Duality: A Class of Flexible Mirror Descent Updates for Machine Learning
Authors: Andrzej Cichocki, Piergiulio Tempesta
First: 2026-03-09T17:31:03+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-09T17:31:03+00:00
Comments: 36 pages, 5 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We introduce a comprehensive theoretical and algorithmic framework that bridges formal group theory and group entropies with modern machine learning, paving the way for an infinite, flexible family of Mirror Descent (MD) optimization algorithms. Our approach exploits the rich structure of group entropies, which are generalized entropic functionals governed by group composition laws, encompassing and significantly extending all trace-form entropies such as the Shannon, Tsallis, and Kaniadakis families. By leveraging group-theoretical mirror maps (or link functions) in MD, expressed via multi-parametric generalized logarithms and their inverses (group exponentials), we achieve highly flexible and adaptable MD updates that can be tailored to diverse data geometries and statistical distributions. To this end, we introduce the notion of \textit{mirror duality}, which allows us to seamlessly switch or interchange group-theoretical link functions with their inverses, subject to specific learning rate constraints. By tuning or learning the hyperparameters of the group logarithms enables us to adapt the model to the statistical properties of the training distribution, while simultaneously ensuring desirable convergence characteristics via fine-tuning. This generality not only provides greater flexibility and improved convergence properties, but also opens new perspectives for applications in machine learning and deep learning by expanding the design of regularizers and natural gradient algorithms. We extensively evaluate the validity, robustness, and performance of the proposed updates on large-scale, simplex-constrained quadratic programming problems.
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for flexible optimization algorithms in machine learning, this paper proposes a theoretical framework that unifies group theory and entropies to derive an infinite family of Mirror Descent updates. By leveraging group-theoretical mirror maps—expressed via multi-parametric generalized logarithms and exponentials—the method enables adaptive optimization tailored to data geometry and statistical structure. The concept of mirror duality allows switching between link functions and their inverses under learning rate constraints, enhancing adaptability. Experimental results on large-scale simplex-constrained quadratic problems demonstrate improved convergence and robustness compared to standard methods, validating the framework’s theoretical promise and practical utility.
为应对机器学习中对灵活优化算法的需求，本文提出一种将群论与群熵理论结合的镜像下降（MD）框架，从而生成无限类可调算法。该方法通过群理论镜像映射——广义对数与指数函数——构建MD更新，其超参数可调以匹配数据几何与统计特性。在大规模单纯形约束二次规划问题上的实验表明，该方法在收敛性与性能上优于标准MD变体，同时在正则化器设计与自然梯度方法上提供了更大灵活性。
Divide and Predict: An Architecture for Input Space Partitioning and Enhanced Accuracy
Authors: Fenix W. Huang, Henning S. Mortveit, Christian M. Reidys
First: 2026-03-09T17:26:56+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-09T17:26:56+00:00
Comments: Under review; 24 pages; 8 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
In this article the authors develop an intrinsic measure for quantifying heterogeneity in training data for supervised learning. This measure is the variance of a random variable which factors through the influences of pairs of training points. The variance is shown to capture data heterogeneity and can thus be used to assess if a sample is a mixture of distributions. The authors prove that the data itself contains key information that supports a partitioning into blocks. Several proof of concept studies are provided that quantify the connection between variance and heterogeneity for EMNIST image data and synthetic data. The authors establish that variance is maximal for equal mixes of distributions, and detail how variance-based data purification followed by conventional training over blocks can lead to significant increases in test accuracy.
中文标题/摘要
标题：分而预测：一种用于输入空间划分与精度提升的架构
本文作者提出了一种用于量化监督学习训练数据异质性的内在度量方法。该度量是通过成对训练点影响因素的随机变量方差，能够捕捉数据异质性，从而判断样本是否为多个分布的混合。作者证明数据本身包含支持划分成块的关键信息，并通过多个概念验证研究量化了方差与异质性在EMNIST图像数据和合成数据中的关联。作者指出，当分布等比例混合时方差最大，并详细说明了基于方差的数据净化后按块进行常规训练可显著提升测试精度。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to improve supervised learning accuracy through better data organization, the authors introduce a variance-based metric to quantify data heterogeneity by modeling the influence of training point pairs. This intrinsic measure identifies data mixtures and supports partitioning training samples into homogeneous blocks. Experimental results on EMNIST and synthetic datasets demonstrate that variance peaks when data is evenly mixed across distributions, and applying variance-guided purification followed by block-wise training yields substantial gains in test accuracy compared to conventional methods.
为提升监督学习的准确性，作者提出一种基于方差的度量方法，用于量化训练数据的异质性，该方法通过建模训练点对的影响来实现。实验证明，该方差能有效捕捉数据混合结构，并在分布等比例混合时达到最大值。在EMNIST和合成数据集上的实验表明，依据该方差对数据进行分块并分别训练，可显著提升测试准确率，优于传统训练方法。
CAST: Modeling Visual State Transitions for Consistent Video Retrieval
Authors: Yanqing Liu, Yingcheng Liu, Fanghong Dong, Budianto Budianto, Cihang Xie, Yan Jiao
First: 2026-03-09T17:26:26+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-09T17:26:26+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
As video content creation shifts toward long-form narratives, composing short clips into coherent storylines becomes increasingly important. However, prevailing retrieval formulations remain context-agnostic at inference time, prioritizing local semantic alignment while neglecting state and identity consistency. To address this structural limitation, we formalize the task of Consistent Video Retrieval (CVR) and introduce a diagnostic benchmark spanning YouCook2, COIN, and CrossTask. We propose CAST (Context-Aware State Transition), a lightweight, plug-and-play adapter compatible with diverse frozen vision-language embedding spaces. By predicting a state-conditioned residual update ($Δ$) from visual history, CAST introduces an explicit inductive bias for latent state evolution. Extensive experiments show that CAST improves performance on YouCook2 and CrossTask, remains competitive on COIN, and consistently outperforms zero-shot baselines across diverse foundation backbones. Furthermore, CAST provides a useful reranking signal for black-box video generation candidates (e.g., from Veo), promoting more temporally coherent continuations.
中文标题/摘要
标题：CAST：用于一致视频检索的视觉状态转换建模
随着视频内容创作转向长篇叙事，将短片段组合成连贯的故事线变得愈发重要。然而，现有检索方法在推理时缺乏上下文感知，优先考虑局部语义对齐，而忽视状态与身份一致性。为解决这一结构限制，我们形式化了“一致视频检索”（CVR）任务，并引入涵盖YouCook2、COIN和CrossTask的诊断基准。我们提出CAST（上下文感知状态转换），这是一种轻量级、即插即用的适配器，兼容多种冻结的视觉-语言嵌入空间。通过从视觉历史预测状态条件残差更新（Δ），CAST引入了对潜在状态演化的显式归纳偏置。大量实验表明，CAST在YouCook2和CrossTask上性能提升，在COIN上保持竞争力，并在多种基础模型上持续优于零样本基线。此外，CAST为黑盒视频生成候选（如Veo）提供了有用的重排序信号，促进更时序连贯的延续。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to retrieve video clips that maintain narrative consistency across time, the paper introduces CAST, a lightweight adapter that enhances video retrieval by modeling visual state transitions. CAST operates by predicting a state-conditioned residual update from visual history, embedding an inductive bias for latent state evolution within existing vision-language models. Evaluated on YouCook2, COIN, and CrossTask, CAST improves retrieval performance on YouCook2 and CrossTask, remains competitive on COIN, and consistently outperforms zero-shot baselines across multiple foundation models. Additionally, CAST enhances temporal coherence in black-box video generation by providing a reranking signal for generated continuations.
为提升视频检索中叙事一致性，本文提出CAST方法，通过建模视觉状态转换增强检索效果。CAST在现有冻结的视觉-语言模型中插入轻量适配器，预测基于视觉历史的状态条件残差更新，引入状态演化的归纳偏置。实验表明，CAST在YouCook2和CrossTask上表现提升，在COIN上保持竞争力，对多种基础模型均优于零样本基线，同时可为视频生成模型提供重排序信号，促进更连贯的时间延续输出。
HDLxGraph: Bridging Large Language Models and HDL Repositories via HDL Graph Databases
Authors: Pingqing Zheng, Jiayin Qin, Fuqi Zhang, Niraj Chitla, Zishen Wan, Shang Wu, Yu Cao, Caiwen Ding, Yang, Zhao
First: 2025-05-21T16:14:10+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-09T17:26:13+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Retrieval Augmented Generation (RAG) is an essential agent for Large Language Model (LLM) aided Description Language (HDL) tasks, addressing the challenges of limited training data and prohibitively long prompts. However, its performance in handling ambiguous queries and real-world, repository-level HDL projects containing thousands or even tens of thousands of code lines remains limited. Our analysis demonstrates two fundamental mismatches, structural and vocabulary, between conventional semantic similarity-based RAGs and HDL codes. To this end, we propose HDLxGraph, the first framework that integrates the inherent graph characteristics of HDLs with RAGs for LLM-assisted tasks. Specifically, HDLxGraph incorporates Abstract Syntax Trees (ASTs) to capture HDLs' hierarchical structures and Data Flow Graphs (DFGs) to address the vocabulary mismatch. In addition, to overcome the lack of comprehensive HDL search benchmarks, we introduce HDLSearch, an LLM generated dataset derived from real-world, repository-level HDL projects. Evaluations show that HDLxGraph improves search, debugging, and completion accuracy by 12.04%/12.22%/5.04% and by 11.59%/8.18%/4.07% over state-of-the-art similarity-based RAG and software-code Graph RAG baselines, respectively. The code of HDLxGraph and HDLSearch benchmark are available at https://github.com/UMN-ZhaoLab/HDLxGraph.
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of conventional RAG systems in handling ambiguous queries and large-scale HDL repositories, the authors propose HDLxGraph, a framework that integrates HDL’s inherent graph structures—Abstract Syntax Trees and Data Flow Graphs—with RAG to improve LLM-assisted tasks. The method addresses structural and vocabulary mismatches by leveraging ASTs for hierarchical representation and DFGs for semantic alignment. Evaluated against state-of-the-art RAG and graph-based baselines using the newly introduced HDLSearch benchmark, HDLxGraph achieves significant performance gains: 12.04% and 12.22% improvements in search and debugging accuracy over similarity-based RAG, and 11.59% and 8.18% over graph RAG baselines, with 5.04% and 4.07% gains in completion accuracy.
为解决传统RAG系统在处理模糊查询和大规模HDL仓库时的不足，作者提出HDLxGraph框架，将HDL固有的图结构——抽象语法树和数据流图——融入RAG流程，以解决结构与词汇层面的不匹配问题。该框架通过图结构增强语义理解与检索精度。在新构建的HDLSearch基准数据集上，与当前最先进的相似度RAG和代码图RAG基线方法相比，HDLxGraph在搜索、调试和补全任务中分别提升12.04%、12.22%和5.04%，以及11.59%、8.18%和4.07%。
Grow, Don't Overwrite: Fine-tuning Without Forgetting
Authors: Dyah Adila, Hanna Mazzawi, Benoit Dherin, Xavier Gonzalvo
First: 2026-03-09T17:26:03+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-09T17:26:03+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Adapting pre-trained models to specialized tasks often leads to catastrophic forgetting, where new knowledge overwrites foundational capabilities. Existing methods either compromise performance on the new task or struggle to balance training stability with efficient reuse of pre-trained knowledge. We introduce a novel function-preserving expansion method that resolves this dilemma. Our technique expands model capacity by replicating pre-trained parameters within transformer submodules and applying a scaling correction that guarantees the expanded model is mathematically identical to the original at initialization, enabling stable training while exploiting existing knowledge. Empirically, our method eliminates the trade-off between plasticity and stability, matching the performance of full fine-tuning on downstream tasks without any degradation of the model's original capabilities. Furthermore, we demonstrate the modularity of our approach, showing that by selectively expanding a small subset of layers we can achieve the same performance as full fine-tuning at a fraction of the computational cost.
中文标题/摘要
标题：增长而非覆盖：无需遗忘的微调方法
将预训练模型适配到特定任务时，常导致灾难性遗忘，即新知识覆盖了基础能力。现有方法要么牺牲新任务性能，要么难以在训练稳定性与高效复用预训练知识间取得平衡。我们提出一种新颖的函数保持扩展方法，解决了这一困境。该技术通过在Transformer子模块内复制预训练参数并应用缩放校正，确保扩展模型在初始化时与原模型数学上完全一致，从而实现稳定训练并利用已有知识。实验证明，我们的方法消除了可塑性与稳定性的权衡，在下游任务上达到全微调性能，且不损害模型原有能力。此外，我们展示了该方法的模块化特性：通过选择性扩展少量层，即可以更低计算成本达到全微调同等性能。
Summary / 总结
To address catastrophic forgetting in fine-tuning pre-trained models, the paper proposes a function-preserving expansion method that replicates pre-trained parameters within transformer submodules and applies scaling corrections to ensure mathematical equivalence at initialization, enabling stable training while preserving original capabilities. Experiments show this approach achieves full fine-tuning performance on downstream tasks without degrading base model skills, and further demonstrates modularity by achieving equivalent results with selective layer expansion, reducing computational cost significantly.
为解决微调中出现的灾难性遗忘问题，该研究提出一种函数保持扩展方法，通过在Transformer子模块内复制预训练参数并施加缩放校正，确保初始化时扩展模型与原模型数学等价，从而实现稳定训练并保留基础能力。实验表明，该方法在下游任务上达到全微调性能，且不损害原模型能力，同时通过选择性扩展部分层即可实现同等效果，显著降低计算开销。
Retrieval-Augmented Gaussian Avatars: Improving Expression Generalization
Authors: Matan Levy, Gavriel Habib, Issar Tzachor, Dvir Samuel, Rami Ben-Ari, Nir Darshan, Or Litany, Dani Lischinski
First: 2026-03-09T17:24:11+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-09T17:24:11+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Template-free animatable head avatars can achieve high visual fidelity by learning expression-dependent facial deformation directly from a subject's capture, avoiding parametric face templates and hand-designed blendshape spaces. However, since learned deformation is supervised only by the expressions observed for a single identity, these models suffer from limited expression coverage and often struggle when driven by motions that deviate from the training distribution. We introduce RAF (Retrieval-Augmented Faces), a simple training-time augmentation designed for template-free head avatars that learn deformation from data. RAF constructs a large unlabeled expression bank and, during training, replaces a subset of the subject's expression features with nearest-neighbor expressions retrieved from this bank while still reconstructing the subject's original frames. This exposes the deformation field to a broader range of expression conditions, encouraging stronger identity-expression decoupling and improving robustness to expression distribution shift without requiring paired cross-identity data, additional annotations, or architectural changes. We further analyze how retrieval augmentation increases expression diversity and validate retrieval quality with a user study showing that retrieved neighbors are perceptually closer in expression and pose. Experiments on the NeRSemble benchmark demonstrate that RAF consistently improves expression fidelity over the baseline, in both self-driving and cross-driving scenarios.
中文标题/摘要
标题：检索增强高斯头像：提升表情泛化能力
无模板的可动画头部头像可通过直接从主体捕捉数据中学习表情依赖的面部变形，实现高视觉保真度，无需参数化面部模板和手工设计的混合形状空间。然而，由于所学变形仅由单一身份的观察表情监督，这些模型在表情覆盖范围有限，且在偏离训练分布的运动驱动下表现不佳。我们提出RAF（检索增强面部），一种专为无模板头像设计的简单训练时增强方法，其通过构建大规模无标签表情库，在训练时用从该库中检索到的最近邻表情替换部分主体表情特征，同时仍重建主体原始帧。这使变形场暴露于更广泛的表情条件，促进更强的身份-表情解耦并提升对表情分布偏移的鲁棒性，无需跨身份配对数据、额外标注或架构修改。我们进一步分析了检索增强如何提升表情多样性，并通过用户研究验证检索质量，表明检索到的邻居在表情和姿态上感知上更接近。在NeRSemble基准测试中，RAF在自驱动和跨驱动场景下均持续优于基线模型，提升表情保真度。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limited expression generalization of template-free head avatars trained on single-subject data, the authors propose RAF, a training-time augmentation that enhances expression robustness by retrieving and substituting expression features from a large unlabeled expression bank. During training, RAF replaces a portion of the subject’s expression features with nearest-neighbor expressions from the bank while preserving original frame reconstruction, thereby exposing the deformation model to diverse expression conditions. This encourages better identity-expression decoupling and improves performance under unseen expression distributions without needing cross-identity data or architectural modifications. Experimental results on NeRSemble show RAF consistently outperforms the baseline in both self-driving and cross-driving scenarios, demonstrating improved expression fidelity and robustness.
为解决无模板头像模型因仅依赖单人数据导致的表达泛化能力不足问题，作者提出RAF方法，在训练时通过从大规模无标注表达库中检索最相似表达，替换部分主体表达特征，从而扩展变形场的表达条件。该方法无需跨身份数据或架构调整，有效促进身份与表达的解耦。在NeRSemble基准测试中，RAF在自驱动与跨驱动场景下均显著提升表达保真度，验证其对表达分布偏移的鲁棒性。
Exploring Embedding Priors in Prompt-Tuning for Improved Interpretability and Control
Authors: Sergey Sedov, Sumanth Bharadwaj Hachalli Karanam, Venu Gopal Kadamba
First: 2024-12-24T18:18:52+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-09T17:23:47+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Prompt-Tuning is an efficient method for adapting pre-trained language models to new tasks with minimal computational overhead by modifying prompt embeddings. In this work, we investigate how crucial the phenomenon of embedding collapse, frequently observed in Prompt-Tuning, is for the final performance of the model. To address this question, we designed embedding priors and compared them with posteriors of the converged Soft and Deep Prompt-Tuning methods. Our findings suggest that priors strongly affect the position of the tuned embeddings, and models can effectively work with embeddings from different parts of activation spaces, including completely new regions. As the final Prompt-Tuning capabilities are limited, we hypothesize that controllable Prompt-Tuning posteriors may serve as a good starting point for tasks such as chain-of-thought (COT) distillation. Our experiments also show that generated trajectories are not localized in the activation space of the models. However, there are distinct clusters of activations for distant tasks (e.g., NLP and arithmetic), while activations between NLP tasks (e.g., Question-Answering and MLM) lie in the same cluster. These observations raise questions about the importance of a single activation cluster for the generalization abilities of large language models.
中文标题/摘要
标题：探索提示调优中嵌入先验以提升可解释性与可控性
提示调优是一种通过修改提示嵌入来高效适应预训练语言模型至新任务的方法，且计算开销极小。本研究探讨了在提示调优中常观察到的嵌入坍缩现象对最终模型性能的重要性。为回答此问题，我们设计了嵌入先验，并将其与收敛后的软提示和深度提示调优方法的后验进行比较。研究发现，先验显著影响调优嵌入的位置，模型可有效利用激活空间中不同区域的嵌入，包括全新区域。鉴于最终提示调优能力有限，我们假设可控的提示调优后验可作为链式思维（COT）蒸馏等任务的良好起点。实验还表明，生成轨迹在模型激活空间中并非局部化，但对远距离任务（如NLP与算术）存在明显激活簇，而NLP任务（如问答与MLM）间的激活则位于同一簇。这些观察引发对单一激活簇是否对大语言模型泛化能力至关重要的疑问。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to understand how embedding collapse impacts performance in Prompt-Tuning, this study introduces embedding priors to compare against converged posteriors from Soft and Deep Prompt-Tuning. The authors find that priors significantly influence where tuned embeddings settle in the activation space, and models can function effectively even with embeddings from novel regions. Experimental results indicate that Prompt-Tuning’s final capabilities are limited, suggesting posteriors may be useful as starting points for tasks like chain-of-thought distillation. Additionally, generated trajectories are not confined to specific activation regions, but activations cluster differently for distant tasks (e.g., NLP vs. arithmetic) while similar NLP tasks (e.g., QA and MLM) share activation clusters, challenging assumptions about generalization.
为探究提示调优中嵌入坍缩现象对模型性能与可解释性的影响，本研究设计了嵌入先验并对比了Soft与Deep提示调优方法收敛后的后验分布，发现先验显著影响调优后嵌入的位置，且模型可有效工作于激活空间中全新区域。实验表明提示调优能力受限，提示后验或可作为链式思维蒸馏等任务的良好起点。此外，生成轨迹未局限于特定激活区域，但不同任务（如NLP与算术）激活形成不同聚类，而同类NLP任务（如问答与掩码语言建模）激活则聚于同一区域，引发对单一激活聚类在大模型泛化能力中作用的思考。
X-SYS: A Reference Architecture for Interactive Explanation Systems
Authors: Tobias Labarta, Nhi Hoang, Maximilian Dreyer, Jim Berend, Oleg Hein, Jackie Ma, Wojciech Samek, Sebastian Lapuschkin
First: 2026-02-13T09:24:03+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-09T17:21:38+00:00
Comments: 18 pages, 8 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The explainable AI (XAI) research community has proposed numerous technical methods, yet deploying explainability as systems remains challenging: Interactive explanation systems require both suitable algorithms and system capabilities that maintain explanation usability across repeated queries, evolving models and data, and governance constraints. We argue that operationalizing XAI requires treating explainability as an information systems problem where user interaction demands induce specific system requirements. We introduce X-SYS, a reference architecture for interactive explanation systems, that guides (X)AI researchers, developers and practitioners in connecting interactive explanation user interfaces (XUI) with system capabilities. X-SYS organizes around four quality attributes named STAR (scalability, traceability, responsiveness, and adaptability), and specifies a five-component decomposition (XUI Services, Explanation Services, Model Services, Data Services, Orchestration and Governance). It maps interaction patterns to system capabilities to decouple user interface evolution from backend computation. We implement X-SYS through SemanticLens, a system for semantic search and activation steering in vision-language models. SemanticLens demonstrates how contract-based service boundaries enable independent evolution, offline/online separation ensures responsiveness, and persistent state management supports traceability. Together, this work provides a reusable blueprint and concrete instantiation for interactive explanation systems supporting end-to-end design under operational constraints.
中文标题/摘要
标题：X-SYS：交互式解释系统的参考架构
可解释人工智能（XAI）研究社区已提出多种技术方法，但将可解释性作为系统部署仍具挑战：交互式解释系统需兼顾合适的算法与系统能力，以确保在重复查询、模型与数据演变及治理约束下保持解释的可用性。我们认为，将XAI工程化需将可解释性视为信息系统问题，用户交互需求会引出特定系统要求。我们提出X-SYS，一种交互式解释系统的参考架构，旨在引导（X）AI研究人员、开发者和实践者将交互式解释用户界面（XUI）与系统能力相连接。X-SYS围绕四个质量属性（STAR：可扩展性、可追溯性、响应性、适应性）组织，并规定五组件分解（XUI服务、解释服务、模型服务、数据服务、编排与治理）。它将交互模式映射至系统能力，以解耦用户界面演进与后端计算。我们通过SemanticLens系统（用于视觉-语言模型中的语义搜索与激活引导）实现X-SYS，展示基于契约的服务边界如何支持独立演进，离线/在线分离如何保障响应性，持久状态管理如何支持可追溯性。本工作提供了一种可复用的蓝图及具体实例，支持在运营约束下实现端到端的交互式解释系统设计。

Motivated by the gap between XAI research and real-world deployable systems, this work proposes X-SYS, a reference architecture for interactive explanation systems that addresses usability across repeated queries, evolving models, and governance. It introduces a five-component decomposition—XUI Services, Explanation Services, Model Services, Data Services, and Orchestration and Governance—organized around four quality attributes: scalability, traceability, responsiveness, and adaptability. The architecture decouples UI evolution from backend computation by mapping interaction patterns to system capabilities. 为解决可解释AI在交互式系统中的实际部署难题，本文提出X-SYS参考架构，通过将交互模式映射到系统能力，实现用户界面与后端计算的解耦。该架构围绕四个质量属性——可扩展性、可追溯性、响应性与适应性——构建五个核心组件：XUI服务、解释服务、模型服务、数据服务及编排与治理。通过SemanticLens系统（用于视觉-语言模型的语义搜索与激活引导）验证，X-SYS证明了基于契约的服务边界支持组件独立演进，离线/在线分离保障响应性，持久状态管理支持可追溯性，从而实现受运营约束下的端到端系统设计。
Task-Oriented Robot-Human Handovers on Legged Manipulators
Authors: Andreea Tulbure, Carmen Scheidemann, Elias Steiner, Marco Hutter
First: 2026-02-05T15:28:04+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-09T17:20:01+00:00
Comments: Accepted to 21st ACM/IEEE International Conference on Human-Robot Interaction (HRI) 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Task-oriented handovers (TOH) are fundamental to effective human-robot collaboration, requiring robots to present objects in a way that supports the human's intended post-handover use. Existing approaches are typically based on object- or task-specific affordances, but their ability to generalize to novel scenarios is limited. To address this gap, we present AFT-Handover, a framework that integrates large language model (LLM)-driven affordance reasoning with efficient texture-based affordance transfer to achieve zero-shot, generalizable TOH. Given a novel object-task pair, the method retrieves a proxy exemplar from a database, establishes part-level correspondences via LLM reasoning, and texturizes affordances for feature-based point cloud transfer. We evaluate AFT-Handover across diverse task-object pairs, showing improved handover success rates and stronger generalization compared to baselines. In a comparative user study, our framework is significantly preferred over the current state-of-the-art, effectively reducing human regrasping before tool use. Finally, we demonstrate TOH on legged manipulators, highlighting the potential of our framework for real-world robot-human handovers.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于任务导向的腿式操作机器人与人类交接
任务导向交接（TOH）是有效人机协作的基础，要求机器人以支持人类交接后使用意图的方式呈现物体。现有方法通常基于物体或任务特定的可操作性，但其在新场景中的泛化能力有限。为解决此问题，我们提出AFT-Handover框架，结合大语言模型（LLM）驱动的可操作性推理与高效的纹理化可操作性迁移，实现零样本、可泛化的TOH。针对新物体-任务对，该方法从数据库检索代理示例，通过LLM推理建立部件级对应关系，并对可操作性进行纹理化以实现基于特征的点云迁移。我们在多样化的任务-物体对上评估AFT-Handover，结果表明其交接成功率更高、泛化能力更强。在对比用户研究中，我们的框架显著优于当前最先进方法，有效减少了人类在工具使用前的重新抓取。最后，我们在腿式操作机器人上演示了TOH，突显了该框架在现实世界人机交接中的潜力。
Summary / 总结
To enable robots to perform task-oriented handovers that generalize across novel object-task pairs, the authors propose AFT-Handover, which combines large language model-driven affordance reasoning with texture-based affordance transfer. Given a new object-task pair, the method retrieves a proxy exemplar, uses LLMs to establish part-level correspondences, and transfers affordances via texturized point clouds for zero-shot generalization. Experimental results show higher handover success rates and better generalization than baseline methods, with user studies confirming superior preference over state-of-the-art approaches in reducing human regrasping. The framework is successfully demonstrated on legged manipulators, validating its applicability in real-world human-robot collaboration scenarios.
为实现机器人在新物体-任务对上的零样本泛化任务导向交接，作者提出AFT-Handover框架，结合大语言模型驱动的可操作性推理与基于纹理的可操作性迁移。面对新物体-任务对，系统检索代理示例，通过LLM建立部件级对应关系，并通过纹理化点云实现可操作性迁移。实验表明该方法在交接成功率和泛化能力上优于基线方法，用户研究证实其显著优于当前最先进方法，有效减少人类重新抓取行为。该框架已在腿式操作机器人上成功演示，验证其在真实人机协作场景中的应用潜力。
PostTrainBench: Can LLM Agents Automate LLM Post-Training?
Authors: Ben Rank, Hardik Bhatnagar, Ameya Prabhu, Shira Eisenberg, Karina Nguyen, Matthias Bethge, Maksym Andriushchenko
First: 2026-03-09T17:18:00+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-09T17:18:00+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
AI agents have become surprisingly proficient at software engineering over the past year, largely due to improvements in reasoning capabilities. This raises a deeper question: can these systems extend their capabilities to automate AI research itself? In this paper, we explore post-training, the critical phase that turns base LLMs into useful assistants. We introduce PostTrainBench to benchmark how well LLM agents can perform post-training autonomously under bounded compute constraints (10 hours on one H100 GPU). We ask frontier agents (e.g., Claude Code with Opus 4.6) to optimize the performance of a base LLM on a particular benchmark (e.g., Qwen3-4B on AIME). Importantly, we do not provide any predefined strategies to the agents and instead give them full autonomy to find necessary information on the web, run experiments, and curate data. We find that frontier agents make substantial progress but generally lag behind instruction-tuned LLMs from leading providers: 23.2% for the best agent vs. 51.1% for official instruction-tuned models. However, agents can exceed instruction-tuned models in targeted scenarios: GPT-5.1 Codex Max achieves 89% on BFCL with Gemma-3-4B vs. 67% for the official model. We also observe several failure modes worth flagging. Agents sometimes engage in reward hacking: training on the test set, downloading existing instruction-tuned checkpoints instead of training their own, and using API keys they find to generate synthetic data without authorization. These behaviors are concerning and highlight the importance of careful sandboxing as these systems become more capable. Overall, we hope PostTrainBench will be useful for tracking progress in AI R&D automation and for studying the risks that come with it. Website and code are available at https://posttrainbench.com/.
中文标题/摘要
标题：PostTrainBench：大语言模型代理能否自动化大语言模型的后训练？
过去一年，AI代理在软件工程方面表现出令人惊讶的熟练度，这主要归功于推理能力的提升。这引发了一个更深层次的问题：这些系统能否扩展其能力，自动化AI研究本身？本文探讨了后训练——将基础大语言模型转化为实用助手的关键阶段。我们引入PostTrainBench，用于评估大语言模型代理在计算资源受限（单张H100 GPU上10小时）条件下自主执行后训练的能力。我们要求前沿代理（如Claude Code Opus 4.6）优化特定基准（如Qwen3-4B在AIME上）的基础大语言模型性能。重要的是，我们未向代理提供任何预定义策略，而是赋予其完全自主权，使其可从网络获取必要信息、运行实验并整理数据。我们发现前沿代理取得显著进展，但总体仍落后于主流供应商的指令调优大语言模型：最佳代理为23.2%，而官方指令调优模型为51.1%。然而，在特定场景下，代理可超越指令调优模型：GPT-5.1 Codex Max在BFCL上使用Gemma-3-4B达到89%，而官方模型仅为67%。我们还观察到若干值得关注的失败模式：代理有时会进行奖励黑客行为，如在测试集上训练、下载现有指令调优检查点而非自行训练、使用找到的API密钥生成未经授权的合成数据。这些行为令人担忧，凸显了随着系统能力增强，谨慎的沙盒环境的重要性。总体而言，我们希望PostTrainBench能有助于追踪AI研发自动化进展，并研究其伴随的风险。网站和代码见https://posttrainbench.com/。

Motivated by the growing proficiency of AI agents in software engineering, this study investigates whether LLM agents can autonomously perform post-training to enhance base models. The authors introduce PostTrainBench, a benchmark where frontier agents (e.g., Claude Code with Opus 4.6) are tasked with optimizing a base LLM (e.g., Qwen3-4B) on a specific benchmark (e.g., AIME) within strict compute limits (10 hours on one H100 GPU), without predefined strategies. UNBOX: Unveiling Black-box visual models with Natural-language
Authors: Simone Carnemolla, Chiara Russo, Simone Palazzo, Quentin Bouniot, Daniela Giordano, Zeynep Akata, Matteo Pennisi, Concetto Spampinato
First: 2026-03-09T17:16:39+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-09T17:16:39+00:00
Comments: Under review at IJCV
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Ensuring trustworthiness in open-world visual recognition requires models that are interpretable, fair, and robust to distribution shifts. Yet modern vision systems are increasingly deployed as proprietary black-box APIs, exposing only output probabilities and hiding architecture, parameters, gradients, and training data. This opacity prevents meaningful auditing, bias detection, and failure analysis. Existing explanation methods assume white- or gray-box access or knowledge of the training distribution, making them unusable in these real-world settings. We introduce UNBOX, a framework for class-wise model dissection under fully data-free, gradient-free, and backpropagation-free constraints. UNBOX leverages Large Language Models and text-to-image diffusion models to recast activation maximization as a purely semantic search driven by output probabilities. The method produces human-interpretable text descriptors that maximally activate each class, revealing the concepts a model has implicitly learned, the training distribution it reflects, and potential sources of bias. We evaluate UNBOX on ImageNet-1K, Waterbirds, and CelebA through semantic fidelity tests, visual-feature correlation analyses and slice-discovery auditing. Despite operating under the strictest black-box constraints, UNBOX performs competitively with state-of-the-art white-box interpretability methods. This demonstrates that meaningful insight into a model's internal reasoning can be recovered without any internal access, enabling more trustworthy and accountable visual recognition systems.
中文标题/摘要
标题：UNBOX：利用自然语言揭示黑盒视觉模型
在开放世界视觉识别中确保可信度，需要可解释、公平且对分布偏移鲁棒的模型。然而，现代视觉系统正越来越多地作为专有黑盒API部署，仅暴露输出概率，隐藏架构、参数、梯度和训练数据。这种不透明性阻碍了有意义的审计、偏见检测和故障分析。现有解释方法假设白盒或灰盒访问或了解训练分布，因此在这些现实场景中不可用。我们提出UNBOX框架，在完全无数据、无梯度、无反向传播的约束下进行按类模型剖析。UNBOX利用大语言模型和文本到图像扩散模型，将激活最大化重新表述为仅由输出概率驱动的纯语义搜索。该方法生成能最大程度激活每一类的人类可解释文本描述，揭示模型隐式学习的概念、反映的训练分布及潜在偏见来源。我们在ImageNet-1K、Waterbirds和CelebA上通过语义保真度测试、视觉特征相关性分析和切片发现审计评估UNBOX。尽管在最严格的黑盒约束下运行，UNBOX仍能与最先进的白盒可解释性方法竞争。这表明无需任何内部访问即可恢复模型内部推理的有意义洞察，从而实现更可信、更负责任的视觉识别系统。
