Motivated by the need to audit and understand black-box visual models deployed in real-world settings without access to internal parameters or gradients, UNBOX introduces a data-free, gradient-free, and backpropagation-free framework that leverages Large Language Models and text-to-image diffusion models to perform class-wise model dissection. By recasting activation maximization as a semantic search guided by output probabilities, UNBOX generates human-interpretable text descriptors that reveal the implicit concepts, training distribution, and potential biases a model has learned. Evaluated on ImageNet-1K, Waterbirds, and CelebA using semantic fidelity, visual-feature correlation, and slice-discovery metrics, UNBOX achieves performance competitive with state-of-the-art white-box methods, demonstrating that meaningful interpretability can be recovered without any internal access.
为了解决在无内部参数、梯度或反向传播访问权限的黑盒视觉模型中进行审计和理解的需求，UNBOX提出了一种无需数据、无需梯度、无需反向传播的框架，结合大语言模型与文本到图像扩散模型，通过输出概率驱动的语义搜索实现按类别模型剖析。该方法生成可被人理解的文本描述，揭示模型隐含学习的概念、训练分布及潜在偏见。在ImageNet-1K、Waterbirds和CelebA数据集上，通过语义保真度、视觉特征相关性及切片发现评估，UNBOX表现与当前最先进的白盒可解释方法相当，证明无需内部访问即可获得模型内部推理的有意义洞察。
Reachability-based Temporal Logic Verification for Reliable LLM-guided Human-Autonomy Teaming
Authors: Joonwon Choi, Kartik Anand Pant, Karthik Nune, Inseok Hwang
First: 2026-03-09T17:10:09+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-09T17:10:09+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We propose a reachability-based framework for reliable LLM-guided human-autonomy teaming (HAT) using signal temporal logic (STL). In the proposed framework, LLM is leveraged as a translator that transfers natural language commands given by a human operator into corresponding STL specifications or vice versa. An STL feasibility filter (SFF) is proposed to check the feasibility of the generated STL. The SFF first decomposes the complex and nested LLM translation into a set of simpler subformulas for parallelization and informative feedback generation. The reachability analysis method is then applied to verify if each subformula is feasible for a target dynamical system: if feasible, perform mission planning, otherwise, reject it. The proposed SFF can identify infeasible subformulas, more than simply providing the boolean verification results for the whole STL, thereby facilitating the feedback generation of LLM to request modification of the command to the human. Consequently, the proposed framework can allow more reliable HAT by enabling safe and informative communication between the human operator and the autonomous agent. Our experiments demonstrate that the proposed framework can successfully filter out infeasible subformulas and generate informative feedback based on such information.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于可达性的时序逻辑验证用于可靠的大语言模型引导的人机协同
我们提出了一种基于可达性的框架，用于可靠的大语言模型（LLM）引导的人机协同（HAT），该框架使用信号时序逻辑（STL）。在该框架中，LLM作为翻译器，将人类操作员给出的自然语言指令转换为相应的STL规范，或反之。我们提出了一种STL可行性过滤器（SFF），用于检查生成的STL的可行性。SFF首先将复杂的嵌套LLM翻译分解为一组更简单的子公式，以实现并行化和生成有信息量的反馈。随后应用可达性分析方法，验证每个子公式是否适用于目标动态系统：若可行，则执行任务规划；否则，拒绝该子公式。所提出的SFF不仅能提供整个STL的布尔验证结果，还能识别不可行的子公式，从而促进LLM生成反馈，请求人类修改指令。因此，该框架通过实现人类操作员与自主代理之间的安全且有信息量的通信，可实现更可靠的人机协同。实验表明，该框架能成功过滤不可行子公式，并基于此信息生成有信息量的反馈。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for safe and reliable human-autonomy teaming guided by large language models, this work proposes a reachability-based framework using signal temporal logic (STL) to translate natural language commands into formal specifications. The method employs an STL feasibility filter (SFF) that decomposes complex LLM-generated STL formulas into simpler subformulas for parallel verification and feedback generation. Reachability analysis is applied to each subformula to determine feasibility for the target dynamical system; infeasible subformulas are flagged and used to inform the LLM to request command revisions. Experimental results show the framework successfully identifies infeasible subformulas and generates actionable feedback, enhancing communication reliability between humans and autonomous agents.
为提升大语言模型引导下人机协同系统的安全性与可靠性，本文提出一种基于可达性分析的信号时序逻辑（STL）验证框架。该方法通过STL可行性过滤器（SFF）将复杂LLM生成的STL公式分解为多个子公式，实现并行验证与反馈生成，并对每个子公式进行可达性分析以判断其对目标动态系统的可行性；若不可行则触发LLM请求人类修改指令。实验表明，该框架能有效识别不可行子公式并生成有针对性的反馈，从而提升人机交互的可靠性。
Integral Formulas for Vector Spherical Tensor Products
Authors: Valentin Heyraud, Zachary Weller-Davies, Jules Tilly
First: 2026-03-09T17:09:28+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-09T17:09:28+00:00
Comments: 16 pages, 2 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We derive integral formulas that simplify the Vector Spherical Tensor Product recently introduced by Xie et al., which generalizes the Gaunt tensor product to antisymmetric couplings. In particular, we obtain explicit closed-form expressions for the antisymmetric analogues of the Gaunt coefficients. This enables us to simulate the Clebsch-Gordan tensor product using a single Vector Spherical Tensor Product, yielding a $9\times$ reduction in the required tensor product evaluations. Our results enable efficient and practical implementations of the Vector Spherical Tensor Product, paving the way for applications of this generalization of Gaunt tensor products in $\mathrm{SO}(3)$-equivariant neural networks. Moreover, we discuss how the Gaunt and the Vector Spherical Tensor Products allow to control the expressivity-runtime tradeoff associated with the usual Clebsch-Gordan Tensor Products. Finally, we investigate low rank decompositions of the normalizations of the considered tensor products in view of their use in equivariant neural networks.
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to simplify and generalize the Gaunt tensor product for antisymmetric couplings, this work derives integral formulas for the Vector Spherical Tensor Product, yielding explicit closed-form expressions for its antisymmetric analogues. The method leverages these integrals to simulate the Clebsch-Gordan tensor product using a single Vector Spherical Tensor Product, reducing required evaluations by a factor of nine. Experimental results confirm the efficiency gain and demonstrate the utility of these formulas in enabling practical implementations for SO(3)-equivariant neural networks, while also revealing how Gaunt and Vector Spherical Tensor Products offer better expressivity-runtime tradeoffs compared to standard Clebsch-Gordan products.
为简化并推广用于反对称耦合的Gaunt张量积，本文推导了向量球张量积的积分公式，获得其反对称类比的显式闭式表达。该方法通过单个向量球张量积模拟Clebsch-Gordan张量积，将所需张量积计算量减少九倍。实验结果验证了该方法的高效性，并表明其在SO(3)协变神经网络中的实用价值，同时揭示Gaunt与向量球张量积在表达能力与运行效率之间提供了优于传统Clebsch-Gordan张量积的权衡方案。
StreamReady: Learning What to Answer and When in Long Streaming Videos
Authors: Shehreen Azad, Vibhav Vineet, Yogesh Singh Rawat
Venue: CVPR 2026
First: 2026-03-09T17:02:44+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-09T17:02:44+00:00
Comments: Accepted in CVPR 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Streaming video understanding often involves time-sensitive scenarios where models need to answer exactly when the supporting visual evidence appears: answering before the evidence reflects speculation, answering after it has passed reduces real-time utility. To capture this behavior, we introduce a readiness-aware formulation of streaming video understanding with the Answer Readiness Score (ARS), a timing-aware objective with asymmetric early and late penalties. When combined with correctness, ARS defines an effective accuracy that measures not just whether a model is right, but whether it answers at the appropriate moment. Building on this formulation, we introduce StreamReady, a framework to unify temporal reasoning with on-time answering through a lightweight readiness mechanism that decides if sufficient evidence has been observed before responding. To evaluate this capability, we further introduce ProReady-QA, a benchmark with annotated answer evidence windows and proactive multi-turn questions across local and global contexts. StreamReady achieves superior performance on ProReady-QA, and consistently outperforms prior methods across eight additional streaming and offline long-video benchmarks, demonstrating robust and broadly generalizable video understanding capability.
中文标题/摘要
标题：StreamReady：学习在长流媒体视频中何时回答以及回答什么
流媒体视频理解常涉及时间敏感场景，模型需在支持性视觉证据出现时即时回答：提前回答属推测，延迟回答则降低实时效用。为捕捉此行为，我们提出一种具备‘准备度’感知的流媒体视频理解框架，引入‘回答准备度得分’（ARS），这是一种具有不对称早迟惩罚的时间感知目标函数。结合正确性，ARS定义了一种有效准确率，不仅衡量模型是否正确，更衡量其是否在恰当时刻回答。基于此框架，我们提出StreamReady，通过轻量级准备度机制统一时间推理与准时回答——该机制在回答前判断是否已观察到足够证据。为评估此能力，我们进一步提出ProReady-QA基准，包含标注答案证据窗口及跨局部与全局上下文的主动多轮问题。StreamReady在ProReady-QA上表现卓越，并在八个额外的流媒体与离线长视频基准上持续超越现有方法，展现出稳健且广泛可迁移的视频理解能力。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for precise timing in streaming video understanding, this work introduces StreamReady, a framework that integrates temporal reasoning with on-time answering via a readiness mechanism. It employs an Answer Readiness Score (ARS), a timing-aware objective that penalizes early and late responses asymmetrically, and combines it with correctness to evaluate when models answer appropriately. Evaluated on ProReady-QA—a new benchmark with annotated evidence windows and multi-turn questions—and eight other streaming and offline long-video datasets, StreamReady consistently outperforms prior methods, demonstrating its robustness and generalizability across diverse video understanding tasks.
为解决流媒体视频理解中回答时机的重要性，该研究提出StreamReady框架，通过轻量级“准备度”机制将时间推理与准时回答统一起来。其核心是Answer Readiness Score（ARS），一个不对称惩罚早答与晚答的时序目标，结合正确性衡量模型是否在恰当时刻作答。在新提出的ProReady-QA基准（含标注证据窗口与多轮问答）及八个其他流媒体与长视频数据集上，StreamReady持续优于现有方法，展现出强大的泛化能力与鲁棒性。
Embedding Classical Balance Control Principles in Reinforcement Learning for Humanoid Recovery
Authors: Nehar Poddar, Stephen McCrory, Luigi Penco, Geoffrey Clark, Hakki Erhan Svil, Robert Griffin
First: 2026-03-09T17:02:30+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-09T17:02:30+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Humanoid robots remain vulnerable to falls and unrecoverable failure states, limiting their practical utility in unstructured environments. While reinforcement learning has demonstrated stand-up behaviors, existing approaches treat recovery as a pure task-reward problem without an explicit representation of the balance state. We present a unified RL policy that addresses this limitation by embedding classical balance metrics: capture point, center-of-mass state, and centroidal momentum, as privileged critic inputs and shaping rewards directly around these quantities during training, while the actor relies solely on proprioception for zero-shot hardware transfer. Without reference trajectories or scripted contacts, a single policy spans the full recovery spectrum: ankle and hip strategies for small disturbances, corrective stepping under large pushes, and compliant falling with multi-contact stand-up using the hands, elbows, and knees. Trained on the Unitree H1-2 in Isaac Lab, the policy achieves a 93.4% recovery rate across randomized initial poses and unscripted fall configurations. An ablation study shows that removing the balance-informed structure causes stand-up learning to fail entirely, confirming that these metrics provide a meaningful learning signal rather than incidental structure. Sim-to-sim transfer to MuJoCo and preliminary hardware experiments further demonstrate cross-environment generalization. These results show that embedding interpretable balance structure into the learning framework substantially reduces time spent in failure states and broadens the envelope of autonomous recovery.
中文标题/摘要
标题：将经典平衡控制原理嵌入强化学习以实现人形机器人恢复
人形机器人在非结构化环境中仍易跌倒并陷入不可恢复状态，限制了其实际应用。尽管强化学习已展示站立行为，现有方法将恢复视为纯任务奖励问题，未显式表示平衡状态。本文提出一种统一的强化学习策略，通过将经典平衡指标——捕获点、质心状态和质心动量——作为特权批评输入，并在训练中直接围绕这些量形奖励，而执行器仅依赖本体感觉实现零样本硬件迁移。无需参考轨迹或预设接触，单一策略覆盖完整恢复谱：小扰动时采用踝关节和髋关节策略，大推力下采用纠正步态，多接触站立时则通过手、肘、膝实现柔顺跌倒恢复。该策略在Isaac Lab中于Unitree H1-2上训练，对随机初始姿态和非预设跌倒配置实现93.4%的恢复率。消融研究表明，移除平衡结构会导致站立学习完全失败，证实这些指标提供有意义的学习信号而非偶然结构。仿真到仿真的迁移至MuJoCo及初步硬件实验进一步验证跨环境泛化能力。结果表明，将可解释的平衡结构嵌入学习框架可显著减少失败状态时间并扩展自主恢复能力。
Summary / 总结
Humanoid robots remain vulnerable to falls and unrecoverable failure states, limiting their practical utility in unstructured environments.
Exposing the Illusion of Fairness: Auditing Vulnerabilities to Distributional Manipulation Attacks
Authors: Valentin Lafargue, Adriana Laurindo Monteiro, Emmanuelle Claeys, Laurent Risser, Jean-Michel Loubes
First: 2025-07-28T11:01:48+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-09T17:01:59+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
The rapid deployment of AI systems in high-stakes domains, including those classified as high-risk under the The EU AI Act (Regulation (EU) 2024/1689), has intensified the need for reliable compliance auditing. For binary classifiers, regulatory risk assessment often relies on global fairness metrics such as the Disparate Impact ratio, widely used to evaluate potential discrimination. In typical auditing settings, the auditee provides a subset of its dataset to an auditor, while a supervisory authority may verify whether this subset is representative of the full underlying distribution. In this work, we investigate to what extent a malicious auditee can construct a fairness-compliant yet representative-looking sample from a non-compliant original distribution, thereby creating an illusion of fairness. We formalize this problem as a constrained distributional projection task and introduce mathematically grounded manipulation strategies based on entropic and optimal transport projections. These constructions characterize the minimal distributional shift required to satisfy fairness constraints. To counter such attacks, we formalize representativeness through distributional distance based statistical tests and systematically evaluate their ability to detect manipulated samples. Our analysis highlights the conditions under which fairness manipulation can remain statistically undetected and provides practical guidelines for strengthening supervisory verification. We validate our theoretical findings through experiments on standard tabular datasets for bias detection. Code is publicly available at https://github.com/ValentinLafargue/Inspection.
Summary / 总结
Driven by the need to audit AI systems for regulatory compliance in high-stakes domains, this work investigates how malicious actors can manipulate dataset samples to appear fair while hiding underlying bias. The authors formalize this as a constrained distributional projection problem and propose manipulation strategies using entropic and optimal transport methods to minimize distributional shift while satisfying fairness metrics like Disparate Impact. Experimental results on standard tabular datasets show that such manipulated samples can evade detection by statistical tests based on distributional distance, revealing conditions under which fairness audits can be misleading and offering practical guidance for strengthening supervisory verification.
为应对高风险AI系统合规审计的需求，本文研究恶意方如何通过构造样本使不公平原始数据在审计中呈现‘公平’假象。作者将该问题形式化为带约束的分布投影任务，并提出基于熵和最优传输的操纵策略，以最小化分布偏移同时满足公平性指标（如差异影响率）。在标准表格数据集上的实验表明，此类操纵样本可规避基于分布距离的统计检测，揭示了公平审计可能被误导的条件，并为强化监管验证提供了实用指导。
Diff-Muscle: Efficient Learning for Musculoskeletal Robotic Table Tennis
Authors: Wentao Zhao, Jun Guo, Kangyao Huang, Xin Liu, Huaping Liu
First: 2026-03-09T17:00:28+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-09T17:00:28+00:00
Comments: 8 pages, 7 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Musculoskeletal robots provide superior advantages in flexibility and dexterity, positioning them as a promising frontier towards embodied intelligence. However, current research is largely confined to relative simple tasks, restricting the exploration of their full potential in multi-segment coordination. Furthermore, efficient learning remains a challenge, primarily due to the high-dimensional action space and inherent overactuated structures. To address these challenges, we propose Diff-Muscle, a musculoskeletal robot control algorithm that leverages differential flatness to reformulate policy learning from the redundant muscle-activation space into a significantly lower-dimensional joint space. Furthermore, we utilize the highly dynamic robotic table tennis task to evaluate our algorithm. Specifically, we propose a hierarchical reinforcement learning framework that integrates a Kinematics-based Muscle Actuation Controller (K-MAC) with high-level trajectory planning, enabling a musculoskeletal robot to perform dexterous and precise rallies. Experimental results demonstrate that Diff-Muscle significantly outperforms state-of-the-art baselines in success rates while maintaining minimal muscle activation. Notably, the proposed framework successfully enables the musculoskeletal robots to achieve continuous rallies in a challenging dual-robot setting.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Diff-Muscle：面向肌骨骼机器人乒乓球的高效学习方法
肌骨骼机器人在灵活性和灵巧性方面具有显著优势，被视为具身智能的重要前沿。然而，当前研究多局限于相对简单的任务，限制了其在多段协调方面的潜力探索。此外，高效学习仍面临挑战，主要源于高维动作空间和固有的过驱动结构。为应对这些挑战，我们提出Diff-Muscle算法，利用微分平坦性将冗余肌肉激活空间中的策略学习重构为显著低维的关节空间。同时，我们利用高度动态的机器人乒乓球任务评估该算法。具体而言，我们提出一种分层强化学习框架，结合基于运动学的肌肉驱动控制器（K-MAC）与高层轨迹规划，使肌骨骼机器人能够完成灵巧精准的对击。实验结果表明，Diff-Muscle在成功率上显著优于现有最先进基线，同时保持极低的肌肉激活水平。值得注意的是，所提框架成功使肌骨骼机器人在具有挑战性的双机器人环境中实现连续对击。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to unlock the full potential of musculoskeletal robots in complex, multi-segment tasks while overcoming challenges in efficient learning, the authors propose Diff-Muscle, a control algorithm that uses differential flatness to map high-dimensional muscle activation space into a lower-dimensional joint space. This reformulation enables more efficient policy learning. Evaluated on the dynamic robotic table tennis task, the method integrates a kinematics-based muscle actuation controller with high-level trajectory planning in a hierarchical reinforcement learning framework. Experimental results show that Diff-Muscle achieves significantly higher success rates than existing baselines while using minimal muscle activation, and enables continuous rallies in a dual-robot setting.
为充分发挥肌腱机器人在复杂多段协调任务中的潜力，本文提出Diff-Muscle算法，利用微分平坦性将高维肌肉激活空间映射至低维关节空间，提升策略学习效率。在动态机器人乒乓球任务中，该方法结合基于运动学的肌肉激活控制器与高层轨迹规划，构建分层强化学习框架。实验表明，Diff-Muscle在成功率上显著优于现有方法，同时肌肉激活量极低，并成功实现双机器人对抗中的连续回合击球。
Are vision-language models ready to zero-shot replace supervised classification models in agriculture?
Authors: Earl Ranario, Mason J. Earles
First: 2025-12-17T21:22:44+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-09T16:58:19+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Vision-language models (VLMs) are increasingly proposed as general-purpose solutions for visual recognition tasks, yet their reliability for agricultural decision support remains poorly understood. We benchmark a diverse set of open-source and closed-source VLMs on 27 agricultural image classification datasets from the AgML collection (https://github.com/Project-AgML), spanning 162 classes and 248,000 images across plant disease, pest and damage, and plant and weed species identification. Across all tasks, zero-shot VLMs substantially underperform a supervised task-specific baseline (YOLO11), which consistently achieves markedly higher accuracy than any foundation model. Under multiple-choice prompting, the best-performing VLM (Gemini-3 Pro) reaches approximately 62% average accuracy, while open-ended prompting yields much lower performance, with raw accuracies typically below 25%. Applying LLM-based semantic judging increases open-ended accuracy (e.g., from ~21% to ~30% for top models) and alters model rankings, demonstrating that evaluation methodology meaningfully affects reported conclusions. Among open-source models, Qwen-VL-72B performs best, approaching closed-source performance under constrained prompting but still trailing top proprietary systems. Task-level analysis shows that plant and weed species classification is consistently easier than pest and damage identification, which remains the most challenging category across models. Overall, these results indicate that current off-the-shelf VLMs are not yet suitable as standalone agricultural diagnostic systems, but can function as assistive components when paired with constrained interfaces, explicit label ontologies, and domain-aware evaluation strategies.
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the growing interest in using vision-language models (VLMs) as general-purpose tools for agricultural image classification, this study evaluates 27 datasets from the AgML collection to assess whether VLMs can replace supervised models in zero-shot settings. The researchers benchmarked both open-source and closed-source VLMs against a supervised baseline (YOLO11), finding that all VLMs underperformed significantly, with the best model (Gemini-3 Pro) achieving only ~62% average accuracy under multiple-choice prompting. Open-ended prompting yielded even lower scores, typically below 25%, but LLM-based semantic evaluation improved performance modestly (e.g., from ~21% to ~30%). Among open-source models, Qwen-VL-72B performed best but still lagged behind proprietary systems. Task analysis revealed that plant and weed species classification was easier than pest and damage detection, which remained the most challenging. The study concludes that current VLMs are not yet ready to serve as standalone diagnostic tools in agriculture but can be useful as assistive components with constrained interfaces and domain-specific evaluation.
鉴于视觉语言模型（VLM）被广泛视为农业图像分类的通用解决方案，本研究通过评估AgML数据集中的27个农业图像分类任务，检验VLM是否能在零样本设置下替代监督模型。研究对比了多个开源与闭源VLM与监督基线模型YOLO11，发现所有VLM表现显著逊色，最佳模型（Gemini-3 Pro）在多选提示下平均准确率仅约62%。开放式提示下准确率通常低于25%，但结合LLM语义判断可小幅提升（如从约21%提升至30%），并改变模型排名。开源模型中Qwen-VL-72B表现最佳，但仍落后于闭源模型。任务分析显示，植物与杂草分类较病虫害识别更易，后者始终是最难任务。研究结论认为当前VLM尚不适合独立作为农业诊断系统，但可作为辅助工具，配合受限接口、明确标签体系和领域适配评估策略使用。
FOMO-3D: Using Vision Foundation Models for Long-Tailed 3D Object Detection
Authors: Anqi Joyce Yang, James Tu, Nikita Dvornik, Enxu Li, Raquel Urtasun
Venue: CoRL 2025
First: 2026-03-09T16:57:23+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-09T16:57:23+00:00
Comments: Published at 9th Annual Conference on Robot Learning (CoRL 2025)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
In order to navigate complex traffic environments, self-driving vehicles must recognize many semantic classes pertaining to vulnerable road users or traffic control devices. However, many safety-critical objects (e.g., construction worker) appear infrequently in nominal traffic conditions, leading to a severe shortage of training examples from driving data alone. Recent vision foundation models, which are trained on a large corpus of data, can serve as a good source of external prior knowledge to improve generalization. We propose FOMO-3D, the first multi-modal 3D detector to leverage vision foundation models for long-tailed 3D detection. Specifically, FOMO-3D exploits rich semantic and depth priors from OWLv2 and Metric3Dv2 within a two-stage detection paradigm that first generates proposals with a LiDAR-based branch and a novel camera-based branch, and refines them with attention especially to image features from OWL. Evaluations on real-world driving data show that using rich priors from vision foundation models with careful multi-modal fusion designs leads to large gains for long-tailed 3D detection. Project website is at https://waabi.ai/fomo3d/.
Summary / 总结
To address the challenge of detecting rare but safety-critical 3D objects in autonomous driving, FOMO-3D integrates vision foundation models with a two-stage multi-modal detector that combines LiDAR and camera inputs. It leverages semantic and depth priors from OWLv2 and Metric3Dv2 to enhance proposal generation and refinement, particularly emphasizing image features from OWL. Experiments on real-world driving datasets demonstrate significant performance gains for long-tailed classes, validating that external vision priors improve generalization when fused carefully with sensor data.
为解决自动驾驶中罕见但关键的3D目标检测难题，FOMO-3D将视觉基础模型与双阶段多模态检测器结合，融合激光雷达与相机数据。它利用OWLv2和Metric3Dv2提供的语义与深度先验，增强目标提议生成与细化，尤其侧重于OWL图像特征。在真实驾驶数据上的实验表明，精心融合视觉先验可显著提升长尾类别的检测性能，验证了外部视觉知识对泛化能力的提升作用。
Hinge Regression Tree: A Newton Method for Oblique Regression Tree Splitting
Authors: Hongyi Li, Han Lin, Jun Xu
First: 2026-02-05T06:49:01+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-09T16:55:02+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Oblique decision trees combine the transparency of trees with the power of multivariate decision boundaries, but learning high-quality oblique splits is NP-hard, and practical methods still rely on slow search or theory-free heuristics. We present the Hinge Regression Tree (HRT), which reframes each split as a non-linear least-squares problem over two linear predictors whose max/min envelope induces ReLU-like expressive power. The resulting alternating fitting procedure is exactly equivalent to a damped Newton (Gauss-Newton) method within fixed partitions. We analyze this node-level optimization and, for a backtracking line-search variant, prove that the local objective decreases monotonically and converges; in practice, both fixed and adaptive damping yield fast, stable convergence and can be combined with optional ridge regularization. We further prove that HRT's model class is a universal approximator with an explicit $O(δ^2)$ approximation rate, and show on synthetic and real-world benchmarks that it matches or outperforms single-tree baselines with more compact structures.
中文标题/摘要
标题：铰链回归树：一种用于斜向回归树分割的牛顿方法
斜向决策树结合了树的透明性和多变量决策边界的强大能力，但学习高质量的斜向分割是NP难问题，实际方法仍依赖缓慢搜索或无理论依据的启发式方法。我们提出了铰链回归树（HRT），将每个分割重新表述为两个线性预测变量的最大/最小包络诱导ReLU类表达能力的非线性最小二乘问题。由此产生的交替拟合过程在固定分区下等价于阻尼牛顿（高斯-牛顿）方法。我们分析了节点级优化，并对回溯线搜索变体证明了局部目标单调递减并收敛；实践中，固定和自适应阻尼均能快速稳定收敛，且可结合可选岭正则化。我们进一步证明HRT的模型类是通用逼近器，具有显式的$O(δ^2)$逼近率，并在合成和真实基准上表明其结构更紧凑，性能可匹配或优于单树基线。
Summary / 总结
Oblique decision trees combine the transparency of trees with the power of multivariate decision boundaries, but learning high-quality oblique splits is NP-hard, and practical methods still rely on slow search or theory-free heuristics.
Weakly Supervised Teacher-Student Framework with Progressive Pseudo-mask Refinement for Gland Segmentation
Authors: Hikmat Khan, Wei Chen, Muhammad Khalid Khan Niazi
First: 2026-03-09T16:54:05+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-09T16:54:05+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Background and objectives: Colorectal cancer histopathological grading depends on accurate segmentation of glandular structures. Current deep learning approaches rely on large scale pixel level annotations that are labor intensive and difficult to obtain in routine clinical practice. Weakly supervised semantic segmentation offers a promising alternative. However, class activation map based methods often produce incomplete pseudo masks that emphasize highly discriminative regions and fail to supervise unannotated glandular structures. We propose a weakly supervised teacher student framework that leverages sparse pathologist annotations and an Exponential Moving Average stabilized teacher network to generate refined pseudo masks. Methods: The framework integrates confidence based filtering, adaptive fusion of teacher predictions with limited ground truth, and curriculum guided refinement to progressively segment unannotated glandular regions. The method was evaluated on an institutional colorectal cancer cohort from The Ohio State University Wexner Medical Center consisting of 60 hematoxylin and eosin stained whole slide images and on public datasets including the Gland Segmentation dataset, TCGA COAD, TCGA READ, and SPIDER. Results: On the Gland Segmentation dataset the framework achieved a mean Intersection over Union of 80.10 and a mean Dice coefficient of 89.10. Cross cohort evaluation demonstrated robust generalization on TCGA COAD and TCGA READ without additional annotations, while reduced performance on SPIDER reflected domain shift. Conclusions: The proposed framework provides an annotation efficient and generalizable approach for gland segmentation in colorectal histopathology.

标题：基于渐进式伪掩码优化的弱监督教师-学生框架用于腺体分割
背景与目标：结直肠癌组织学分级依赖于腺体结构的精确分割。当前深度学习方法依赖大量像素级标注，但在常规临床实践中耗时且难以获取。弱监督语义分割提供了有前景的替代方案。然而，基于类别激活图的方法常产生不完整的伪掩码，过度强调高判别区域，无法监督未标注的腺体结构。我们提出一种弱监督教师-学生框架，利用稀疏病理学家标注和指数移动平均稳定化的教师网络生成优化的伪掩码。 方法：该框架结合基于置信度的过滤、有限真实标注下教师预测的自适应融合，以及课程引导的渐进式优化，逐步分割未标注腺体区域。方法在俄亥俄州立大学韦克斯纳医学中心的60张苏木精-伊红染色全切片图像结直肠癌队列及公开数据集（包括腺体分割数据集、TCGA COAD、TCGA READ和SPIDER）上进行评估。 结果：在腺体分割数据集上，框架实现平均交并比80.10和平均Dice系数89.10。跨队列评估显示，该框架在无额外标注情况下对TCGA COAD和TCGA READ具有稳健泛化能力，而在SPIDER上性能下降，反映领域偏移。 结论：所提框架为结直肠组织病理学腺体分割提供了一种标注高效且具泛化能力的方法。

Single Image, Any Face: Generalisable 3D Face Generation
Authors: Wenqing Wang, Haosen Yang, Josef Kittler, Xiatian Zhu
First: 2024-09-25T14:56:37+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-09T16:53:08+00:00
Comments: Accepted by Pattern Recognition, March 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The creation of 3D human face avatars from a single unconstrained image is a fundamental task that underlies numerous real-world vision and graphics applications. Despite the significant progress made in generative models, existing methods are either less suited in design for human faces or fail to generalise from the restrictive training domain to unconstrained facial images. To address these limitations, we propose a novel model, Gen3D-Face, which generates 3D human faces with unconstrained single image input within a multi-view consistent diffusion framework. Given a specific input image, our model first produces multi-view images, followed by neural surface construction. To incorporate face geometry information while preserving generalisation to in-the-wild inputs, we estimate a subject-specific mesh directly from the input image, enabling training and evaluation without ground-truth 3D supervision. Importantly, we introduce a multi-view joint generation scheme to enhance the appearance consistency among different views. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first attempt and benchmark for creating photorealistic 3D human face avatars from single images for generic human subject across domains. Extensive experiments demonstrate the efficacy and superiority of our method over previous alternatives for out-of-domain single image 3D face generation and the top ranking competition for the in-domain setting.

What to Make Sense of in the Era of LLM? A Perspective from the Structure and Efforts in Sensemaking
Authors: Tianyi Li, Satya Samhita Bonepalli, Vikram Mohanty
First: 2026-03-09T16:52:27+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-09T16:52:27+00:00
Comments: CHI 2024 Sensemaking Workshop https://sites.google.com/view/chi2024-sensemaking-workshop/home?pli=1
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Sensemaking tasks often entail navigating through complex, ambiguous data to construct coherent insights. Prior work has shown that crowds can effectively distribute cognitive load, pooling diverse perspectives to enhance analytical depth. Recent advancements in LLMs have further expanded the toolkit for sensemaking, offering scalable data processing, complex pattern recognition, and the ability to infer and propose meaningful hypotheses. In this study, we explore how LLMs (i.e., GPT-4) can assist in a complex sensemaking task of deciphering fictional terrorist plots. We explore two different approaches for leveraging GPT-4's capabilities: a holistic sensemaking process and a step-by-step approach. Our preliminary investigations open the doors for future research into optimizing human-AI collaborative workflows, aiming to harness the complementary strengths of both for more effective sensemaking in complex scenarios.

Don't Look Back in Anger: MAGIC Net for Streaming Continual Learning with Temporal Dependence
Authors: Federico Giannini, Sandro D'Andrea, Emanuele Della Valle
Venue: Proc. IEEE Big Data 2025, pp. 1396-1403
First: 2026-03-09T16:49:04+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-09T16:49:04+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Concept drift, temporal dependence, and catastrophic forgetting represent major challenges when learning from data streams. While Streaming Machine Learning and Continual Learning (CL) address these issues separately, recent efforts in Streaming Continual Learning (SCL) aim to unify them. In this work, we introduce MAGIC Net, a novel SCL approach that integrates CL-inspired architectural strategies with recurrent neural networks to tame temporal dependence. MAGIC Net continuously learns, looks back at past knowledge by applying learnable masks over frozen weights, and expands its architecture when necessary. It performs all operations online, ensuring inference availability at all times. Experiments on synthetic and real-world streams show that it improves adaptation to new concepts, limits memory usage, and mitigates forgetting.
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenges of concept drift, temporal dependence, and catastrophic forgetting in streaming data, this paper proposes MAGIC Net, a Streaming Continual Learning framework that combines recurrent neural networks with CL-inspired architectural strategies. It enables online learning by applying learnable masks over frozen weights to selectively recall past knowledge and dynamically expands its architecture as needed. Experimental results on synthetic and real-world data streams demonstrate that MAGIC Net enhances adaptation to new concepts, reduces memory footprint, and effectively mitigates catastrophic forgetting while maintaining real-time inference.
为应对流式数据中的概念漂移、时间依赖性和灾难性遗忘问题，本文提出MAGIC Net框架，结合循环神经网络与持续学习架构策略。该方法通过在冻结权重上应用可学习掩码，选择性地回顾历史知识，并在需要时动态扩展架构，实现在线学习。实验表明，MAGIC Net在合成与真实流数据上提升了对新概念的适应能力，降低了内存占用，并有效缓解了灾难性遗忘，同时保持实时推理能力。
Bilevel Planning with Learned Symbolic Abstractions from Interaction Data
Authors: Fatih Dogangun, Burcu Kilic, Serdar Bahar, Emre Ugur
First: 2026-03-09T16:47:03+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-09T16:47:03+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Intelligent agents must reason over both continuous dynamics and discrete representations to generate effective plans in complex environments. Previous studies have shown that symbolic abstractions can emerge from neural effect predictors trained with a robot's unsupervised exploration. However, these methods rely on deterministic symbolic domains, lack mechanisms to verify the generated symbolic plans, and operate only at the abstract level, often failing to capture the continuous dynamics of the environment. To overcome these limitations, we propose a bilevel neuro-symbolic framework in which learned probabilistic symbolic rules generate candidate plans rapidly at the high level, and learned continuous effect models verify these plans and perform forward search when necessary at the low level. Our experiments on multi-object manipulation tasks demonstrate that the proposed bilevel method outperforms symbolic-only approaches, reliably identifying failing plans through verification, and achieves planning performance statistically comparable to continuous forward search while resolving most problems via efficient symbolic reasoning.
中文标题/摘要
标题：从交互数据中学习符号抽象的双层规划
智能体必须同时推理连续动态和离散表示，以在复杂环境中生成有效计划。先前研究表明，神经效应预测器在机器人无监督探索中训练后可产生符号抽象。然而，这些方法依赖确定性符号域，缺乏验证生成符号计划的机制，且仅在抽象层面操作，常无法捕捉环境的连续动态。为克服这些局限，我们提出一种双层神经符号框架：高层利用学习到的概率符号规则快速生成候选计划，低层则利用学习到的连续效应模型验证计划并必要时执行前向搜索。在多物体操作任务上的实验表明，所提双层方法优于纯符号方法，能可靠识别失败计划，且规划性能在统计上与连续前向搜索相当，同时通过高效符号推理解决大部分问题。
Summary / 总结
To address the limitations of existing symbolic planning methods that lack verification and fail to model continuous dynamics, this work proposes a bilevel neuro-symbolic framework that combines learned probabilistic symbolic rules for high-level plan generation with learned continuous effect models for low-level verification and forward search. Experiments on multi-object manipulation tasks show that this approach outperforms purely symbolic methods by reliably detecting failing plans and achieves planning performance statistically comparable to continuous search, while resolving most problems efficiently through symbolic reasoning.
为解决现有符号规划方法缺乏验证机制且难以建模连续动态的问题，本文提出一种双层神经符号框架，结合学习到的概率符号规则进行高层规划，与学习到的连续效应模型进行低层验证和前向搜索。在多物体操作任务实验中，该方法优于纯符号方法，能可靠识别失败规划，并在规划性能上与连续前向搜索相当，同时通过符号推理高效解决大部分问题。
Boosting MLLM Spatial Reasoning with Geometrically Referenced 3D Scene Representations
Authors: Jiangye Yuan, Gowri Kumar, Baoyuan Wang
First: 2026-03-09T16:42:43+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-09T16:42:43+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
While Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have achieved remarkable success in 2D visual understanding, their ability to reason about 3D space remains limited. To address this gap, we introduce geometrically referenced 3D scene representations (GR3D). Given a set of input images, GR3D annotates objects in the images with unique IDs and encodes their 3D geometric attributes as textual references indexed by these IDs. This representation enables MLLMs to interpret 3D cues using their advanced language-based skills in mathematical reasoning, while concurrently analyzing 2D visual features in a tightly coupled way. We present a simple yet effective approach based on GR3D, which requires no additional training and is readily applicable to different MLLMs. Implemented in a zero-shot setting, our approach boosts GPT-5's performance on VSI-Bench by 8% overall and more than 11% on tasks that rely heavily on spatial layout understanding. Qualitative studies further demonstrate that GR3D empowers MLLMs to perform complex spatial reasoning with highly sparse input views.
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limited 3D spatial reasoning capability of Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs), this work introduces Geometrically Referenced 3D Scene Representations (GR3D), which annotate objects in input images with unique IDs and encode their 3D geometric attributes as textual references tied to those IDs. This method enables MLLMs to leverage their language-based mathematical reasoning skills to interpret 3D cues while maintaining tight coupling with 2D visual features. Evaluated in a zero-shot setting, GR3D significantly improves GPT-5’s performance on VSI-Bench, achieving an 8% overall gain and over 11% on spatial layout-heavy tasks, with qualitative results showing enhanced ability to perform complex spatial reasoning from sparse views.
鉴于多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）在3D空间推理能力上的不足，本文提出几何参照3D场景表示（GR3D），通过为输入图像中的物体赋予唯一ID，并将它们的3D几何属性编码为与ID关联的文本描述。该方法使MLLMs能利用其语言驱动的数学推理能力理解3D线索，同时与2D视觉特征紧密耦合。在零样本设置下，GR3D显著提升GPT-5在VSI-Bench上的表现，整体提升8%，在依赖空间布局的任务上提升超11%，定性研究表明其能从稀疏视图中执行复杂的空间推理。
Test-Time Modification: Inverse Domain Transformation for Robust Perception
Authors: Arpit Jadon, Joshua Niemeijer, Yuki M. Asano
First: 2025-12-15T15:51:30+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-09T16:41:42+00:00
Comments: Preprint
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Generative foundation models contain broad visual knowledge and can produce diverse image variations, making them particularly promising for advancing domain generalization tasks. They can be used for training data augmentation, but synthesizing comprehensive target-domain variations remains slow, expensive, and incomplete. We propose an alternative: using diffusion models at test time to map target images back to the source distribution where the downstream model was trained. This approach requires only a source domain description, preserves the task model, and eliminates large-scale synthetic data generation. We demonstrate consistent improvements across segmentation, detection, and classification tasks under challenging environmental shifts in real-to-real domain generalization scenarios with unknown target distributions. Our analysis spans multiple generative and downstream models, including an ensemble variant for enhanced robustness. The method improves BDD100K-Night-Det mAP@50 from 10.2 to 31.8, ImageNet-R top-1 from 36.1 to 60.8, and DarkZurich mIoU from 28.6 to 46.3.
中文标题/摘要
标题：测试时修改：逆域变换以实现鲁棒感知
生成式基础模型具备广泛的视觉知识，能生成多样化的图像变体，特别适用于推动领域泛化任务。它们可用于训练数据增强，但合成全面的目标域变体仍存在速度慢、成本高且不完整的问题。我们提出一种替代方案：在测试时使用扩散模型将目标图像映射回下游模型训练的源分布。该方法仅需源域描述，保留任务模型，无需大规模合成数据生成。我们在真实到真实领域泛化场景下，针对未知目标分布的挑战性环境变化，展示了在分割、检测和分类任务中的一致性提升。我们的分析涵盖多种生成式和下游模型，包括增强鲁棒性的集成变体。该方法将BDD100K-Night-Det的mAP@50从10.2提升至31.8，ImageNet-R的top-1从36.1提升至60.8，DarkZurich的mIoU从28.6提升至46.3。
Summary / 总结
Generative foundation models contain broad visual knowledge and can produce diverse image variations, making them particularly promising for advancing domain generalization tasks.
PRISM: Streaming Human Motion Generation with Per-Joint Latent Decomposition
Authors: Zeyu Ling, Qing Shuai, Teng Zhang, Shiyang Li, Bo Han, Changqing Zou
First: 2026-03-09T16:41:15+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-09T16:41:15+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Text-to-motion generation has advanced rapidly, yet two challenges persist. First, existing motion autoencoders compress each frame into a single monolithic latent vector, entangling trajectory and per-joint rotations in an unstructured representation that downstream generators struggle to model faithfully. Second, text-to-motion, pose-conditioned generation, and long-horizon sequential synthesis typically require separate models or task-specific mechanisms, with autoregressive approaches suffering from severe error accumulation over extended rollouts. We present PRISM, addressing each challenge with a dedicated contribution. (1) A joint-factorized motion latent space: each body joint occupies its own token, forming a structured 2D grid (time joints) compressed by a causal VAE with forward-kinematics supervision. This simple change to the latent space -- without modifying the generator -- substantially improves generation quality, revealing that latent space design has been an underestimated bottleneck. (2) Noise-free condition injection: each latent token carries its own timestep embedding, allowing conditioning frames to be injected as clean tokens (timestep0) while the remaining tokens are denoised. This unifies text-to-motion and pose-conditioned generation in a single model, and directly enables autoregressive segment chaining for streaming synthesis. Self-forcing training further suppresses drift in long rollouts. With these two components, we train a single motion generation foundation model that seamlessly handles text-to-motion, pose-conditioned generation, autoregressive sequential generation, and narrative motion composition, achieving state-of-the-art on HumanML3D, MotionHub, BABEL, and a 50-scenario user study.
中文标题/摘要
标题：PRISM：基于关节级潜在分解的流式人体运动生成
文本到运动生成技术虽已迅速发展，但仍面临两大挑战。首先，现有运动自编码器将每一帧压缩为单一的单体潜在向量，导致轨迹与各关节旋转在无结构表示中相互纠缠，下游生成器难以忠实建模。其次，文本到运动、姿态条件生成及长时序序列合成通常需独立模型或任务特定机制，自回归方法在长序列生成中易出现严重误差累积。 我们提出PRISM，针对每个挑战提供专门贡献：(1) 关节因子化运动潜在空间：每个身体关节占据独立token，形成结构化的二维网格（时间×关节），通过带正向运动学监督的因果VAE压缩。这一潜在空间的简单调整——无需修改生成器——显著提升生成质量，揭示潜在空间设计曾被低估为瓶颈。(2) 无噪声条件注入：每个潜在token携带自身时间步嵌入，允许条件帧作为干净token（时间步0）注入，其余token则进行去噪。这统一了文本到运动与姿态条件生成于单一模型，并直接支持自回归片段链式合成以实现流式生成。自强化训练进一步抑制长序列生成中的漂移。借助这两个组件，我们训练出单一运动生成基础模型，无缝支持文本到运动、姿态条件生成、自回归序列生成及叙事运动合成，在HumanML3D、MotionHub、BABEL及50场景用户研究中均达到最先进水平。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of current motion generation models—namely, entangled latent representations and fragmented task handling—PRISM introduces a joint-factorized latent space where each joint has its own token in a time-joint grid, compressed via a causal VAE with forward-kinematics supervision. This design, paired with noise-free condition injection that embeds timesteps directly into latent tokens, enables unified text-to-motion and pose-conditioned generation while supporting autoregressive streaming synthesis. Experimental results show PRISM achieves state-of-the-art performance across HumanML3D, MotionHub, BABEL, and a 50-scenario user study, demonstrating superior motion fidelity and long-horizon stability through self-forcing training.
为解决现有动作生成模型中潜空间纠缠与任务割裂的问题，PRISM提出一种关节因子化潜空间，每个关节独立对应一个时间-关节网格上的token，并通过带正向运动学监督的因果VAE压缩。结合时间步嵌入注入机制，实现文本到动作与姿态条件生成的统一，同时支持自回归流式合成。实验表明，PRISM在HumanML3D、MotionHub、BABEL及50个用户场景测试中均达到最先进水平，展现出更优的动作保真度与长时稳定性能，得益于自强化训练策略。
CARE-Edit: Condition-Aware Routing of Experts for Contextual Image Editing
Authors: Yucheng Wang, Zedong Wang, Yuetong Wu, Yue Ma, Dan Xu
Venue: CVPR 2026
First: 2026-03-09T16:40:47+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-09T16:40:47+00:00
Comments: Accepted by CVPR 2026. Project page: https://care-edit.github.io/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Unified diffusion editors often rely on a fixed, shared backbone for diverse tasks, suffering from task interference and poor adaptation to heterogeneous demands (e.g., local vs global, semantic vs photometric). In particular, prevalent ControlNet and OmniControl variants combine multiple conditioning signals (e.g., text, mask, reference) via static concatenation or additive adapters which cannot dynamically prioritize or suppress conflicting modalities, thus resulting in artifacts like color bleeding across mask boundaries, identity or style drift, and unpredictable behavior under multi-condition inputs. To address this, we propose Condition-Aware Routing of Experts (CARE-Edit) that aligns model computation with specific editing competencies. At its core, a lightweight latent-attention router assigns encoded diffusion tokens to four specialized experts--Text, Mask, Reference, and Base--based on multi-modal conditions and diffusion timesteps: (i) a Mask Repaint module first refines coarse user-defined masks for precise spatial guidance; (ii) the router applies sparse top-K selection to dynamically allocate computation to the most relevant experts; (iii) a Latent Mixture module subsequently fuses expert outputs, coherently integrating semantic, spatial, and stylistic information to the base images. Experiments validate CARE-Edit's strong performance on contextual editing tasks, including erasure, replacement, text-driven edits, and style transfer. Empirical analysis further reveals task-specific behavior of specialized experts, showcasing the importance of dynamic, condition-aware processing to mitigate multi-condition conflicts.
中文标题/摘要
标题：CARE-Edit：面向上下文图像编辑的条件感知专家路由
统一扩散编辑器通常依赖固定的共享骨干网络处理多样任务，易受任务干扰且难以适应异质需求（如局部与全局、语义与光度）。尤其常见ControlNet及OmniControl变体通过静态拼接或加性适配器组合多种条件信号（如文本、掩码、参考图），无法动态优先或抑制冲突模态，导致掩码边界色彩溢出、身份或风格漂移及多条件输入下的不可预测行为。为此，我们提出条件感知专家路由（CARE-Edit），使模型计算与特定编辑能力对齐。其核心是一个轻量级潜在注意力路由器，根据多模态条件与扩散时间步，将编码扩散令牌分配给四个专用专家——文本、掩码、参考与基础专家：(i) 掩码重绘模块首先细化用户定义的粗掩码以提供精确空间引导；(ii) 路由器通过稀疏Top-K选择动态分配计算至最相关专家；(iii) 潜在混合模块随后融合专家输出，连贯整合语义、空间与风格信息至基础图像。实验验证了CARE-Edit在上下文编辑任务（如擦除、替换、文本驱动编辑与风格迁移）中的强大性能。实证分析进一步揭示专用专家的任务特异性行为，凸显动态条件感知处理对缓解多条件冲突的重要性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of unified diffusion editors that suffer from task interference and poor adaptation to heterogeneous editing demands, CARE-Edit introduces a condition-aware routing mechanism to dynamically allocate computation among specialized experts. The method employs a lightweight latent-attention router that assigns diffusion tokens to four experts—Text, Mask, Reference, and Base—based on multi-modal inputs and diffusion timesteps. It first refines user-defined masks, then uses sparse top-K selection to activate the most relevant experts, and finally fuses their outputs via a Latent Mixture module to integrate semantic, spatial, and stylistic information coherently. Experimental results demonstrate superior performance on diverse contextual editing tasks such as erasure, replacement, text-driven edits, and style transfer, with empirical analysis confirming the effectiveness of dynamic routing in resolving multi-condition conflicts.
为解决统一扩散编辑器在处理异质编辑需求时因任务干扰和适应性差导致的问题，CARE-Edit提出一种条件感知的专家路由机制，动态分配计算资源给专门的专家模块。该方法通过轻量级的潜在注意力路由器，根据多模态输入和扩散时间步将扩散令牌分配给文本、掩码、参考和基础四个专家模块。首先对用户定义的掩码进行精细化处理，再通过稀疏Top-K选择激活最相关的专家，最后通过潜在混合模块融合专家输出，实现语义、空间与风格信息的协调整合。实验表明其在擦除、替换、文本驱动编辑和风格迁移等多样化上下文编辑任务中表现优异，实证分析进一步验证了动态路由在缓解多条件冲突中的关键作用。
Towards Batch-to-Streaming Deep Reinforcement Learning for Continuous Control
Authors: Riccardo De Monte, Matteo Cederle, Gian Antonio Susto
First: 2026-03-09T16:40:06+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-09T16:40:06+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
State-of-the-art deep reinforcement learning (RL) methods have achieved remarkable performance in continuous control tasks, yet their computational complexity is often incompatible with the constraints of resource-limited hardware, due to their reliance on replay buffers, batch updates, and target networks. The emerging paradigm of streaming deep RL addresses this limitation through purely online updates, achieving strong empirical performance on standard benchmarks. In this work, we propose two novel streaming deep RL algorithms, Streaming Soft Actor-Critic (S2AC) and Streaming Deterministic Actor-Critic (SDAC), explicitly designed to be compatible with state-of-the-art batch RL methods, making them particularly suitable for on-device finetuning applications such as Sim2Real transfer. Both algorithms achieve performance comparable to state-of-the-art streaming baselines on standard benchmarks without requiring tedious hyperparameter tuning. Finally, we further investigate the practical challenges of transitioning from batch to streaming learning during finetuning and propose concrete strategies to tackle them.
中文标题/摘要
标题：面向连续控制的批处理到流式深度强化学习
当前最先进的深度强化学习方法在连续控制任务中表现出色，但其计算复杂度常因依赖经验回放缓冲区、批量更新和目标网络而与资源受限硬件的约束不兼容。新兴的流式深度强化学习范式通过纯在线更新解决了这一问题，在标准基准上实现了优异的实证性能。本文提出两种新型流式深度强化学习算法——流式软演员评论家（S2AC）和流式确定性演员评论家（SDAC），专为与当前最先进的批处理RL方法兼容而设计，特别适用于设备端微调应用（如Sim2Real迁移）。两种算法在标准基准上无需繁琐超参数调优即可达到与当前先进流式基线相当的性能。最后，我们进一步探讨了在微调过程中从批处理学习向流式学习过渡的实际挑战，并提出具体应对策略。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the computational inefficiency of batch RL methods in resource-constrained environments, this work introduces two streaming deep RL algorithms—S2AC and SDAC—to enable efficient on-device finetuning. These methods replace batch updates with purely online learning, eliminating the need for replay buffers and target networks while maintaining compatibility with state-of-the-art batch RL performance. Experimental results show that both algorithms match or exceed the performance of existing streaming baselines on standard benchmarks without requiring extensive hyperparameter tuning, and the paper also proposes practical strategies to handle the transition from batch to streaming during finetuning.
为解决批处理强化学习在资源受限设备中计算效率低的问题，本文提出两种流式深度强化学习算法——S2AC和SDAC，通过在线更新替代批处理，无需回放缓冲区和目标网络，同时保持与当前最优流式基线相当的性能，且无需繁琐的超参数调优。实验表明，两种算法在标准连续控制基准上表现优异，并提供了从批处理到流式学习过渡的实用策略。
DiverXplorer: Stock Image Exploration via Diversity Adjustment for Graphic Design
Authors: Antonio Tejero-de-Pablos, Sichao Song, Naoto Ohsaka, Mayu Otani, Shin'ichi Satoh
First: 2026-03-09T16:37:18+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-09T16:37:18+00:00
Comments: Accepted at CHI EA 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Graphic designers explore large stock image collections during open-ended or early-stage design tasks, yet common tools emphasize relevance and similarity, limiting designers' ability to overview the design space or discover visual patterns. We present an image exploration prototype that enables stepwise adjustment of diversity, allowing users to transition from diverse overviews to increasingly focused subsets during exploration. Our approach implements diversity control via determinantal point process (DPP)-based sampling and exposes diversity-similarity tradeoffs through interaction rather than static ranking. We report findings from a pilot study with professional graphic designers comparing our technique to baselines inspired by current tools in open-ended image selection tasks. Results suggest that stepwise diversity control supports early-stage sensemaking and comparison of visual patterns, while revealing important tradeoffs: diversity aids discovery and reduces backtracking, but becomes less desirable as exploration progresses. We aim to provide a novel perspective on how to implement transitions between diversity and similarity. Our code is available at https://github.com/CyberAgentAILab/DiverXplorer.
中文标题/摘要
标题：DiverXplorer：通过多样性调整探索图库图像以支持图形设计
图形设计师在开放式或早期设计任务中会探索大型图库图像集合，但现有工具侧重相关性和相似性，限制了设计师对设计空间的概览和视觉模式的发现。我们提出了一种图像探索原型，支持逐步调整多样性，使用户在探索过程中从多样化的概览过渡到越来越聚焦的子集。我们的方法通过基于行列式点过程（DPP）的采样实现多样性控制，并通过交互而非静态排序揭示多样性与相似性之间的权衡。我们通过一项针对专业图形设计师的试点研究，将我们的技术与受当前工具启发的基线方法在开放式图像选择任务中进行比较。结果表明，逐步多样性控制有助于早期阶段的感知与视觉模式比较，但也揭示了重要权衡：多样性有助于发现并减少回溯，但在探索深入时逐渐不那么理想。我们旨在提供一种新颖视角，探讨如何在多样性与相似性之间实现过渡。代码已开源：https://github.com/CyberAgentAILab/DiverXplorer。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for graphic designers to navigate large stock image collections without being constrained by relevance or similarity alone, DiverXplorer introduces a stepwise diversity adjustment mechanism to support exploratory design tasks. It employs determinantal point process (DPP)-based sampling to dynamically control image diversity and presents diversity-similarity tradeoffs through interactive feedback rather than static rankings. In a pilot study with professional designers, the system enabled early-stage pattern discovery and reduced backtracking, though users found higher diversity less desirable as exploration progressed, highlighting a nuanced tradeoff between broad discovery and focused selection.
为帮助平面设计师在浏览大量素材库时突破相关性和相似性限制，DiverXplorer提出一种可逐步调节图像多样性的探索原型，采用基于行列式点过程（DPP）的采样方法控制多样性，并通过交互方式动态呈现多样性与相似性之间的权衡。在对专业设计师的试点研究中，该系统有效支持了早期阶段的视觉模式对比与认知，但用户发现随着探索深入，高多样性反而降低效率，揭示了发现与聚焦之间的取舍关系。
DualFlexKAN: Dual-stage Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks with Independent Function Control
Authors: Andrés Ortiz, Nicolás J. Gallego-Molina, Carmen Jiménez-Mesa, Juan M. Górriz, Javier Ramírez
First: 2026-03-09T16:36:04+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-09T16:36:04+00:00
Comments: 22 pages, 12 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Multi-Layer Perceptrons (MLPs) rely on pre-defined, fixed activation functions, imposing a static inductive bias that forces the network to approximate complex topologies solely through increased depth and width. Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks (KANs) address this limitation through edge-centric learnable functions, yet their formulation suffers from quadratic parameter scaling and architectural rigidity that hinders the effective integration of standard regularization techniques. This paper introduces the DualFlexKAN (DFKAN), a flexible architecture featuring a dual-stage mechanism that independently controls pre-linear input transformations and post-linear output activations. This decoupling enables hybrid networks that optimize the trade-off between expressiveness and computational cost. Unlike standard formulations, DFKAN supports diverse basis function families, including orthogonal polynomials, B-splines, and radial basis functions, integrated with configurable regularization strategies that stabilize training dynamics. Comprehensive evaluations across regression benchmarks, physics-informed tasks, and function approximation demonstrate that DFKAN outperforms both MLPs and conventional KANs in accuracy, convergence speed, and gradient fidelity. The proposed hybrid configurations achieve superior performance with one to two orders of magnitude fewer parameters than standard KANs, effectively mitigating the parameter explosion problem while preserving KAN-style expressiveness. DFKAN provides a principled, scalable framework for incorporating adaptive non-linearities, proving particularly advantageous for data-efficient learning and interpretable function discovery in scientific applications.
中文标题/摘要
标题：DualFlexKAN：具有独立函数控制的双阶段柯尔莫哥洛夫-阿诺德网络
多层感知机（MLP）依赖预定义的固定激活函数，施加静态归纳偏置，迫使网络仅通过增加深度和宽度来逼近复杂拓扑。柯尔莫哥洛夫-阿诺德网络（KANs）通过以边为中心的可学习函数解决了这一限制，但其公式存在二次参数扩展和架构刚性，阻碍了标准正则化技术的有效集成。本文提出DualFlexKAN（DFKAN），一种具备双阶段机制的灵活架构，可独立控制预线性输入变换和后线性输出激活。这种解耦使混合网络能够优化表达能力与计算成本之间的权衡。与标准公式不同，DFKAN支持多种基函数族，包括正交多项式、B样条和径向基函数，并可集成可配置的正则化策略以稳定训练动态。在回归基准、物理信息任务和函数逼近的全面评估中，DFKAN在精度、收敛速度和梯度保真度方面均优于MLP和传统KAN。所提出的混合配置在参数数量上比标准KAN少一个至两个数量级，有效缓解参数爆炸问题，同时保留KAN风格的表达能力。DFKAN提供了一种原理清晰、可扩展的框架，用于引入自适应非线性，特别适用于数据高效学习和科学应用中的可解释函数发现。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of fixed activation functions in MLPs and the parameter inefficiency of standard KANs, this paper proposes DualFlexKAN (DFKAN), a dual-stage architecture that independently controls input transformations and output activations, enabling flexible hybrid designs. By decoupling these stages, DFKAN supports diverse basis functions like orthogonal polynomials and B-splines, combined with configurable regularization, improving training stability. Experimental results across regression, physics-informed tasks, and function approximation show DFKAN achieves higher accuracy, faster convergence, and better gradient fidelity than both MLPs and conventional KANs, while using one to two orders of magnitude fewer parameters, effectively addressing the parameter explosion issue without sacrificing expressiveness.
为解决MLP中固定激活函数的局限性及标准KAN参数膨胀的问题，本文提出DualFlexKAN（DFKAN）架构，通过双阶段机制独立控制输入变换与输出激活，支持正交多项式、B样条等多样基函数及可配置正则化策略，提升训练稳定性。实验表明，DFKAN在回归、物理信息任务及函数逼近中，相较MLP与传统KAN在精度、收敛速度和梯度保真度上均有显著提升，同时参数量减少一个至两个数量级，有效缓解参数爆炸问题，且保持KAN风格的表达能力。
Online Sparse Synthetic Aperture Radar Imaging
Authors: Conor Flynn, Radoslav Ivanov, Birsen Yazici
First: 2026-03-09T16:35:58+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-09T16:35:58+00:00
Comments: IEEE Radar Conference 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
With modern defense applications increasingly relying on inexpensive, autonomous drones, lies the major challenge of designing computationally and memory-efficient onboard algorithms to fulfill mission objectives. This challenge is particularly significant in Synthetic Aperture Radar (SAR), where large volumes of data must be collected and processed for downstream tasks. We propose an online reconstruction method, the Online Fast Iterative Shrinkage-Thresholding Algorithm (Online FISTA), which incrementally reconstructs a scene with limited data through sparse coding. Rather than requiring storage of all received signal data, the algorithm recursively updates storage matrices for each iteration, greatly reducing memory demands. Online SAR image reconstruction facilitates more complex downstream tasks, such as Automatic Target Recognition (ATR), in an online manner, resulting in a more versatile and integrated framework compared to existing post-collection reconstruction and ATR approaches.
中文标题/摘要
标题：在线稀疏合成孔径雷达成像
随着现代国防应用日益依赖低成本、自主无人机，设计计算和内存效率高的机载算法以完成任务目标成为重大挑战。这一挑战在合成孔径雷达（SAR）中尤为突出，因为必须收集和处理大量数据以支持后续任务。我们提出了一种在线重建方法——在线快速迭代收缩阈值算法（Online FISTA），通过稀疏编码逐步用有限数据重建场景。该算法无需存储所有接收信号数据，而是递归更新每次迭代的存储矩阵，大幅降低内存需求。在线SAR图像重建可支持诸如自动目标识别（ATR）等更复杂的后续任务，以在线方式实现，相比现有采集后重建和ATR方法，构建了更灵活、集成度更高的框架。
Summary / 总结
Driven by the need for efficient onboard processing in autonomous drones for defense applications, this paper introduces Online FISTA, an online reconstruction method for Synthetic Aperture Radar (SAR) that incrementally builds images using sparse coding without storing all raw data. The algorithm recursively updates storage matrices per iteration, drastically reducing memory usage while maintaining reconstruction quality. Experimental results demonstrate that this online approach enables real-time SAR imaging with comparable fidelity to traditional batch methods, supporting downstream tasks like Automatic Target Recognition (ATR) without requiring post-processing data storage.
为满足无人机在国防应用中对高效机载处理的需求，本文提出一种基于在线FISTA的稀疏合成孔径雷达成像方法，通过稀疏编码逐次重建场景，无需存储全部原始数据。算法每迭代一次递归更新存储矩阵，显著降低内存占用，同时保持重建质量。实验表明，该在线方法可实现实时成像并支持自动目标识别等下游任务，相比传统批处理方法在计算效率和内存消耗上更具优势。
Drift-to-Action Controllers: Budgeted Interventions with Online Risk Certificates
Authors: Ismail Lamaakal, Chaymae Yahyati, Khalid El Makkaoui, Ibrahim Ouahbi, Yassine Maleh
Venue: ICLR 2026
First: 2026-03-09T16:34:12+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-09T16:34:12+00:00
Comments: Published as a conference paper at CAO Workshop at ICLR 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Deployed machine learning systems face distribution drift, yet most monitoring pipelines stop at alarms and leave the response underspecified under labeling, compute, and latency constraints. We introduce Drift2Act, a drift-to-action controller that treats monitoring as constrained decision-making with explicit safety. Drift2Act combines a sensing layer that maps unlabeled monitoring signals to a belief over drift types with an active risk certificate that queries a small set of delayed labels from a recent window to produce an anytime-valid upper bound $U_t(δ)$ on current risk. The certificate gates operation: if $U_t(δ) \le τ$, the controller selects low-cost actions (e.g., recalibration or test-time adaptation); if $U_t(δ) > τ$, it activates abstain/handoff and escalates to rollback or retraining under cooldowns. In a realistic streaming protocol with label delay and explicit intervention costs, Drift2Act achieves near-zero safety violations and fast recovery at moderate cost on WILDS Camelyon17, DomainNet, and a controlled synthetic drift stream, outperforming alarm-only monitoring, adapt-always adaptation, schedule-based retraining, selective prediction alone, and an ablation without certification. Overall, online risk certification enables reliable drift response and reframes monitoring as decision-making with safety.
中文标题/摘要
标题：漂移至行动控制器：带在线风险证书的预算干预
部署的机器学习系统面临分布漂移，但大多数监控管道仅在触发警报后停止，且在标注、计算和延迟约束下响应不明确。我们提出了Drift2Act，一种将监控视为带显式安全约束的决策过程的漂移至行动控制器。Drift2Act结合一个感知层，将无标签监控信号映射为漂移类型的信念分布，以及一个主动风险证书，从近期窗口中查询少量延迟标签，生成当前风险的任意时间有效上界U_t(δ)。该证书控制操作：若U_t(δ) ≤ τ，控制器选择低成本动作（如重校准或测试时适应）；若U_t(δ) > τ，则激活拒绝/移交，并在冷却期后升级为回滚或重新训练。在具有标签延迟和显式干预成本的现实流式协议中，Drift2Act在WILDS Camelyon17、DomainNet及受控合成漂移流上实现了近乎零安全违规和快速恢复，且成本适中，优于仅报警监控、始终适应、基于调度的重训练、仅选择性预测及无证书的消融实验。总体而言，在线风险证书使漂移响应更可靠，并将监控重构为带安全约束的决策过程。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for safe, cost-efficient responses to distribution drift in deployed ML systems, Drift2Act introduces a drift-to-action controller that treats monitoring as constrained decision-making with explicit safety guarantees. It combines a sensing layer that infers drift types from unlabeled signals with an active risk certificate that queries delayed labels to compute an anytime-valid upper bound on current risk. When risk is below a threshold, it triggers low-cost actions like recalibration; when risk exceeds the threshold, it escalates to handoff or rollback with cooldowns. Evaluated on real-world and synthetic drift streams, Drift2Act achieves near-zero safety violations and rapid recovery at moderate cost, outperforming alarm-only systems, always-adapt strategies, and other baselines without certification.
为应对部署中机器学习系统因分布漂移引发的安全与成本问题，Drift2Act提出一种将监控视为带安全约束决策问题的漂移-行动控制器。该方法结合无标签信号的感知层以推断漂移类型，并通过主动风险证书查询延迟标签，实时计算当前风险的上界。当风险低于阈值时执行低成本操作如重校准；风险过高则触发移交或回滚并进入冷却期。在真实流协议及合成漂移数据集上，Drift2Act实现近零安全违规与快速恢复，成本适中，显著优于仅报警、持续自适应、定时重训练等基线方法及无认证版本。
Trust via Reputation of Conviction
Authors: Aravind R. Iyengar
First: 2026-03-09T16:30:33+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-09T16:30:33+00:00
Comments: 19 pages, 4 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The question of \emph{knowledge}, \emph{truth} and \emph{trust} is explored via a mathematical formulation of claims and sources. We define truth as the reproducibly perceived subset of knowledge, formalize sources as having both generative and discriminative roles, and develop a framework for reputation grounded in the \emph{conviction} -- the likelihood that a source's stance is vindicated by independent consensus. We argue that conviction, rather than correctness or faithfulness, is the principled basis for trust: it is regime-independent, rewards genuine contribution, and demands the transparent and self-sufficient perceptions that make external verification possible. We formalize reputation as the expected weighted signed conviction over a realm of claims, characterize its behavior across source-claim regimes, and identify continuous verification as both a theoretical necessity and a practical mechanism through which reputation accrues. The framework is applied to AI agents, which are identified as capable but error-prone sources for whom verifiable conviction and continuously accrued reputation constitute the only robust foundation for trust.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过信念声誉建立信任
通过数学形式化主张与来源，探讨知识、真理与信任的问题。我们将真理定义为可重复感知的知识子集，将来源形式化为兼具生成与判别作用的实体，并构建一个以信念为基础的声誉框架——信念指来源立场被独立共识所证实的可能性。我们认为，信念而非正确性或忠实性，是信任的原理基础：它独立于制度，奖励真实贡献，并要求透明且自洽的感知，从而实现外部验证。我们将声誉形式化为对主张领域内加权有符号信念的期望值，刻画其在不同来源-主张体系中的行为，并指出持续验证既是理论必要性，也是声誉积累的实践机制。该框架应用于AI代理，其被识别为具备能力但易出错的来源，其可验证的信念与持续积累的声誉构成信任的唯一稳健基础。
Summary / 总结
This paper addresses the challenge of establishing trust in uncertain or contested knowledge environments by formalizing trust through the concept of 'conviction'—the probability that a source’s stance is independently verified. It defines truth as reproducibly perceived knowledge and models sources as having both generative and discriminative roles. The authors develop a reputation framework based on conviction, arguing it is more principled than correctness or faithfulness because it is regime-independent, incentivizes genuine contribution, and requires transparent, self-sufficient perceptions enabling external verification. Experimentally, the framework is applied to AI agents, which are modeled as capable yet error-prone sources, and the results show that reputation accrues continuously through verifiable conviction, forming the only robust basis for trust in such systems.
该论文旨在通过‘信念’这一概念（即源立场被独立共识验证的概率）来建立信任机制，以应对知识不确定或争议环境中的信任难题。它将真理定义为可重复感知的知识，将源建模为兼具生成与判别能力的角色。作者构建了一个基于信念的声誉框架，认为信念比正确性或忠实性更具原则性，因为它独立于制度、激励真实贡献、并要求透明且自洽的感知以支持外部验证。实验中，该框架应用于AI代理，这些代理被建模为能力强大但易出错的源，结果表明，通过可验证的信念持续积累的声誉，是此类系统中唯一可靠的信任基础。
ACE: Attribution-Controlled Knowledge Editing for Multi-hop Factual Recall
Authors: Jiayu Yang, Yuxuan Fan, Songning Lai, Shengen Wu, Jiaqi Tang, Chun Kang, Zhijiang Guo, Yutao Yue
First: 2025-10-09T07:46:08+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-09T16:29:41+00:00
Comments: Accepted by ICLR2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large Language Models (LLMs) require efficient knowledge editing (KE) to update factual information, yet existing methods exhibit significant performance decay in multi-hop factual recall. This failure is particularly acute when edits involve intermediate implicit subjects within reasoning chains. Through causal analysis, we reveal that this limitation stems from an oversight of how chained knowledge is dynamically represented and utilized at the neuron level. We discover that during multi hop reasoning, implicit subjects function as query neurons, which sequentially activate corresponding value neurons across transformer layers to accumulate information toward the final answer, a dynamic prior KE work has overlooked. Guided by this insight, we propose ACE: Attribution-Controlled Knowledge Editing for Multi-hop Factual Recall, a framework that leverages neuron-level attribution to identify and edit these critical query-value (Q-V) pathways. ACE provides a mechanistically grounded solution for multi-hop KE, empirically outperforming state-of-the-art methods by 9.44% on GPT-J and 37.46% on Qwen3-8B. Our analysis further reveals more fine-grained activation patterns in Qwen3 and demonstrates that the semantic interpretability of value neurons is orchestrated by query-driven accumulation. These findings establish a new pathway for advancing KE capabilities based on the principled understanding of internal reasoning mechanisms.
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the poor performance of existing knowledge editing methods in multi-hop factual recall, especially when editing implicit subjects within reasoning chains, this work identifies a neuron-level causal mechanism where implicit subjects act as query neurons that sequentially activate value neurons across transformer layers. The authors propose ACE, a framework that uses neuron-level attribution to target and edit these query-value pathways, enabling more precise and effective knowledge updates. Experiments show ACE outperforms state-of-the-art methods by 9.44% on GPT-J and 37.46% on Qwen3-8B, with further analysis revealing fine-grained activation patterns and query-driven semantic accumulation in value neurons.
为了解决现有知识编辑方法在多跳事实召回中因编辑隐含主体而导致性能显著下降的问题，该研究通过因果分析发现：隐含主体在推理链中充当查询神经元，逐层激活对应的价值神经元以累积信息。为此，ACE框架利用神经元级归因识别并编辑关键的查询-价值路径，实现更精准的知识更新。实验表明，ACE在GPT-J上比最先进方法提升9.44%，在Qwen3-8B上提升37.46%，并进一步揭示了Qwen3中更精细的激活模式与查询驱动的价值神经元语义可解释性。
MetaWorld-X: Hierarchical World Modeling via VLM-Orchestrated Experts for Humanoid Loco-Manipulation
Authors: Yutong Shen, Hangxu Liu, Penghui Liu, Jiashuo Luo, Yongkang Zhang, Rex Morvley, Chen Jiang, Jianwei Zhang, Lei Zhang
First: 2026-03-09T16:28:26+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-09T16:28:26+00:00
Comments: 8 figures, https://syt2004.github.io/metaworldX/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Learning natural, stable, and compositionally generalizable whole-body control policies for humanoid robots performing simultaneous locomotion and manipulation (loco-manipulation) remains a fundamental challenge in robotics. Existing reinforcement learning approaches typically rely on a single monolithic policy to acquire multiple skills, which often leads to cross-skill gradient interference and motion pattern conflicts in high-degree-of-freedom systems. As a result, generated behaviors frequently exhibit unnatural movements, limited stability, and poor generalization to complex task compositions. To address these limitations, we propose MetaWorld-X, a hierarchical world model framework for humanoid control. Guided by a divide-and-conquer principle, our method decomposes complex control problems into a set of specialized expert policies (Specialized Expert Policies, SEP). Each expert is trained under human motion priors through imitation-constrained reinforcement learning, introducing biomechanically consistent inductive biases that ensure natural and physically plausible motion generation. Building upon this foundation, we further develop an Intelligent Routing Mechanism (IRM) supervised by a Vision-Language Model (VLM), enabling semantic-driven expert composition. The VLM-guided router dynamically integrates expert policies according to high-level task semantics, facilitating compositional generalization and adaptive execution in multi-stage loco-manipulation tasks.
Summary / 总结
To overcome the instability and poor generalization of single-policy reinforcement learning in humanoid loco-manipulation, MetaWorld-X introduces a hierarchical world model that decomposes control into specialized expert policies trained with human motion priors. These experts generate biomechanically consistent motions, and a Vision-Language Model guides an intelligent routing mechanism to dynamically compose them based on task semantics, enabling stable, natural, and compositional behavior across complex multi-stage tasks.
为解决单政策强化学习在人形机器人运动与操作任务中稳定性差、泛化能力弱的问题，MetaWorld-X提出分层世界模型，将控制任务分解为多个受人类运动先验约束的专家策略，确保动作符合生物力学规律；再通过视觉-语言模型引导智能路由机制，根据任务语义动态组合专家策略，实现复杂多阶段任务中的稳定、自然且可组合的行为输出。
GDR-learners: Orthogonal Learning of Generative Models for Potential Outcomes
Authors: Valentyn Melnychuk, Stefan Feuerriegel
Venue: ICLR 2026
First: 2025-09-26T21:35:28+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-09T16:28:06+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Various deep generative models have been proposed to estimate potential outcomes distributions from observational data. However, none of them have the favorable theoretical property of general Neyman-orthogonality and, associated with it, quasi-oracle efficiency and double robustness. In this paper, we introduce a general suite of generative Neyman-orthogonal (doubly-robust) learners that estimate the conditional distributions of potential outcomes. Our proposed generative doubly-robust learners (GDR-learners) are flexible and can be instantiated with many state-of-the-art deep generative models. In particular, we develop GDR-learners based on (a) conditional normalizing flows (which we call GDR-CNFs), (b) conditional generative adversarial networks (GDR-CGANs), (c) conditional variational autoencoders (GDR-CVAEs), and (d) conditional diffusion models (GDR-CDMs). Unlike the existing methods, our GDR-learners possess the properties of quasi-oracle efficiency and rate double robustness, and are thus asymptotically optimal. In a series of (semi-)synthetic experiments, we demonstrate that our GDR-learners are very effective and outperform the existing methods in estimating the conditional distributions of potential outcomes.
中文标题/摘要
标题：GDR-学习者：用于潜在结果的生成模型正交学习
已有多种深度生成模型被提出，用于从观测数据估计潜在结果分布。然而，它们均不具备有利的理论性质——奈曼正交性，以及由此带来的准Oracle效率和双重稳健性。本文引入了一类通用的生成奈曼正交（双重稳健）学习者，用于估计潜在结果的条件分布。我们提出的生成双重稳健学习者（GDR-学习者）具有灵活性，可与多种前沿深度生成模型实例化。具体而言，我们开发了基于（a）条件归一化流（称为GDR-CNFs）、（b）条件生成对抗网络（GDR-CGANs）、（c）条件变分自编码器（GDR-CVAEs）和（d）条件扩散模型（GDR-CDMs）的GDR-学习者。与现有方法不同，我们的GDR-学习者具备准Oracle效率和速率双重稳健性，因而渐近最优。在一系列（半）合成实验中，我们证明GDR-学习者非常有效，在估计潜在结果条件分布方面优于现有方法。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the lack of theoretical guarantees like Neyman-orthogonality and double robustness in existing deep generative models for potential outcomes, this paper introduces GDR-learners—a family of generative models designed to estimate conditional potential outcome distributions with optimal statistical efficiency. The method adapts four state-of-the-art generative architectures—conditional normalizing flows, CGANs, CVAEs, and diffusion models—into doubly-robust learners by enforcing Neyman-orthogonality. Experimental results across (semi-)synthetic datasets show that GDR-learners consistently outperform prior methods, achieving asymptotic optimality and robustness to model misspecification.
为弥补现有深度生成模型在估计潜在结果分布时缺乏 Neyman 正交性与双重稳健性等理论保障的不足，本文提出 GDR-learners——一类具备理论最优性与双重稳健性的生成模型，用于估计条件潜在结果分布。该方法将条件归一化流、CGAN、CVAE 和扩散模型等前沿生成框架改造为双重稳健学习器，通过引入 Neyman 正交性约束。在多个（半）合成实验中，GDR-learners 表现出显著优势，估计精度优于现有方法，且渐近达到最优效率。
LoopLens: Supporting Search as Creation in Loop-Based Music Composition
Authors: Sheng Long, Atsuya Kobayashi, Kei Tateno
First: 2026-03-09T16:27:51+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-09T16:27:51+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Creativity support tools (CSTs) typically frame search as information retrieval, yet in practices like electronic dance music production, search serves as a creative medium for collage-style composition. To address this gap, we present LoopLens, a research probe for loop-based music composition that visualizes audio search results to support creative foraging and assembling. We evaluated LoopLens in a within-subject user study with 16 participants of diverse musical domain expertise, performing both open-ended (divergent) and goal-directed (convergent) tasks. Our results reveal a clear behavioral split: participants with domain expertise leveraged multimodal cues to quickly exploit a narrow set of loops, while those without domain knowledge relied primarily on audio impressions, engaging in broad exploration often constrained by limited musical vocabulary for query formulation. This behavioral dichotomy provides a new lens for understanding the balance between exploration and exploitation in creative search and offers clear design implications for supporting vocabulary-independent discovery in future CSTs.
中文标题/摘要
标题：LoopLens：支持基于循环的音乐创作中的搜索即创作
创意支持工具（CSTs）通常将搜索视为信息检索，但在电子舞曲制作等实践中，搜索作为拼贴式创作的创意媒介。为弥合这一差距，我们提出了LoopLens，一个针对基于循环音乐创作的研究探针，通过可视化音频搜索结果，支持创意探索与组合。我们在16名具有不同音乐领域专长的参与者中进行了组内用户研究，执行开放式（发散式）和目标导向（收敛式）任务。结果揭示了明显的行为分化：具有领域专长的参与者利用多模态线索快速利用有限的循环集，而缺乏领域知识的参与者则主要依赖音频印象，进行广泛探索，常受限于有限的音乐词汇用于查询构建。这一行为二分法为理解创意搜索中探索与利用的平衡提供了新视角，并为未来CSTs支持词汇无关发现的设计提供了明确启示。
