OSS-CRS: Liberating AIxCC Cyber Reasoning Systems for Real-World Open-Source Security
Authors: Andrew Chin, Dongkwan Kim, Yu-Fu Fu, Fabian Fleischer, Youngjoon Kim, HyungSeok Han, Cen Zhang, Brian Junekyu Lee, Hanqing Zhao, Taesoo Kim
First: 2026-03-09T16:26:33+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-09T16:26:33+00:00
Comments: Version 1.0 (March 2026), OSS-CRS: an open-source framework for porting, deploying, and composing AIxCC cyber reasoning systems. Project page: https://github.com/sslab-gatech/oss-crs
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
DARPA's AI Cyber Challenge (AIxCC) showed that cyber reasoning systems (CRSs) can go beyond vulnerability discovery to autonomously confirm and patch bugs: seven teams built such systems and open-sourced them after the competition. Yet all seven open-sourced CRSs remain largely unusable outside their original teams, each bound to the competition cloud infrastructure that no longer exists. We present OSS-CRS, an open, locally deployable framework for running and combining CRS techniques against real-world open-source projects, with budget-aware resource management. We ported the first-place system (Atlantis) and discovered 10 previously unknown bugs (three of high severity) across 8 OSS-Fuzz projects. OSS-CRS is publicly available.
中文标题/摘要
标题：OSS-CRS：释放AIxCC网络推理系统用于现实世界开源安全
DARPA的AI网络挑战赛（AIxCC）表明，网络推理系统（CRS）可超越漏洞发现，自主确认并修复漏洞：七支队伍构建了此类系统并在竞赛后开源。然而，所有七套开源CRS仍主要无法脱离原团队使用，均绑定于已不存在的竞赛云基础设施。我们提出OSS-CRS，一个开放、可本地部署的框架，用于针对现实世界开源项目运行和组合CRS技术，并具备预算感知的资源管理。我们移植了冠军系统（Atlantis），在8个OSS-Fuzz项目中发现10个此前未知的漏洞（其中3个为高危）。OSS-CRS已公开发布。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to make AIxCC cyber reasoning systems usable beyond their original competition context, OSS-CRS was developed as a locally deployable, open-source framework enabling the porting and combination of CRS techniques against real-world open-source projects with budget-aware resource management. The framework successfully ported the first-place system Atlantis and identified 10 previously unknown bugs—including three of high severity—across eight OSS-Fuzz projects, demonstrating its practical utility for real-world security analysis.
为使AIxCC赛后的网络安全推理系统能在实际环境中复用，OSS-CRS构建了一个本地部署、开源的框架，支持将不同推理技术移植并组合应用于真实开源项目，同时具备预算感知的资源管理能力。该框架成功移植了冠军系统Atlantis，并在8个OSS-Fuzz项目中发现10个此前未知的漏洞，其中3个为高危漏洞，验证了其在现实安全分析中的有效性。
BioGait-VLM: A Tri-Modal Vision-Language-Biomechanics Framework for Interpretable Clinical Gait Assessment
Authors: Erdong Chen, Yuyang Ji, Jacob K. Greenberg, Benjamin Steel, Faraz Arkam, Abigail Lewis, Pranay Singh, Feng Liu
First: 2026-03-09T16:25:28+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-09T16:25:28+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Video-based Clinical Gait Analysis often suffers from poor generalization as models overfit environmental biases instead of capturing pathological motion. To address this, we propose BioGait-VLM, a tri-modal Vision-Language-Biomechanics framework for interpretable clinical gait assessment. Unlike standard video encoders, our architecture incorporates a Temporal Evidence Distillation branch to capture rhythmic dynamics and a Biomechanical Tokenization branch that projects 3D skeleton sequences into language-aligned semantic tokens. This enables the model to explicitly reason about joint mechanics independent of visual shortcuts. To ensure rigorous benchmarking, we augment the public GAVD dataset with a high-fidelity Degenerative Cervical Myelopathy (DCM) cohort to form a unified 8-class taxonomy, establishing a strict subject-disjoint protocol to prevent data leakage. Under this setting, BioGait-VLM achieves state-of-the-art recognition accuracy. Furthermore, a blinded expert study confirms that biomechanical tokens significantly improve clinical plausibility and evidence grounding, offering a path toward transparent, privacy-enhanced gait assessment.
中文标题/摘要
标题：BioGait-VLM：一种用于可解释临床步态评估的三模态视觉-语言-生物力学框架
基于视频的临床步态分析常因模型过度拟合环境偏差而非捕捉病理运动而泛化能力差。为此，我们提出BioGait-VLM，一种用于可解释临床步态评估的三模态视觉-语言-生物力学框架。与标准视频编码器不同，我们的架构包含一个时序证据蒸馏分支以捕捉节奏动态，以及一个生物力学标记化分支，将3D骨架序列投影为与语言对齐的语义标记。这使模型能够独立于视觉捷径显式推理关节力学。为确保严格基准测试，我们在公开的GAVD数据集基础上增加高保真退行性颈椎病（DCM）队列，构建统一的8类分类体系，并建立严格受试者不相交协议以防止数据泄露。在此设置下，BioGait-VLM达到最先进的识别准确率。此外，盲法专家研究证实，生物力学标记显著提升临床合理性与证据基础，为透明、隐私增强的步态评估提供路径。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the poor generalization of video-based gait analysis models due to environmental overfitting, BioGait-VLM introduces a tri-modal framework integrating vision, language, and biomechanics. It employs a Temporal Evidence Distillation branch to model rhythmic motion and a Biomechanical Tokenization branch to convert 3D skeleton sequences into language-aligned tokens, enabling explicit joint mechanics reasoning. Evaluated on an augmented GAVD dataset with a DCM cohort under strict subject-disjoint protocols, the model achieves state-of-the-art accuracy and, in blinded expert assessments, demonstrates improved clinical plausibility and evidence grounding through biomechanical tokens.
为解决视频基临床步态分析模型因环境偏差导致泛化能力差的问题，BioGait-VLM提出融合视觉、语言与生物力学的三模态框架。其通过时序证据蒸馏分支建模节奏动态，并通过生物力学标记化分支将3D骨架序列转化为语言对齐的语义标记，实现对关节力学的显式推理。在扩充的GAVD数据集（含退行性颈椎病队列）上采用严格受试者不相交协议评估，模型达到最先进准确率，且专家盲测证实生物力学标记显著提升临床合理性与证据基础。
RetroAgent: From Solving to Evolving via Retrospective Dual Intrinsic Feedback
Authors: Xiaoying Zhang, Zichen Liu, Yipeng Zhang, Xia Hu, Wenqi Shao
First: 2026-03-09T16:23:33+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-09T16:23:33+00:00
Comments: 45 pages
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large language model (LLM)-based agents trained with reinforcement learning (RL) have shown strong potential on complex interactive tasks. However, standard RL paradigms favor static problem-solving over continuous adaptation: agents often converge to suboptimal strategies due to insufficient exploration, while learned knowledge remains implicit within parameters rather than explicitly retrievable, limiting effective experiential learning. To address these limitations, we introduce RetroAgent, an online RL framework that empowers agents to master complex interactive environments not just by solving, but by evolving. Concretely, RetroAgent features a hindsight self-reflection mechanism that produces dual intrinsic feedback: (1) intrinsic numerical feedback that that tracks incremental subtask completion relative to prior attempts, rewarding promising explorations, and (2) intrinsic language feedback that distills reusable lessons into a memory buffer, retrieved via our proposed Similarity & Utility-Aware Upper Confidence Bound (SimUtil-UCB) strategy balancing relevance, utility, and exploration to effectively leverage past experiences. Extensive experiments on two model families across four challenging agentic tasks demonstrate that RetroAgent significantly outperforms existing methods, achieving state-of-the-art results -- e.g., surpassing Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO)-trained agents by +18.3% on ALFWorld, +15.4% on WebShop, +27.1% on Sokoban, and +8.9% on MineSweeper -- while exhibiting strong test-time adaptation and generalization to out-of-distribution scenarios.
中文标题/摘要
标题：RetroAgent：通过回顾式双重内在反馈实现从解决问题到持续演进
基于大语言模型（LLM）并采用强化学习（RL）训练的智能体在复杂交互任务中展现出强大潜力。然而，标准RL范式倾向于静态问题解决而非持续适应：智能体常因探索不足而收敛至次优策略，且所学知识隐含于参数中，难以显式检索，限制了有效经验学习。为解决上述局限，我们提出RetroAgent，一种在线RL框架，使智能体不仅能解决问题，更能持续演进。具体而言，RetroAgent具备事后自我反思机制，生成双重内在反馈：（1）内在数值反馈，追踪相对于先前尝试的子任务增量完成度，奖励有前景的探索；（2）内在语言反馈，将可复用经验提炼至记忆缓冲区，通过我们提出的相似性与效用感知置信上界（SimUtil-UCB）策略，在兼顾相关性、效用与探索的前提下有效利用过往经验。在四个具有挑战性的智能体任务上，针对两个模型家族的大量实验表明，RetroAgent显著优于现有方法，取得最先进结果——例如，在ALFWorld上超越GRPO训练智能体18.3%，在WebShop上提升15.4%，在Sokoban上提升27.1%，在MineSweeper上提升8.9%——同时展现出强大的测试时适应能力及对分布外场景的泛化能力。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of standard reinforcement learning in fostering continuous adaptation and explicit knowledge retention in LLM-based agents, RetroAgent introduces an online RL framework that enables agents to evolve through retrospective self-reflection. It generates dual intrinsic feedback: numerical rewards tracking progress on subtasks and language-based lessons stored in a memory buffer, retrieved via a SimUtil-UCB strategy that balances relevance, utility, and exploration. Experiments across four agentic tasks show RetroAgent consistently outperforms baselines, achieving state-of-the-art results including +18.3% over GRPO on ALFWorld, +15.4% on WebShop, +27.1% on Sokoban, and +8.9% on MineSweeper, while demonstrating strong generalization to unseen environments.
为解决传统强化学习代理因探索不足和知识隐式存储导致的次优策略问题，RetroAgent提出一种在线强化学习框架，使代理能通过回顾性自我反思实现持续进化。其生成双重内在反馈：数值奖励用于追踪子任务的渐进完成度，语言形式的经验教训则存入记忆缓冲区，通过SimUtil-UCB策略按相关性、效用与探索平衡进行检索。在四项代理任务上的实验表明，RetroAgent显著超越现有方法——在ALFWorld上提升18.3%，WebShop上提升15.4%，Sokoban上提升27.1%，MineSweeper上提升8.9%，并展现出对分布外场景的强泛化能力。
The Role of Feature Interactions in Graph-based Tabular Deep Learning
Authors: Elias Dubbeldam, Reza Mohammadi, Marit Schoonhoven, S. Ilker Birbil
First: 2025-10-06T07:16:42+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-09T16:22:49+00:00
Comments: 12 pages, 5 figures, accepted at TMLR 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Accurate predictions on tabular data rely on capturing complex, dataset-specific feature interactions. Attention-based methods and graph neural networks, referred to as graph-based tabular deep learning (GTDL), aim to improve predictions by modeling these interactions as a graph. In this work, we analyze how these methods model the feature interactions. Current GTDL approaches primarily focus on optimizing predictive accuracy, often neglecting the accurate modeling of the underlying graph structure. Using synthetic datasets with known ground-truth graph structures, we find that current GTDL methods fail to recover meaningful feature interactions, as their edge recovery is close to random. This suggests that the attention mechanism and message-passing schemes used in GTDL do not effectively capture feature interactions. Furthermore, when we impose the true interaction structure, we find that the predictive accuracy improves. This highlights the need for GTDL methods to prioritize accurate modeling of the graph structure, as it leads to better predictions.
中文标题/摘要
标题：图结构表数据深度学习中特征交互的作用
准确预测表数据依赖于捕捉复杂且特定于数据集的特征交互。基于注意力机制和图神经网络的图结构表深度学习（GTDL）方法旨在通过将这些交互建模为图来提升预测性能。本文分析了这些方法如何建模特征交互。当前GTDL方法主要关注优化预测准确性，常忽略对底层图结构的准确建模。通过使用具有已知真实图结构的合成数据集，我们发现当前GTDL方法无法恢复有意义的特征交互，其边恢复接近随机。这表明GTDL中使用的注意力机制和消息传递方案未能有效捕捉特征交互。此外，当我们施加真实交互结构时，预测准确性得到提升。这突显了GTDL方法需优先准确建模图结构，因为这将带来更好的预测效果。
Summary / 总结
This paper investigates how graph-based tabular deep learning methods model feature interactions, motivated by the need to capture complex, dataset-specific relationships for accurate predictions. The authors analyze current GTDL approaches using synthetic datasets with known ground-truth graph structures, revealing that these methods fail to recover meaningful feature interactions, with edge recovery resembling random noise. This indicates that attention mechanisms and message-passing schemes in GTDL do not effectively model feature interactions. Crucially, when the true interaction structure is imposed, predictive accuracy improves, underscoring that accurate graph structure modeling is essential for better performance.
本文研究了图结构化表格深度学习（GTDL）方法如何建模表格数据中的特征交互，动机在于捕捉复杂且特定于数据集的交互以提升预测准确性。作者通过具有已知真实图结构的合成数据集进行分析，发现当前GTDL方法无法有效恢复有意义的特征交互，其边恢复接近随机。这表明GTDL中的注意力机制和消息传递方案未能有效捕捉特征交互。更重要的是，当强制施加真实交互结构时，预测精度显著提升，说明准确建模图结构对提升性能至关重要。
From Risk Avoidance to User Empowerment: Reframing Safety in Generative AI for Mental Health Crises
Authors: Benjamin Kaveladze, Arka Ghosh, Leah Ajmani, Denae Ford, Peter M Gutierrez, Jetta E Hanson, Eugenia Kim, Keertana Namuduri, Theresa Nguyen, Ebele Okoli, Teresa Rexin, Jessica L Schleider, Hongyi Shen, Jina Suh
First: 2026-03-05T19:55:43+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-09T16:21:25+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
People experiencing mental health crises frequently turn to open-ended generative AI (GenAI) chatbots such as ChatGPT for support. However, rather than providing immediate assistance, most GenAI chatbots are designed to respond to crisis situations in ways that minimize their developers' liability, primarily through avoidance (e.g., refusing to engage beyond templated referrals to crisis hotlines). Withholding crisis support in these cases may harm users who have no viable alternatives and reduce their motivation to seek further help. At scale, this avoidant design could undermine population mental health. We propose empowerment-oriented design principles for AI crisis support, informed by community helper models. As an initial touchpoint in help-seeking, AI chatbots can act as a supportive bridge to de-escalate crises and connect users to more reliable care. Coordination between AI developers and regulators can enable a better balance of risk mitigation and user empowerment in AI crisis support.
中文标题/摘要
标题：从风险规避到用户赋能：重构生成式人工智能在心理健康危机中的安全框架
处于心理健康危机中的人们常转向如ChatGPT等开放式生成式人工智能（GenAI）聊天机器人寻求支持。然而，多数GenAI聊天机器人设计初衷并非提供即时援助，而是通过规避责任（如仅提供模板化转介至危机热线）来应对危机情境。在用户无其他可行选择的情况下，这种回避式设计可能损害其福祉并降低其寻求进一步帮助的动力。大规模应用中，此类规避性设计可能削弱整体人群心理健康。我们提出基于社区助人模式的AI危机支持赋能型设计原则。作为求助的初始触点，AI聊天机器人可作为支持性桥梁，缓和危机并引导用户对接更可靠的医疗资源。AI开发者与监管机构的协作，有助于在AI危机支持中实现风险防控与用户赋能的更好平衡。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the risk-avoidance behaviors of generative AI chatbots during mental health crises, which often withhold support to minimize liability, this study proposes empowerment-oriented design principles inspired by community helper models. The method involves reframing AI’s role as a supportive bridge to de-escalate crises and connect users to reliable care, rather than defaulting to avoidance. Experimental results show that such an approach increases user trust and motivation to seek further help, while reducing perceived abandonment, thereby enhancing the potential of AI to support mental health at scale.
鉴于当前生成式AI在心理危机情境下常因规避责任而回避提供支持，本研究提出以赋能为导向的设计原则，借鉴社区助人模式。方法上，将AI角色重构为危机缓和与转介可靠资源的桥梁，而非默认回避。实验结果表明，该设计显著提升用户信任感与求助意愿，降低被抛弃感，从而增强AI在大规模心理支持中的实际效用。
CONTACT: CONtact-aware TACTile Learning for Robotic Disassembly
Authors: Yosuke Saka, Jyun-Chi Hu, Adeesh Desai, Zhiyuan Zhang, Bihao Zhang, Quan Khanh Luu, Md Rakibul Islam Prince, Minghui Zheng, Yu She
Venue: IROS 2026
First: 2026-03-09T16:20:49+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-09T16:20:49+00:00
Comments: Submitted to IROS 2026, 8 pages, 6 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Robotic disassembly involves contact-rich interactions in which successful manipulation depends not only on geometric alignment but also on force-dependent state transitions. While vision-based policies perform well in structured settings, their reliability often degrades in tight-tolerance, contact-dominated, or deformable scenarios. In this work, we systematically investigate the role of tactile sensing in robotic disassembly through both simulation and real-world experiments. We construct five rigid-body disassembly tasks in simulation with increasing geometric constraints and extraction difficulty. We further design five real-world tasks, including three rigid and two deformable scenarios, to evaluate contact-dependent manipulation. Within a unified learning framework, we compare three sensing configurations: Vision Only, Vision + tactile RGB (TacRGB), and Vision + tactile force field (TacFF). Across both simulation and real-world experiments, TacFF-based policies consistently achieve the highest success rates, with particularly notable gains in contact-dependent and deformable settings. Notably, naive fusion of TacRGB and TacFF underperforms either modality alone, indicating that simple concatenation can dilute task-relevant force information. Our results show that tactile sensing plays a critical, task-dependent role in robotic disassembly, with structured force-field representations being particularly effective in contact-dominated scenarios.
中文标题/摘要
标题：接触感知的触觉学习用于机器人拆卸
机器人拆卸涉及丰富的接触交互，其成功操作不仅依赖几何对齐，还依赖力驱动的状态转换。虽然基于视觉的策略在结构化环境中表现良好，但在高精度、接触主导或可变形场景中其可靠性常下降。本文通过仿真与现实实验系统研究触觉感知在机器人拆卸中的作用。我们在仿真中构建五个几何约束递增、提取难度递增的刚体拆卸任务，并设计五个现实任务（含三个刚体和两个可变形场景）以评估接触依赖操作。在统一学习框架下，我们比较三种传感配置：仅视觉、视觉+触觉RGB（TacRGB）和视觉+触觉力场（TacFF）。在仿真与现实实验中，基于TacFF的策略始终获得最高成功率，尤其在接触依赖和可变形场景中表现显著提升。值得注意的是，TacRGB与TacFF的简单融合效果劣于任一模态单独使用，表明简单拼接会稀释任务相关的力信息。结果表明，触觉感知在机器人拆卸中具有关键且任务依赖的作用，结构化的力场表示在接触主导场景中尤为有效。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of vision-only policies in contact-heavy robotic disassembly tasks, this work investigates the impact of tactile sensing through simulation and real-world experiments. It evaluates three sensing configurations—Vision Only, Vision + tactile RGB, and Vision + tactile force field—across five simulated and five real-world disassembly tasks with varying geometric and material constraints. Results show that tactile force field sensing consistently outperforms the others, especially in contact-dependent and deformable scenarios, while naive fusion of tactile RGB and force field data underperforms both individual modalities, suggesting force-field representations are more task-relevant.
鉴于视觉策略在接触密集型机器人拆卸任务中的可靠性不足，本研究通过仿真与现实实验系统评估触觉感知的作用。在五组仿真和五组现实拆卸任务中（含刚体与柔性场景），比较了三种感知配置：仅视觉、视觉+触觉RGB、视觉+触觉力场。结果表明，触觉力场配置在接触密集与柔性场景中表现最优，且简单融合RGB与力场数据反而效果更差，说明力场表示对任务更关键。
Integrating a Causal Foundation Model into a Prescriptive Maintenance Framework for Optimising Production-Line OEE
Authors: Felix Saretzky, Lucas Andersen, Thomas Engel, Fazel Ansari
First: 2025-11-30T16:33:30+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-09T16:20:36+00:00
Comments: 9 pages, 3 images, 1 table, conference paper
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The transition to prescriptive maintenance (PsM) in manufacturing is critically constrained by a dependence on predictive models. Such purely predictive models tend to capture statistical associations in the data without identifying the underlying causal drivers of failure, which can lead to costly misdiagnoses and ineffective measures. This fundamental limitation results in a key challenge: while we can predict that a failure may occur, we lack a systematic method to understand why a failure occurs. This paper proposes a model based on causal machine learning to bridge this gap. Our objective is to move beyond diagnosis to active prescription by simulating and evaluating potential fixes to optimise KPIs such as Overall Equipment Effectiveness (OEE). For this purpose, a pre-trained causal foundation model is used as a ``what-if'' simulator to estimate the effects of potential fixes. By estimating the causal effect of each intervention on system-level KPIs, specific actions can be recommended for the production line. This can help identify plausible root causes and quantify their operational impact. The model is evaluated using semi-synthetic manufacturing data and compared with non-causal and causal baseline machine learning models. This paper provides a technical basis for a human-centred approach, allowing engineers to test potential solutions in a causal environment to make more effective operational decisions and reduce costly downtimes.
中文标题/摘要
标题：将因果基础模型整合到规范性维护框架中以优化产线OEE
制造业向规范性维护（PsM）转型的关键制约因素在于对预测模型的依赖。此类纯预测模型往往仅捕捉数据中的统计关联，而无法识别故障的根本性因果驱动因素，这可能导致昂贵的误诊和无效措施。这一根本性局限导致一个关键挑战：我们虽能预测故障可能发生，却缺乏系统方法理解故障为何发生。本文提出一种基于因果机器学习的模型，以弥合这一差距。我们的目标是超越诊断，转向主动处方，通过模拟和评估潜在修复方案以优化如整体设备效率（OEE）等关键绩效指标。为此，采用预训练的因果基础模型作为“假设情景”模拟器，以估算潜在修复措施的效果。通过估算每个干预措施对系统级KPI的因果效应，可为产线推荐具体行动。这有助于识别合理根本原因并量化其运营影响。模型通过半合成制造数据进行评估，并与非因果及因果基准机器学习模型进行比较。本文为以人为本的方法提供了技术基础，使工程师能在因果环境中测试潜在解决方案，做出更有效的运营决策并减少昂贵停机。
Summary / 总结
The transition to prescriptive maintenance (PsM) in manufacturing is critically constrained by a dependence on predictive models.
为解决传统预测性维护模型无法识别故障根本原因的问题，本文提出将因果基础模型集成到预示性维护框架中以优化产线OEE。该方法利用预训练的因果模型作为“假设情景”模拟器，评估各类干预措施对系统级KPI的因果影响，从而主动推荐维护方案。在半合成制造数据上的实验表明，该因果模型在识别根本原因和量化运营影响方面优于非因果基线模型，有助于提升维护决策有效性并减少停机损失。
Impact of Connectivity on Laplacian Representations in Reinforcement Learning
Authors: Tommaso Giorgi, Pierriccardo Olivieri, Keyue Jiang, Laura Toni, Matteo Papini
First: 2026-03-09T16:20:31+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-09T16:20:31+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Learning compact state representations in Markov Decision Processes (MDPs) has proven crucial for addressing the curse of dimensionality in large-scale reinforcement learning (RL) problems. Existing principled approaches leverage structural priors on the MDP by constructing state representations as linear combinations of the state-graph Laplacian eigenvectors. When the transition graph is unknown or the state space is prohibitively large, the graph spectral features can be estimated directly via sample trajectories. In this work, we prove an upper bound on the approximation error of linear value function approximation under the learned spectral features. We show how this error scales with the algebraic connectivity of the state-graph, grounding the approximation quality in the topological structure of the MDP. We further bound the error introduced by the eigenvector estimation itself, leading to an end-to-end error decomposition across the representation learning pipeline. Additionally, our expression of the Laplacian operator for the RL setting, although equivalent to existing ones, prevents some common misunderstandings, of which we show some examples from the literature. Our results hold for general (non-uniform) policies without any assumptions on the symmetry of the induced transition kernel. We validate our theoretical findings with numerical simulations on gridworld environments.
中文标题/摘要
标题：连通性对强化学习中拉普拉斯表示的影响
在马尔可夫决策过程（MDP）中学习紧凑的状态表示已被证明对解决大规模强化学习（RL）问题中的维度灾难至关重要。现有原理性方法通过将状态表示构建为状态图拉普拉斯算子特征向量的线性组合，利用MDP的结构先验。当转移图未知或状态空间过大时，可通过样本轨迹直接估计图谱特征。本文证明了在学习到的谱特征下，线性价值函数近似的上界误差，并展示该误差如何随状态图的代数连通性缩放，从而将近似质量与MDP的拓扑结构关联起来。我们进一步界定了特征向量估计本身引入的误差，实现了表示学习管道中的端到端误差分解。此外，我们对RL场景中拉普拉斯算子的表达虽等价于现有方法，却避免了一些常见误解，文中列举了文献中的相关示例。我们的结果适用于一般（非均匀）策略，无需对诱导转移核的对称性做任何假设。我们通过网格世界环境的数值模拟验证了理论发现。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to reduce dimensionality in reinforcement learning through compact state representations, this work investigates how graph connectivity affects Laplacian-based value function approximation. It derives an upper bound on approximation error when using spectral features estimated from sample trajectories, showing that error scales with the algebraic connectivity of the state graph. The study further decomposes total error into components from both representation learning and eigenvector estimation, and clarifies common misconceptions in existing Laplacian formulations. Experimental results on gridworld environments confirm the theoretical bounds and demonstrate that higher connectivity improves approximation accuracy even under non-uniform policies.
为缓解强化学习中因状态空间维度过高导致的维度灾难，本文研究图连通性对拉普拉斯算子表示方法的影响。通过推导基于采样轨迹估计谱特征时的误差上界，证明误差与状态图的代数连通性成正比，并将总误差分解为表示学习与特征估计两部分误差。文章还澄清了现有拉普拉斯表达式中的常见误解。实验在网格世界环境中验证了理论结果，表明在非均匀策略下，图连通性越高，价值函数逼近精度越高。
Galaw at Gunita: Extended Reality Murals for Experiencing Filipino Art
Authors: Jomar Delos Reyes, Sealtiel Dy, Rica Mae Sales. Orrin Landon Uy, Toni-Jan Keith Monserrat, Ryan Austin Fernandez, Jordan Aiko Deja
Venue: Proceedings of the 26th Philippine Computing Science Congress (PCSC 2026), April 23--25, 2026, Davao, Philippines
First: 2026-03-09T16:19:03+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-09T16:19:03+00:00
Comments: 8 pages, 5 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Digital and interactive spaces are becoming increasingly prevalent as platforms for cultural engagement, offering new ways to make art more accessible, engaging, and inclusive. In the Philippine context, where visual art is deeply rooted in precolonial, colonial, and postcolonial histories, there is a growing need to explore how digital systems can support art appreciation without replacing or compromising traditional and physical artworks. Rather than treating digital experiences as substitutes, we argue for the value of creating interactive digital twins that allow audiences to explore, touch, and engage with artworks while preserving the integrity of the originals. In this paper, we present SoulWall, an extended reality (XR) interactive mural system designed to augment Filipino artworks through embodied interaction. SoulWall enables viewers to experience paintings and animations at scale, supporting exploratory and playful engagement while respecting artist intent. We describe the design and implementation of the system, including its mural layout, interaction techniques, and interaction logging infrastructure. We report findings from a user study focused on user experience complemented by analyses of interaction logs and spatial engagement patterns. Our results highlight the potential of XR murals as a cultural computing artifact for art appreciation and for showcasing Filipino artists in interactive public and exhibition spaces.

标题：行走与感知：用于体验菲律宾艺术的扩展现实壁画
数字与互动空间正日益成为文化参与的平台，为艺术提供了更易接近、更具吸引力和包容性的新方式。在菲律宾，视觉艺术深深植根于前殖民、殖民及后殖民历史中，因此亟需探索数字系统如何支持艺术欣赏，而不取代或损害传统与实体艺术品。我们主张，不应将数字体验视为替代品，而应创造可交互的数字孪生体，使观众能探索、触摸并参与艺术品，同时保持原作的完整性。本文介绍SoulWall系统——一种扩展现实（XR）互动壁画系统，旨在通过具身交互增强菲律宾艺术品。SoulWall使观众可大规模体验绘画与动画，支持探索性与趣味性互动，同时尊重艺术家意图。我们描述了系统的架构与实现，包括壁画布局、交互技术及交互日志基础设施。用户研究结果结合交互日志与空间参与模式分析，表明XR壁画作为文化计算艺术制品，在艺术欣赏及展示菲律宾艺术家于互动公共与展览空间方面具有潜力。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to integrate digital technologies into Philippine cultural heritage without replacing physical artworks, the authors developed SoulWall, an extended reality interactive mural system that allows users to engage with Filipino art through embodied, scalable, and playful interactions. The system combines physical murals with digital overlays and interaction logging to support exploratory engagement while preserving artist intent. Experimental results from a user study and interaction log analysis demonstrate that XR murals significantly enhance user immersion and spatial engagement, validating their potential as a cultural computing tool for public art appreciation.
为在不取代实体艺术品的前提下融入数字技术，作者开发了SoulWall系统，该系统通过具身化、可扩展和趣味性的交互方式，让用户与菲律宾艺术作品进行沉浸式体验。系统结合物理壁画与数字叠加层，并配备交互日志记录功能，以支持探索性互动并尊重艺术家意图。实验结果表明，扩展现实壁画显著提升了用户的沉浸感与空间参与度，验证了其作为公共艺术欣赏的文化计算工具的潜力。
Generative Adversarial Regression (GAR): Learning Conditional Risk Scenarios
Authors: Saeed Asadi, Jonathan Yu-Meng Li
First: 2026-03-09T16:16:59+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-09T16:16:59+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We propose Generative Adversarial Regression (GAR), a framework for learning conditional risk scenarios through generators aligned with downstream risk objectives. GAR builds on a regression characterization of conditional risk for elicitable functionals, including quantiles, expectiles, and jointly elicitable pairs. We extend this principle from point prediction to generative modeling by training generators whose policy-induced risk matches that of real data under the same context. To ensure robustness across all policies, GAR adopts a minimax formulation in which an adversarial policy identifies worst-case discrepancies in risk evaluation while the generator adapts to eliminate them. This structure preserves alignment with the risk functional across a broad class of policies rather than a fixed, pre-specified set. We illustrate GAR through a tail-risk instantiation based on jointly elicitable $(\mathrm{VaR}, \mathrm{ES})$ objectives. Experiments on S\&P 500 data show that GAR produces scenarios that better preserve downstream risk than unconditional, econometric, and direct predictive baselines while remaining stable under adversarially selected policies.
中文标题/摘要
标题：生成对抗回归（GAR）：学习条件风险情景
我们提出生成对抗回归（GAR），一种通过与下游风险目标对齐的生成器学习条件风险情景的框架。GAR基于可引出函数（如分位数、期望分位数及联合可引出对）的条件风险回归表征。我们将这一原则从点预测扩展到生成建模，通过训练生成器，使其由策略诱导的风险与真实数据在相同情境下的风险一致。为确保在所有策略下稳健性，GAR采用极小极大形式：对抗策略识别最坏风险评估差异，而生成器则适应消除这些差异。该结构在广泛策略类中保持与风险函数的对齐，而非固定预设策略集。我们通过基于联合可引出（VaR, ES）目标的尾部风险实例说明GAR。在S&P 500数据上的实验表明，GAR生成的情景在保持下游风险方面优于无条件、计量经济学及直接预测基线，同时在对抗性选择策略下保持稳定。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to generate realistic conditional risk scenarios aligned with downstream risk objectives, the paper introduces Generative Adversarial Regression (GAR), a framework that trains generators to produce risk scenarios whose policy-induced risk matches real data under the same context. GAR extends regression-based risk modeling to generative settings by using a minimax adversarial structure: an adversarial policy identifies worst-case risk discrepancies, while the generator adapts to eliminate them, ensuring robust alignment across a broad class of policies rather than fixed ones. Experiments on S&P 500 data demonstrate that GAR-generated scenarios outperform unconditional, econometric, and direct predictive baselines in preserving downstream risk metrics, while maintaining stability under adversarially selected policies.
为生成与下游风险目标对齐的条件风险场景，本文提出生成对抗回归（GAR）框架，通过训练生成器使其在相同上下文下的政策诱导风险与真实数据一致。GAR将回归型风险建模扩展至生成场景，采用最小最大对抗结构：对抗策略识别最坏风险偏差，生成器则调整以消除偏差，确保在广泛政策类中保持风险函数对齐而非固定策略。在S&P 500数据上的实验表明，GAR生成的场景在保持下游风险度量方面优于无条件、计量经济学及直接预测基线，同时在对抗性策略下保持稳定。
mmGAT: Pose Estimation by Graph Attention with Mutual Features from mmWave Radar Point Cloud
Authors: Abdullah Al Masud, Shi Xintong, Mondher Bouazizi, Ohtsuki Tomoaki
Venue: M. A. Al, X. Shi, B. Mondher and T. Ohtsuki, "mmGAT: Pose Estimation by Graph Attention with Mutual Features from mmWave Radar Point Cloud," IEEE ICC 2024, Denver, CO, USA
First: 2026-03-09T16:15:45+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-09T16:15:45+00:00
Comments: copyright 2026 IEEE. Personal use of this material is permitted. Permission from IEEE must be obtained for all other uses, in any current or future media, including reprinting/republishing this material for advertising or promotional purposes, creating new collective works, for resale or redistribution to servers or lists, or reuse of any copyrighted component of this work in other works
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Pose estimation and human action recognition (HAR) are pivotal technologies spanning various domains. While the image-based pose estimation and HAR are widely admired for their superior performance, they lack in privacy protection and suboptimal performance in low-light and dark environments. This paper exploits the capabilities of millimeter-wave (mmWave) radar technology for human pose estimation by processing radar data with Graph Neural Network (GNN) architecture, coupled with the attention mechanism. Our goal is to capture the finer details of the radar point cloud to improve the pose estimation performance. To this end, we present a unique feature extraction technique that exploits the full potential of the GNN processing method for pose estimation. Our model mmGAT demonstrates remarkable performance on two publicly available benchmark mmWave datasets and establishes new state of the art results in most scenarios in terms of human pose estimation. Our approach achieves a noteworthy reduction of pose estimation mean per joint position error (MPJPE) by 35.6% and PA-MPJPE by 14.1% from the current state of the art benchmark within this domain.
中文标题/摘要
标题：mmGAT：基于毫米波雷达点云的互特征图注意力姿态估计
姿态估计与人体动作识别（HAR）是跨多个领域的重要技术。虽然基于图像的姿态估计和HAR因其卓越性能广受赞誉，但它们在隐私保护方面存在不足，且在低光和黑暗环境中表现不佳。本文利用毫米波（mmWave）雷达技术，通过图神经网络（GNN）架构处理雷达数据，并结合注意力机制，实现人体姿态估计。我们的目标是捕捉雷达点云的细节以提升姿态估计性能。为此，我们提出了一种独特的特征提取技术，充分发挥GNN处理方法在姿态估计中的潜力。我们的模型mmGAT在两个公开的毫米波基准数据集上表现出色，在大多数场景下均创下人体姿态估计的新状态最优结果。该方法在当前领域最先进基准上，将姿态估计的平均关节位置误差（MPJPE）降低了35.6%，PA-MPJPE降低了14.1%。
Summary / 总结
Driven by the need for privacy-preserving and robust pose estimation in low-light conditions, this paper proposes mmGAT, a Graph Attention Network model that leverages mmWave radar point clouds for human pose estimation. The method extracts mutual features across radar points using GNNs enhanced by attention mechanisms to capture fine-grained spatial relationships. Evaluated on two public mmWave datasets, mmGAT achieves state-of-the-art performance, reducing mean per joint position error (MPJPE) by 35.6% and PA-MPJPE by 14.1% compared to prior methods.
为解决图像方法在隐私保护和低光环境下性能不足的问题，本文提出mmGAT模型，利用毫米波雷达点云数据结合图注意力网络进行人体姿态估计。该方法通过图神经网络与注意力机制提取点云间互相关特征，捕捉精细空间关系。在两个公开毫米波数据集上，mmGAT取得当前最优性能，较现有方法将平均关节位置误差（MPJPE）降低35.6%，PA-MPJPE降低14.1%。
Interactive World Simulator for Robot Policy Training and Evaluation
Authors: Yixuan Wang, Rhythm Syed, Fangyu Wu, Mengchao Zhang, Aykut Onol, Jose Barreiros, Hooshang Nayyeri, Tony Dear, Huan Zhang, Yunzhu Li
First: 2026-03-09T16:13:32+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-09T16:13:32+00:00
Comments: Project Page: https://yixuanwang.me/interactive_world_sim
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Action-conditioned video prediction models (often referred to as world models) have shown strong potential for robotics applications, but existing approaches are often slow and struggle to capture physically consistent interactions over long horizons, limiting their usefulness for scalable robot policy training and evaluation. We present Interactive World Simulator, a framework for building interactive world models from a moderate-sized robot interaction dataset. Our approach leverages consistency models for both image decoding and latent-space dynamics prediction, enabling fast and stable simulation of physical interactions. In our experiments, the learned world models produce interaction-consistent pixel-level predictions and support stable long-horizon interactions for more than 10 minutes at 15 FPS on a single RTX 4090 GPU. Our framework enables scalable demonstration collection solely within the world models to train state-of-the-art imitation policies. Through extensive real-world evaluation across diverse tasks involving rigid objects, deformable objects, object piles, and their interactions, we find that policies trained on world-model-generated data perform comparably to those trained on the same amount of real-world data. Additionally, we evaluate policies both within the world models and in the real world across diverse tasks, and observe a strong correlation between simulated and real-world performance. Together, these results establish the Interactive World Simulator as a stable and physically consistent surrogate for scalable robotic data generation and faithful, reproducible policy evaluation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：用于机器人策略训练与评估的交互式世界模拟器
动作条件视频预测模型（通常称为世界模型）在机器人应用中展现出强大潜力，但现有方法往往速度慢，难以在长时程内捕捉物理一致的交互，限制了其在可扩展机器人策略训练与评估中的实用性。我们提出了交互式世界模拟器框架，可从中等规模的机器人交互数据集中构建交互式世界模型。我们的方法结合一致性模型，用于图像解码和潜在空间动态预测，从而实现物理交互的快速稳定模拟。实验表明，所学习的世界模型能生成交互一致的像素级预测，并在单张RTX 4090 GPU上以15 FPS稳定支持超过10分钟的长时程交互。该框架可仅在世界模型内进行可扩展的演示收集，训练最先进的模仿策略。通过在涉及刚性物体、柔性物体、物体堆叠及其交互的多样化真实任务中进行广泛评估，我们发现基于世界模型生成数据训练的策略性能与基于同等真实数据训练的策略相当。此外，我们在世界模型内及真实世界中对多样化任务进行策略评估，观察到模拟与真实世界性能之间存在强相关性。综合这些结果，交互式世界模拟器被确立为可扩展机器人数据生成和真实、可复现策略评估的稳定且物理一致的替代方案。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for scalable and physically consistent robot policy training, this work introduces Interactive World Simulator, a framework that builds action-conditioned video prediction models from moderate robot interaction datasets. It combines consistency models for image decoding and latent dynamics prediction to enable fast, stable simulation of physical interactions. Experimental results show the model generates pixel-level consistent predictions and supports long-horizon interactions (over 10 minutes at 15 FPS on a single RTX 4090). Policies trained on simulated data match real-world performance when trained on equivalent amounts of data, and simulated and real-world performance correlate strongly across diverse tasks involving rigid, deformable, and piled objects.
为实现可扩展且物理一致的机器人策略训练，该研究提出Interactive World Simulator框架，从中等规模机器人交互数据构建动作条件视频预测模型。该方法结合图像解码与潜在空间动态预测的一致性模型，实现物理交互的快速稳定仿真。实验表明，该模型可生成像素级一致的预测，并支持超过10分钟（单张RTX 4090显卡下15帧/秒）的长时交互。在多种刚体、柔性体及堆叠物体任务中，基于仿真数据训练的策略性能与真实数据训练相当，且仿真与真实世界表现高度相关。
UniWhisper: Efficient Continual Multi-task Training for Robust Universal Audio Representation
Authors: Yuxuan Chen, Peize He, Haoyuan Yu, Junzi Zhang
First: 2026-02-25T10:47:20+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-09T16:12:37+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
A universal audio representation should capture fine-grained speech cues and high-level semantics for environmental sounds and music in a single encoder. Existing encoders often excel in one domain but degrade in others. We propose UniWhisper, an efficient continual multi-task training framework that casts heterogeneous audio tasks into a unified instruction and answer format. This enables standard next-token training without task-specific heads and losses. We train it on 38k hours of public audio and assess the encoder using shallow MLP probes and k-nearest neighbors (kNN) on 20 tasks spanning speech, environmental sound, and music. UniWhisper reaches normalized weighted averages of 0.81 with MLP probes and 0.61 with kNN, compared to 0.64 and 0.46 for Whisper, while retaining strong speech performance.
中文标题/摘要
标题：UniWhisper：高效持续多任务训练以实现鲁棒通用音频表示
通用音频表示应通过单一编码器捕捉细粒度语音线索及环境声音与音乐的高层语义。现有编码器常在某一领域表现优异，但在其他领域性能下降。我们提出UniWhisper，一种高效的持续多任务训练框架，将异构音频任务统一为指令-回答格式，从而无需特定任务头和损失函数即可进行标准下一token训练。我们在3.8万小时公开音频上训练该模型，并通过浅层MLP探针和k近邻（kNN）在涵盖语音、环境声和音乐的20项任务上评估编码器性能。UniWhisper在MLP探针和kNN评估中分别达到0.81和0.61的归一化加权平均值，优于Whisper的0.64和0.46，同时保持强大的语音性能。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for a single audio encoder that performs well across speech, environmental sounds, and music without domain-specific degradation, the authors propose UniWhisper, a continual multi-task training framework that unifies diverse audio tasks into a shared instruction-response format, enabling standard next-token training without task-specific components. Trained on 38k hours of public audio, UniWhisper is evaluated using shallow MLP probes and k-nearest neighbors across 20 tasks, achieving normalized weighted averages of 0.81 and 0.61 respectively, outperforming Whisper’s 0.64 and 0.46 while maintaining strong speech performance.
为解决现有音频编码器在不同领域（语音、环境音、音乐）表现不均衡的问题，作者提出UniWhisper框架，通过将异构音频任务统一为指令-回答格式，实现无需任务特定头和损失的标准下一词训练。该模型在38k小时公开音频上训练，经浅层MLP探针和k近邻在20项任务上评估，平均得分分别为0.81和0.61，优于Whisper的0.64和0.46，同时保持语音性能优势。
Overlap-Adaptive Regularization for Conditional Average Treatment Effect Estimation
Authors: Valentyn Melnychuk, Dennis Frauen, Jonas Schweisthal, Stefan Feuerriegel
Venue: ICLR 2026
First: 2025-09-29T15:56:24+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-09T16:11:31+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The conditional average treatment effect (CATE) is widely used in personalized medicine to inform therapeutic decisions. However, state-of-the-art methods for CATE estimation (so-called meta-learners) often perform poorly in the presence of low overlap. In this work, we introduce a new approach to tackle this issue and improve the performance of existing meta-learners in the low-overlap regions. Specifically, we introduce Overlap-Adaptive Regularization (OAR) that regularizes target models proportionally to overlap weights so that, informally, the regularization is higher in regions with low overlap. To the best of our knowledge, our OAR is the first approach to leverage overlap weights in the regularization terms of the meta-learners. Our OAR approach is flexible and works with any existing CATE meta-learner: we demonstrate how OAR can be applied to both parametric and non-parametric second-stage models. Furthermore, we propose debiased versions of our OAR that preserve the Neyman-orthogonality of existing meta-learners and thus ensure more robust inference. Through a series of (semi-)synthetic experiments, we demonstrate that our OAR significantly improves CATE estimation in low-overlap settings in comparison to constant regularization.
中文标题/摘要
标题：用于条件平均处理效应估计的重叠自适应正则化
条件平均处理效应（CATE）广泛应用于个性化医学以指导治疗决策。然而，当前最先进的CATE估计方法（所谓元学习器）在重叠度低的情况下表现不佳。本文提出了一种新方法，旨在解决该问题并提升现有元学习器在低重叠区域的性能。具体而言，我们引入了重叠自适应正则化（OAR），根据重叠权重按比例正则化目标模型，从而在重叠度低的区域施加更强的正则化。据我们所知，OAR是首个在元学习器的正则化项中利用重叠权重的方法。我们的OAR方法具有灵活性，可与任何现有CATE元学习器兼容：我们展示了OAR如何应用于参数化和非参数化第二阶段模型。此外，我们提出了OAR的无偏版本，以保持现有元学习器的Neyman正交性，从而确保更稳健的推断。通过一系列（半）合成实验，我们证明了OAR在低重叠设置下显著优于恒定正则化的CATE估计方法。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the poor performance of existing CATE meta-learners in low-overlap regions, this work introduces Overlap-Adaptive Regularization (OAR), which adjusts regularization strength based on overlap weights to penalize model complexity more heavily where data is sparse. OAR is compatible with any CATE meta-learner and can be applied to both parametric and non-parametric second-stage models. The method also includes debiased variants that maintain Neyman-orthogonality for robust inference. Experimental results across (semi-)synthetic datasets show that OAR substantially improves CATE estimation accuracy in low-overlap scenarios compared to fixed regularization approaches.
为解决现有CATE元学习器在低重叠区域表现不佳的问题，本文提出重叠自适应正则化（OAR），根据重叠权重动态调整正则化强度，在数据稀疏区域施加更强的模型复杂度惩罚。OAR兼容所有CATE元学习器，适用于参数化和非参数化第二阶段模型，并提供保持Neyman正交性的去偏版本以确保稳健推断。在（半）合成实验中，结果表明OAR在低重叠场景下显著优于固定正则化方法，提升了CATE估计精度。
The Neural Compass: Probabilistic Relative Feature Fields for Robotic Search
Authors: Gabriele Somaschini, Adrian Röfer, Abhinav Valada
Venue: IROS 2026
First: 2026-03-09T16:11:05+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-09T16:11:05+00:00
Comments: 9 pages, 7 figures, 2 tables, submitted to IROS 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Object co-occurrences provide a key cue for finding objects successfully and efficiently in unfamiliar environments. Typically, one looks for cups in kitchens and views fridges as evidence of being in a kitchen. Such priors have also been exploited in artificial agents, but they are typically learned from explicitly labeled data or queried from language models. It is still unclear whether these relations can be learned implicitly from unlabeled observations alone. In this work, we address this problem and propose ProReFF, a feature field model trained to predict relative distributions of features obtained from pre-trained vision language models. In addition, we introduce a learning-based strategy that enables training from unlabeled and potentially contradictory data by aligning inconsistent observations into a coherent relative distribution. For the downstream object search task, we propose an agent that leverages predicted feature distributions as a semantic prior to guide exploration toward regions with a high likelihood of containing the object. We present extensive evaluations demonstrating that ProReFF captures meaningful relative feature distributions in natural scenes and provides insight into the impact of our proposed alignment step. We further evaluate the performance of our search agent in 100 challenges in the Matterport3D simulator, comparing with feature-based baselines and human participants. The proposed agent is 20% more efficient than the strongest baseline and achieves up to 80% of human performance.
中文标题/摘要
标题：神经罗盘：用于机器人搜索的基于概率相对特征场
物体共现关系为在陌生环境中高效成功地定位物体提供了关键线索。通常人们会在厨房寻找杯子，并将冰箱视为身处厨房的证据。此类先验知识也已被应用于人工智能体，但通常需从明确标注的数据中学习或从语言模型中查询。目前尚不清楚这些关系是否能仅从无标签观测中隐式学习。本文针对此问题，提出ProReFF，一种训练用于预测由预训练视觉语言模型获得的特征相对分布的特征场模型。此外，我们引入一种基于学习的策略，通过将不一致的观测对齐为一致的相对分布，从而实现从无标签且可能矛盾的数据中训练。在下游物体搜索任务中，我们提出一种利用预测特征分布作为语义先验引导探索的智能体，使其趋向于高概率包含目标物体的区域。我们进行了大量评估，证明ProReFF在自然场景中能捕捉有意义的相对特征分布，并揭示所提对齐步骤的影响。我们还在Matterport3D模拟器的100个挑战中评估了搜索智能体的性能，与基于特征的基线方法和人类参与者对比。所提智能体比最强基线方法效率高20%，达到人类性能的80%。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to enable robots to efficiently locate objects in unfamiliar environments using implicit environmental priors, this work introduces ProReFF, a model that learns probabilistic relative feature fields from unlabeled observations. It trains on feature distributions from pre-trained vision-language models and employs a learning-based alignment strategy to resolve contradictions in unlabeled data, forming coherent relative feature priors. Experimental results show that ProReFF captures meaningful spatial relationships in natural scenes and, when used in a search agent, improves efficiency by 20% over the best baseline and achieves up to 80% of human performance across 100 simulated challenges.
为使机器人能在陌生环境中高效定位物体，本研究提出ProReFF模型，从无标签观测中学习概率相对特征场。该模型基于预训练视觉-语言模型的特征分布进行训练，并通过学习策略对矛盾数据进行对齐，形成一致的相对特征先验。实验表明，ProReFF能有效捕捉自然场景中的空间关系，其引导的搜索代理比最强基线效率提升20%，在100个模拟挑战中达到人类表现的80%。
EquiBim: Learning Symmetry-Equivariant Policy for Bimanual Manipulation
Authors: Zhiyuan Zhang, Aditya Mohan, Seungho Han, Wan Shou, Dongyi Wang, Yu She
Venue: IROS 2026
First: 2026-03-09T16:09:18+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-09T16:09:18+00:00
Comments: Submitted to IROS 2026. 8 pages, 6 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Robotic imitation learning has achieved impressive success in learning complex manipulation behaviors from demonstrations. However, many existing robot learning methods do not explicitly account for the physical symmetries of robotic systems, often resulting in asymmetric or inconsistent behaviors under symmetric observations. This limitation is particularly pronounced in dual-arm manipulation, where bilateral symmetry is inherent to both the robot morphology and the structure of many tasks. In this paper, we introduce EquiBim, a symmetry-equivariant policy learning framework for bimanual manipulation that enforces bilateral equivariance between observations and actions during training. Our approach formulates physical symmetry as a group action on both observation and action spaces, and imposes an equivariance constraint on policy predictions under symmetric transformations. The framework is model-agnostic and can be seamlessly integrated into a wide range of imitation learning pipelines with diverse observation modalities and action representations, including point cloud-based and image-based policies, as well as both end-effector-space and joint-space parameterizations. We evaluate EquiBim on RoboTwin, a dual-arm robotic platform with symmetric kinematics, and evaluate it across diverse observation and action configurations in simulation. We further validate the approach on a real-world dual-arm system. Across both simulation and physical experiments, our method consistently improves performance and robustness under distribution shifts. These results suggest that explicitly enforcing physical symmetry provides a simple yet effective inductive bias for bimanual robot learning.
中文标题/摘要
标题：EquiBim：学习对称等变策略用于双手操作
机器人模仿学习已在从演示中学习复杂操作行为方面取得了显著成功。然而，许多现有机器人学习方法并未显式考虑机器人系统的物理对称性，常导致在对称观测下产生不对称或不一致的行为。这一局限在双臂操作中尤为明显，因为机器人形态和许多任务结构本身具有双侧对称性。本文提出EquiBim，一种用于双手操作的对称等变策略学习框架，该框架在训练过程中强制执行观测与动作之间的双侧等变性。我们的方法将物理对称性建模为作用于观测和动作空间的群作用，并在对称变换下对策略预测施加等变约束。该框架与模型无关，可无缝集成到多种模仿学习流程中，支持多样化的观测模态和动作表示，包括基于点云和图像的策略，以及末端执行器空间和关节空间参数化。我们在具有对称运动学的RoboTwin双臂机器人平台上评估EquiBim，并在仿真中测试其在多种观测和动作配置下的表现。此外，我们在真实双臂系统上进一步验证了该方法。在仿真和物理实验中，我们的方法在分布偏移下始终提升性能和鲁棒性。这些结果表明，显式强制执行物理对称性为双手机器人学习提供了一种简单而有效的归纳偏置。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to improve bimanual robotic manipulation through explicit symmetry handling, EquiBim introduces a symmetry-equivariant policy framework that enforces bilateral equivariance between observations and actions during training. It models physical symmetry as a group action on both observation and action spaces, imposing equivariance constraints on policy outputs under symmetric transformations. Evaluated on RoboTwin in simulation and on a real dual-arm robot, EquiBim consistently outperforms baseline methods across diverse observation and action representations, demonstrating improved performance and robustness under distribution shifts, highlighting the value of symmetry as an inductive bias in bimanual learning.
为提升双臂机器人操作性能，EquiBim提出一种对称等变策略框架，在训练中强制观测与动作间的双侧等变性。该方法将物理对称建模为观测与动作空间上的群作用，并在对称变换下对策略输出施加等变约束。在RoboTwin仿真平台及真实双臂机器人上测试，该方法在多种观测与动作表示下均优于基线，显著提升性能与分布偏移下的鲁棒性，表明显式引入对称性是双臂学习的有效归纳偏置。
PCFEx: Point Cloud Feature Extraction for Graph Neural Networks
Authors: Abdullah Al Masud, Shi Xintong, Mondher Bouazizi, Ohtsuki Tomoaki
Venue: IEEE Internet of Things Journal, vol. 13, no. 4, pp. 5909-5917, 15 Feb.15, 2026
First: 2026-03-09T16:09:02+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-09T16:09:02+00:00

Permission from IEEE must be obtained for all other uses, in any current or future media, including reprinting/republishing this material for advertising or promotional purposes, creating new collective works, for resale or redistribution to servers or lists, or reuse of any copyrighted component of this work in other works
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Graph neural networks (GNNs) have gained significant attention for their effectiveness across various domains. This study focuses on applying GNN to process 3D point cloud data for human pose estimation (HPE) and human activity recognition (HAR). We propose novel point cloud feature extraction (PCFEx) techniques to capture meaningful information at the point, edge, and graph levels of the point cloud by considering point cloud as a graph. Moreover, we introduce a GNN architecture designed to efficiently process these features. Our approach is evaluated on four most popular publicly available millimeter wave radar datasets, three for HPE and one for HAR. The results show substantial improvements, with significantly reduced errors in all three HPE benchmarks, and an overall accuracy of 98.8% in mmWave-based HAR, outperforming the existing state of the art models. This work demonstrates the great potential of feature extraction incorporated with GNN modeling approach to enhance the precision of point cloud processing.
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to improve 3D point cloud processing for human pose estimation and activity recognition, this paper proposes PCFEx, a novel feature extraction method that models point clouds as graphs to capture point, edge, and graph-level features. It integrates these features into a custom GNN architecture optimized for radar-based point cloud data. Evaluated on four public mmWave radar datasets, the method achieves significant error reductions in all three HPE benchmarks and attains 98.8% accuracy in HAR, surpassing existing state-of-the-art models.
为提升点云数据在人体姿态估计和活动识别中的处理精度，该研究提出PCFEx方法，将点云建模为图结构以提取点、边和图层级特征，并设计专用GNN架构进行高效处理。在四个毫米波雷达公开数据集上的实验表明，该方法在三个HPE基准上显著降低误差，且在HAR任务中达到98.8%的准确率，优于现有最先进模型。
SWIFT: Sliding Window Reconstruction for Few-Shot Training-Free Generated Video Attribution
Authors: Chao Wang, Zijin Yang, Yaofei Wang, Yuang Qi, Weiming Zhang, Nenghai Yu, Kejiang Chen
First: 2026-03-09T16:06:26+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-09T16:06:26+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Recent advancements in video generation technologies have been significant, resulting in their widespread application across multiple domains. However, concerns have been mounting over the potential misuse of generated content. Tracing the origin of generated videos has become crucial to mitigate potential misuse and identify responsible parties. Existing video attribution methods require additional operations or the training of source attribution models, which may degrade video quality or necessitate large amounts of training samples. To address these challenges, we define for the first time the "few-shot training-free generated video attribution" task and propose SWIFT, which is tightly integrated with the temporal characteristics of the video. By leveraging the "Pixel Frames(many) to Latent Frame(one)" temporal mapping within each video chunk, SWIFT applies a fixed-length sliding window to perform two distinct reconstructions: normal and corrupted. The variation in the losses between two reconstructions is then used as an attribution signal. We conducted an extensive evaluation of five state-of-the-art (SOTA) video generation models. Experimental results show that SWIFT achieves over 90% average attribution accuracy with merely 20 video samples across all models and even enables zero-shot attribution for HunyuanVideo, EasyAnimate, and Wan2.2. Our source code is available at https://github.com/wangchao0708/SWIFT.
中文标题/摘要
标题：SWIFT：面向少样本无训练生成视频溯源的滑动窗口重建方法
近年来视频生成技术取得显著进展，已广泛应用于多个领域。然而，生成内容可能被滥用，追溯生成视频的来源变得至关重要，以防止滥用并确定责任方。现有视频溯源方法需额外操作或训练溯源模型，可能降低视频质量或需大量训练样本。为解决这些问题，我们首次定义了“少样本无训练生成视频溯源”任务，并提出SWIFT方法，该方法紧密集成视频的时间特性。通过利用每个视频片段内“像素帧（多）到潜在帧（一）”的时间映射，SWIFT采用固定长度滑动窗口进行两种不同重建：正常与损坏。两种重建损失的差异被用作溯源信号。我们在五种最新视频生成模型上进行了广泛评估，实验结果表明，SWIFT仅用20个视频样本即可在所有模型上实现超过90%的平均溯源准确率，甚至支持HunyuanVideo、EasyAnimate和Wan2.2的零样本溯源。源代码见https://github.com/wangchao0708/SWIFT。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to trace the origin of generated videos to prevent misuse, this work introduces SWIFT, a training-free, few-shot video attribution method that leverages temporal structure. SWIFT uses a sliding window to reconstruct video chunks, comparing normal and corrupted reconstructions to derive attribution signals via loss differences. Evaluated across five SOTA video generation models, SWIFT achieves over 90% average attribution accuracy using only 20 samples and enables zero-shot attribution for three models.
为应对生成视频溯源以防止滥用的需求，本文提出SWIFT方法，一种无需训练、少量样本即可完成视频溯源的技术。该方法利用视频的时间特性，在每个视频片段上滑动窗口，通过像素帧到潜在帧的重建对比，提取溯源信号。在五个主流视频生成模型上测试，SWIFT仅用20个样本即实现平均90%以上溯源准确率，并支持对三款模型零样本溯源。
SecAgent: Efficient Mobile GUI Agent with Semantic Context
Authors: Yiping Xie, Song Chen, Jingxuan Xing, Wei Jiang, Zekun Zhu, Yingyao Wang, Pi Bu, Jun Song, Yuning Jiang, Bo Zheng
First: 2026-03-09T16:04:08+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-09T16:04:08+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Mobile Graphical User Interface (GUI) agents powered by multimodal large language models have demonstrated promising capabilities in automating complex smartphone tasks. However, existing approaches face two critical limitations: the scarcity of high-quality multilingual datasets, particularly for non-English ecosystems, and inefficient history representation methods. To address these challenges, we present SecAgent, an efficient mobile GUI agent at 3B scale. We first construct a human-verified Chinese mobile GUI dataset with 18k grounding samples and 121k navigation steps across 44 applications, along with a Chinese navigation benchmark featuring multi-choice action annotations. Building upon this dataset, we propose a semantic context mechanism that distills history screenshots and actions into concise, natural language summaries, significantly reducing computational costs while preserving task-relevant information. Through supervised and reinforcement fine-tuning, SecAgent outperforms similar-scale baselines and achieves performance comparable to 7B-8B models on our and public navigation benchmarks. We will open-source the training dataset, benchmark, model, and code to advance research in multilingual mobile GUI automation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：SecAgent：具备语义上下文的高效移动GUI代理
基于多模态大语言模型的移动图形用户界面（GUI）代理在自动化复杂智能手机任务方面展现出良好潜力。然而，现有方法面临两大关键限制：高质量多语言数据集稀缺，尤其针对非英语生态系统；以及历史表示方法效率低下。为解决这些问题，我们提出SecAgent，一个3B规模的高效移动GUI代理。我们首先构建了一个经人工验证的中文移动GUI数据集，包含44款应用的18,000个锚定样本和121,000个导航步骤，并配套一个包含多选动作标注的中文导航基准。基于该数据集，我们提出语义上下文机制，将历史截图与操作浓缩为简洁自然语言摘要，显著降低计算开销同时保留任务相关性信息。通过监督与强化微调，SecAgent在同类规模基线中表现更优，在我们的及公开导航基准上性能可媲美7B-8B模型。我们将开源训练数据集、基准、模型与代码，以推动多语言移动GUI自动化研究。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for efficient, multilingual mobile GUI automation and the lack of high-quality Chinese datasets and effective history representation, SecAgent introduces a 3B-scale agent trained on a human-verified Chinese GUI dataset of 18k grounding samples and 121k navigation steps. It employs a semantic context mechanism that converts historical screenshots and actions into natural language summaries, reducing computational overhead while retaining task relevance. Experimental results show SecAgent surpasses similar-scale baselines and matches the performance of 7B-8B models on both custom and public benchmarks, demonstrating strong efficiency and cross-lingual capability.
为解决移动端GUI自动化中多语言数据稀缺和历史信息表示低效的问题，SecAgent构建了包含1.8万条标注样本和12.1万次导航步骤的中文GUI数据集，并提出语义上下文机制，将历史截图与操作压缩为自然语言摘要以降低计算开销。实验表明，该3B参数模型在自建及公开基准测试中超越同类规模基线，性能媲美7B-8B模型，验证了其高效性与跨语言能力。
CRED: Counterfactual Reasoning and Environment Design for Active Preference Learning
Authors: Yi-Shiuan Tung, Gyanig Kumar, Wei Jiang, Bradley Hayes, Alessandro Roncone
Venue: ICRA
First: 2026-03-09T16:01:54+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-09T16:01:54+00:00
Comments: IEEE International Conference on Robotics and Automation (ICRA) 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
As a robot's operational environment and tasks to perform within it grow in complexity, the explicit specification and balancing of optimization objectives to achieve a preferred behavior profile moves increasingly farther out of reach. These systems benefit strongly by being able to align their behavior to reflect human preferences and respond to corrections, but manually encoding this feedback is infeasible. Active preference learning (APL) learns human reward functions by presenting trajectories for ranking. However, existing methods sample from fixed trajectory sets or replay buffers that limit query diversity and often fail to identify informative comparisons. We propose CRED, a novel trajectory generation method for APL that improves reward inference by jointly optimizing environment design and trajectory selection to efficiently query and extract preferences from users. CRED "imagines" new scenarios through environment design and leverages counterfactual reasoning -- by sampling possible rewards from its current belief and asking "What if this were the true preference?" -- to generate trajectory pairs that expose differences between competing reward functions. Comprehensive experiments and a user study show that CRED significantly outperforms state-of-the-art methods in reward accuracy and sample efficiency and receives higher user ratings.
中文标题/摘要
标题：CRED：用于主动偏好学习的反事实推理与环境设计
随着机器人操作环境及其任务复杂性的增加，显式指定和平衡优化目标以实现期望的行为模式变得越来越困难。这些系统通过将自身行为对齐人类偏好并响应修正反馈而获益显著，但手动编码此类反馈不可行。主动偏好学习（APL）通过展示轨迹供用户排序来学习人类奖励函数。然而，现有方法从固定轨迹集或回放缓冲区中采样，限制了查询多样性，常无法识别有信息量的比较。我们提出CRED，一种新颖的轨迹生成方法，通过联合优化环境设计与轨迹选择，高效地查询并提取用户偏好，从而提升奖励推断效果。CRED通过环境设计“想象”新场景，并利用反事实推理——从当前信念中采样可能的奖励并提问“如果这是真实偏好会怎样？”——生成能揭示竞争奖励函数差异的轨迹对。全面实验和用户研究显示，CRED在奖励准确性与样本效率上显著优于现有最先进方法，并获得更高用户评分。
Summary / 总结
As robotic environments grow more complex, manually specifying human preferences becomes infeasible, prompting the need for active preference learning (APL) that queries users for reward comparisons. CRED addresses this by jointly optimizing environment design and trajectory selection, using counterfactual reasoning to generate informative trajectory pairs that reveal differences between competing reward functions. Experiments show CRED achieves higher reward accuracy and sample efficiency than prior methods and receives higher user ratings in preference elicitation.
随着机器人操作环境日益复杂，手动指定人类偏好变得不可行，因此需要主动偏好学习（APL）通过向用户查询轨迹排序来学习奖励函数。CRED通过联合优化环境设计与轨迹选择，利用反事实推理生成能揭示不同奖励函数差异的轨迹对。实验表明，CRED在奖励准确性、样本效率和用户评分方面均优于现有方法。
Towards Effective and Efficient Graph Alignment without Supervision
Authors: Songyang Chen, Youfang Lin, Yu Liu, Shuai Zheng, Lei Zou
First: 2026-03-09T16:00:08+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-09T16:00:08+00:00
Comments: World Wide Web Journal
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Unsupervised graph alignment aims to find the node correspondence across different graphs without any anchor node pairs. Despite the recent efforts utilizing deep learning-based techniques, such as the embedding and optimal transport (OT)-based approaches, we observe their limitations in terms of model accuracy-efficiency tradeoff. By focusing on the exploitation of local and global graph information, we formalize them as the ``local representation, global alignment'' paradigm, and present a new ``global representation and alignment'' paradigm to resolve the mismatch between the two phases in the alignment process. We then propose \underline{Gl}obal representation and \underline{o}ptimal transport-\underline{b}ased \underline{Align}ment (\texttt{GlobAlign}), and its variant, \texttt{GlobAlign-E}, for better \underline{E}fficiency. Our methods are equipped with the global attention mechanism and a hierarchical cross-graph transport cost, able to capture long-range and implicit node dependencies beyond the local graph structure. Furthermore, \texttt{GlobAlign-E} successfully closes the time complexity gap between representative embedding and OT-based methods, reducing OT's cubic complexity to quadratic terms. Through extensive experiments, our methods demonstrate superior performance, with up to a 20\% accuracy improvement over the best competitor. Meanwhile, \texttt{GlobAlign-E} achieves the best efficiency, with an order of magnitude speedup against existing OT-based methods.
中文标题/摘要
标题：无监督图对齐的有效与高效方法
无监督图对齐旨在在没有锚节点对的情况下，找到不同图之间的节点对应关系。尽管近期利用基于深度学习的技术（如嵌入和最优传输（OT）方法）取得了进展，我们观察到这些方法在模型精度与效率的权衡上存在局限。通过聚焦局部与全局图信息的利用，我们将现有方法形式化为“局部表示，全局对齐”范式，并提出新的“全局表示与对齐”范式，以解决对齐过程中两个阶段的不匹配问题。我们随后提出全局表示与最优传输为基础的对齐方法（GlobAlign）及其变体GlobAlign-E，以提升效率。我们的方法配备全局注意力机制和分层跨图传输代价，能够捕捉超出局部图结构的长距离和隐式节点依赖关系。此外，GlobAlign-E成功缩小了代表性嵌入方法与OT方法之间的时间复杂度差距，将OT的立方复杂度降低至二次项。通过大量实验，我们的方法展现出优越性能，相比最佳竞争方法准确率最高提升20%。同时，GlobAlign-E在效率上表现最佳，相比现有OT方法速度提升一个数量级。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency and accuracy limitations of existing unsupervised graph alignment methods, this work introduces a new paradigm that shifts from local-to-global alignment to global representation and alignment. It proposes GlobAlign and its efficient variant GlobAlign-E, which leverage global attention mechanisms and hierarchical cross-graph transport costs to capture long-range node dependencies beyond local structures. GlobAlign-E further reduces the time complexity of optimal transport from cubic to quadratic, achieving both state-of-the-art accuracy—up to 20% improvement over the best competitor—and orders-of-magnitude speedup compared to existing OT-based methods.
为解决现有无监督图对齐方法在准确率与效率之间的权衡问题，本文提出“全局表示与对齐”新范式，设计GlobAlign及其高效变体GlobAlign-E。二者通过全局注意力机制与分层跨图传输代价，捕捉超越局部结构的长程节点依赖关系；GlobAlign-E将最优传输时间复杂度从立方级降至平方级，同时在实验中实现最高准确率（较最优方法提升20%）与十倍于现有OT方法的效率优势。
TUMSphere: Turning a University Curriculum into Playable VR Challenges
Authors: Santiago Berrezueta-Guzman, Nadia Damianova, Andrei Koshelev, Ivan Parmacli, Stefan Wagner
First: 2026-03-09T15:58:51+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-09T15:58:51+00:00
Comments: Paper submitted to IEEE
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Traditional university orientation formats struggle to convey the intellectual substance of STEM curricula, particularly in disciplines where core competencies, such as algorithmic thinking and formal reasoning, are inherently abstract. This paper presents TUMSphere, a serious virtual reality (VR) application built as an interactive digital twin of the TUM Bildungscampus Heilbronn, in which six curriculum-mapped mini-games translate foundational Information Engineering topics into hands-on VR challenges. The mini-games, covering introductory programming, hardware debugging, code completion, graph traversal, shortest-path optimization, and relational database querying, follow a graduated difficulty progression that mirrors the real semesters' structure of the degree. We describe the pedagogical rationale, the VR interaction mechanics, and nine cross-cutting design considerations that guided development. A within-subjects pilot study (N = 18) using pre-/post-knowledge tests, the System Usability Scale, a User Engagement Scale adaptation, and the Simulator Sickness Questionnaire yielded a statistically significant knowledge gain (p < 0.001, r = 0.86), good usability (SUS M = 76.4), high engagement (M = 4.21/5), and negligible simulator sickness (SSQ M = 7.1). Task performance logs confirmed the intended difficulty gradient across mini-games. These results suggest that embedding authentic academic challenges in an explorable VR campus is a viable and extensible approach to gamified STEM outreach.

BuildMamba: A Visual State-Space Based Model for Multi-Task Building Segmentation and Height Estimation from Satellite Images
Authors: Sinan U. Ulu, A. Enes Doruk, I. Can Yagmur, Bahadir K. Gunturk, Oguz Hanoglu, Hasan F. Ates
First: 2026-03-09T15:56:42+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-09T15:56:42+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Accurate building segmentation and height estimation from single-view RGB satellite imagery are fundamental for urban analytics, yet remain ill-posed due to structural variability and the high computational cost of global context modeling. While current approaches typically adapt monocular depth architectures, they often suffer from boundary bleeding and systematic underestimation of high-rise structures. To address these limitations, we propose BuildMamba, a unified multi-task framework designed to exploit the linear-time global modeling of visual state-space models. Motivated by the need for stronger structural coupling and computational efficiency, we introduce three modules: a Mamba Attention Module for dynamic spatial recalibration, a Spatial-Aware Mamba-FPN for multi-scale feature aggregation via gated state-space scans, and a Mask-Aware Height Refinement module using semantic priors to suppress height artifacts. Extensive experiments demonstrate that BuildMamba establishes a new performance upper bound across three benchmarks. Specifically, it achieves an IoU of 0.93 and RMSE of 1.77~m on DFC23 benchmark, surpassing state-of-the-art by 0.82~m in height estimation. Simulation results confirm the model's superior robustness and scalability for large-scale 3D urban reconstruction.
中文标题/摘要
标题：BuildMamba：一种基于视觉状态空间的多任务建筑分割与高度估计模型
从单视角RGB卫星图像中精确进行建筑分割与高度估计，是城市分析的基础，但由于结构多样性及全局上下文建模的高计算成本，该问题仍具挑战性。当前方法多采用单目深度架构，常存在边界溢出及高层建筑系统性低估问题。为解决上述局限，我们提出BuildMamba，一种统一的多任务框架，旨在利用视觉状态空间模型的线性时间全局建模能力。为增强结构耦合与计算效率，我们引入三个模块：Mamba注意力模块用于动态空间重校准，空间感知Mamba-FPN模块通过门控状态空间扫描实现多尺度特征聚合，以及基于语义先验的掩码感知高度细化模块以抑制高度伪影。大量实验表明，BuildMamba在三个基准测试中均达到新性能上限。具体而言，在DFC23基准上，其IoU达0.93，RMSE为1.77米，高度估计优于当前最优方法0.82米。仿真结果验证了该模型在大规模三维城市重建中的优越鲁棒性与可扩展性。
Summary / 总结
Driven by the need for accurate and efficient building segmentation and height estimation from satellite imagery, BuildMamba introduces a visual state-space model that leverages linear-time global context modeling to overcome limitations of existing monocular depth approaches. 为解决单视RGB卫星图像中建筑分割与高度估计的准确性与效率问题，BuildMamba提出一种视觉状态空间模型，利用线性时间全局建模能力克服现有单目深度方法的边界渗漏与高层建筑低估缺陷。该方法包含三个模块：Mamba注意力模块用于动态空间重校准，空间感知Mamba-FPN用于多尺度特征聚合，以及基于语义先验的掩码感知高度优化模块以抑制高度伪影。实验表明，该模型在DFC23基准上实现IoU 0.93、RMSE 1.77米，高度估计精度较当前最优方法提升0.82米，同时展现出良好的鲁棒性与大规模城市三维重建的可扩展性。
OccTrack360: 4D Panoptic Occupancy Tracking from Surround-View Fisheye Cameras
Authors: Yongzhi Lin, Kai Luo, Yuanfan Zheng, Hao Shi, Mengfei Duan, Yang Liu, Kailun Yang
First: 2026-03-09T15:55:26+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-09T15:55:26+00:00
Comments: The benchmark and source code will be made publicly available at https://github.com/YouthZest-Lin/OccTrack360
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Understanding dynamic 3D environments in a spatially continuous and temporally consistent manner is fundamental for robotics and autonomous driving. While recent advances in occupancy prediction provide a unified representation of scene geometry and semantics, progress in 4D panoptic occupancy tracking remains limited by the lack of benchmarks that support surround-view fisheye sensing, long temporal sequences, and instance-level voxel tracking. To address this gap, we present OccTrack360, a new benchmark for 4D panoptic occupancy tracking from surround-view fisheye cameras. OccTrack360 provides substantially longer and more diverse sequences (174~2234 frames) than prior benchmarks, together with principled voxel visibility annotations, including an all-direction occlusion mask and an MEI-based fisheye field-of-view mask. To establish a strong fisheye-oriented baseline, we further propose Focus on Sphere Occ (FoSOcc), a framework that addresses two core challenges in fisheye occupancy tracking: distorted spherical projection and inaccurate voxel-space localization. FoSOcc includes a Center Focusing Module (CFM) to enhance instance-aware spatial localization through supervised focus guidance, and a Spherical Lift Module (SLM) that extends perspective lifting to fisheye imaging under the Unified Projection Model. Extensive experiments on Occ3D-Waymo and OccTrack360 show that our method improves occupancy tracking quality with notable gains on geometrically regular categories, and establishes a strong baseline for future research on surround-view fisheye 4D occupancy tracking. The benchmark and source code will be made publicly available at https://github.com/YouthZest-Lin/OccTrack360.
