AtomVLA: Scalable Post-Training for Robotic Manipulation via Predictive Latent World Models
Authors: Xiaoquan Sun, Zetian Xu, Chen Cao, Zonghe Liu, Yihan Sun, Jingrui Pang, Ruijian Zhang, Zhen Yang, Kang Pang, Dingxin He, Mingqi Yuan, Jiayu Chen
First: 2026-03-09T15:52:48+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-09T15:52:48+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models demonstrate remarkable potential for generalizable robotic manipulation. The execution of complex multi-step behaviors in VLA models can be improved by robust instruction grounding, a critical component for effective control. However, current paradigms predominantly rely on coarse, high-level task instructions during supervised fine-tuning. This instruction grounding gap leaves models without explicit intermediate guidance, leading to severe compounding errors in long-horizon tasks. Therefore, bridging this instruction gap and providing scalable post-training for VLA models is urgent. To tackle this problem, we propose \method, the first subtask-aware VLA framework integrated with a scalable offline post-training pipeline. Our framework leverages a large language model to decompose high-level demonstrations into fine-grained atomic subtasks. This approach utilizes a pretrained predictive world model to score candidate action chunks against subtask goals in the latent space, mitigating error accumulation while significantly improving long-horizon robustness. Furthermore, this approach enables highly efficient Group Relative Policy Optimization without the prohibitive expenses associated with online rollouts on physical robots. Extensive simulations validate that our AtomVLA maintains strong robustness under perturbations. When evaluated against fundamental baseline models, it achieves an average success rate of 97.0\% on the LIBERO benchmark and 48.0\% on the LIBERO-PRO benchmark. Finally, experiments conducted in the real world using the Galaxea R1 Lite platform confirm its broad applicability across diverse tasks, especially long-horizon tasks. All datasets, checkpoints, and code will be released to the public domain following the acceptance of this work for future research.
中文标题/摘要
标题：AtomVLA：通过预测式潜在世界模型实现机器人操作的可扩展后训练
视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型在通用机器人操作中展现出巨大潜力。VLA模型执行复杂多步骤行为可通过稳健的指令锚定得到提升，这是有效控制的关键组成部分。然而，当前范式在监督微调阶段主要依赖粗粒度、高层次任务指令，这种指令锚定差距导致模型缺乏明确的中间指导，在长时任务中引发严重累积误差。因此，弥合该指令差距并为VLA模型提供可扩展的后训练迫在眉睫。为解决此问题，我们提出\method，首个集成可扩展离线后训练流程的子任务感知VLA框架。该框架利用大型语言模型将高层次演示分解为细粒度原子子任务，并借助预训练预测世界模型在潜在空间中评估候选动作片段与子任务目标的匹配度，从而缓解误差累积并显著提升长时鲁棒性。此外，该方法可高效实现组相对策略优化，无需在物理机器人上进行昂贵的在线回放。大量仿真验证了AtomVLA在扰动下的强鲁棒性。与基础模型对比，其在LIBERO基准上平均成功率97.0\%，在LIBERO-PRO基准上为48.0\%。最后，基于Galaxea R1 Lite平台的现实世界实验验证了其在多样化任务（尤其是长时任务）中的广泛适用性。所有数据集、检查点和代码将在本工作被接受后公开，以供未来研究使用。
Summary / 总结
To address the limitation of current VLA models lacking fine-grained instruction grounding for robust long-horizon robotic manipulation, AtomVLA introduces a subtask-aware framework that integrates scalable offline post-training. It uses a large language model to decompose high-level demonstrations into atomic subtasks, then leverages a pretrained predictive world model to score action sequences in latent space against subtask goals, reducing error accumulation. Experimental results show AtomVLA achieves 97.0% success on LIBERO and 48.0% on LIBERO-PRO, outperforming baselines, and demonstrates real-world viability on the Galaxea R1 Lite platform across diverse tasks.
为解决当前VLA模型在长时程机器人操作中因缺乏细粒度指令引导而导致的误差累积问题，AtomVLA提出一种子任务感知框架，结合可扩展的离线后训练。该框架利用大语言模型将高层演示分解为原子级子任务，并借助预训练预测世界模型在潜在空间中评估动作序列与子任务目标的匹配度，从而降低误差累积。实验表明，AtomVLA在LIBERO上成功率达97.0%，在LIBERO-PRO上达48.0%，优于基线模型，并在Galaxea R1 Lite真实平台验证了其在多样化任务中的适用性。
GALACTIC: Global and Local Agnostic Counterfactuals for Time-series Clustering
Authors: Christos Fragkathoulas, Eleni Psaroudaki, Themis Palpanas, Evaggelia Pitoura
First: 2026-03-05T15:59:33+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-09T15:52:20+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Time-series clustering is a fundamental tool for pattern discovery, yet existing explainability methods, primarily based on feature attribution or metadata, fail to identify the transitions that move an instance across cluster boundaries. While Counterfactual Explanations (CEs) identify the minimal temporal perturbations required to alter the prediction of a model, they have been mostly confined to supervised settings. This paper introduces GALACTIC, the first unified framework to bridge local and global counterfactual explainability for unsupervised time-series clustering. At instance level (local), GALACTIC generates perturbations via a cluster-aware optimization objective that respects the target and underlying cluster assignments. At cluster level (global), to mitigate cognitive load and enhance interpretability, we formulate a representative CE selection problem. We propose a Minimum Description Length (MDL) objective to extract a non-redundant summary of global explanations that characterize the transitions between clusters. We prove that our MDL objective is supermodular, which allows the corresponding MDL reduction to be framed as a monotone submodular set function. This enables an efficient greedy selection algorithm with provable $(1-1/e)$ approximation guarantees. Extensive experimental evaluation on the UCR Archive demonstrates that GALACTIC produces significantly sparser local CEs and more concise global summaries than state-of-the-art baselines adapted for our problem, offering the first unified approach for interpreting clustered time-series through counterfactuals.
中文标题/摘要
标题：GALACTIC：面向时间序列聚类的全局与局部无关反事实方法
时间序列聚类是模式发现的基本工具，但现有可解释性方法主要基于特征归因或元数据，无法识别使实例跨越聚类边界的转变。虽然反事实解释（CEs）可识别改变模型预测所需的最小时间扰动，但它们主要局限于监督设置。本文提出GALACTIC，首个统一框架，弥合无监督时间序列聚类的局部与全局反事实可解释性。在实例层面（局部），GALACTIC通过聚类感知优化目标生成扰动，尊重目标及底层聚类分配；在聚类层面（全局），为降低认知负荷并增强可解释性，我们提出代表性CE选择问题。我们提出最小描述长度（MDL）目标，提取非冗余的全局解释摘要，刻画聚类间转变。我们证明MDL目标具有超模性，使对应MDL缩减可建模为单调子模集函数，从而支持具有$(1-1/e)$近似保证的高效贪心选择算法。在UCR数据集上的广泛实验表明，GALACTIC相比适配本问题的现有基线方法，生成更稀疏的局部CE和更简洁的全局摘要，首次实现通过反事实解释理解聚类时间序列的统一方法。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the lack of effective counterfactual explanations for unsupervised time-series clustering, GALACTIC introduces a unified framework that generates both local and global counterfactuals. At the instance level, it uses a cluster-aware optimization to produce minimal temporal perturbations that move data points across cluster boundaries while preserving cluster assignments. At the cluster level, it selects representative counterfactuals via a Minimum Description Length objective, proven to be supermodular, enabling efficient greedy selection with theoretical approximation guarantees. Experiments on UCR Archive show GALACTIC produces significantly sparser local explanations and more concise global summaries than existing methods, demonstrating its effectiveness in interpreting clustering results through counterfactuals.
为解决无监督时间序列聚类缺乏有效反事实解释的问题，GALACTIC 提出首个统一框架，同时生成局部与全局反事实。在实例层面，通过聚类感知优化生成最小时间扰动，使数据点跨越聚类边界同时保持聚类分配；在聚类层面，采用最小描述长度目标选择代表性反事实，该目标被证明为超模函数，支持高效贪心选择并具备理论近似保证。在UCR数据集上的实验表明，GALACTIC 生成的局部反事实更稀疏、全局摘要更简洁，优于现有方法，首次实现通过反事实解释聚类结果。
BNEM: A Boltzmann Sampler Based on Bootstrapped Noised Energy Matching
Authors: RuiKang OuYang, Bo Qiang, José Miguel Hernández-Lobato
Venue: Transactions on Machine Learning Research (TMLR), 2026
First: 2024-09-15T16:41:30+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-09T15:51:28+00:00
Comments: Camera-ready version for TMLR (03/2026)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Developing an efficient sampler capable of generating independent and identically distributed (IID) samples from a Boltzmann distribution is a crucial challenge in scientific research, e.g. molecular dynamics. In this work, we intend to learn neural samplers given energy functions instead of data sampled from the Boltzmann distribution. By learning the energies of the noised data, we propose a diffusion-based sampler, Noised Energy Matching, which theoretically has lower variance and more complexity compared to related works. Furthermore, a novel bootstrapping technique is applied to NEM to balance between bias and variance. We evaluate NEM and BNEM on a 2-dimensional 40 Gaussian Mixture Model (GMM) and a 4-particle double-well potential (DW-4). The experimental results demonstrate that BNEM can achieve state-of-the-art performance while being more robust.
中文标题/摘要
标题：BNEM：基于自举噪声能量匹配的玻尔兹曼采样器
开发一种能够从玻尔兹曼分布中生成独立同分布（IID）样本的高效采样器，是科学研究中的关键挑战，例如分子动力学。本文旨在根据能量函数而非从玻尔兹曼分布采样的数据，学习神经采样器。通过学习噪声数据的能量，我们提出了一种基于扩散的采样器——噪声能量匹配（NEM），其理论上具有更低的方差和更高的复杂度。此外，我们对NEM应用了一种新颖的自举技术，以在偏差与方差之间取得平衡。我们在二维40高斯混合模型（GMM）和四粒子双阱势能（DW-4）上评估了NEM和BNEM，实验结果表明BNEM在性能上达到最先进水平，同时更具鲁棒性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for efficient Boltzmann samplers in scientific domains like molecular dynamics, this work proposes BNEM, a diffusion-based neural sampler that learns from energy functions rather than direct samples. It employs a bootstrapped noised energy matching technique to reduce variance and improve robustness compared to existing methods. Experiments on a 2D 40-Gaussian Mixture Model and a 4-particle double-well potential show BNEM achieves state-of-the-art performance while maintaining stability and generalization.
为解决科学计算中如分子动力学所需的高效玻尔兹曼采样问题，本文提出BNEM方法，基于扩散模型从能量函数而非直接样本中学习采样。通过引入自举噪声能量匹配技术，该方法在降低方差、提升鲁棒性方面优于现有方法。在二维40高斯混合模型和四粒子双阱势能模型上的实验表明，BNEM达到当前最优性能并保持稳定性。
LIVE-GS: Online LiDAR-Inertial-Visual State Estimation and Globally Consistent Mapping with 3D Gaussian Splatting
Authors: Jaeseok Park, Chanoh Park, Minsu Kim, Minkyoung Kim, Soohwan Kim
First: 2025-07-31T06:15:51+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-09T15:49:13+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
While 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) enabled photorealistic mapping, its integration into SLAM has largely followed traditional camera-centric pipelines. As a result, they inherit well-known weaknesses such as high computational load, failure in texture-poor or illumination-varying environments, and limited operational range, particularly for RGB-D setups. On the other hand, LiDAR emerges as a robust alternative, but its integration with 3DGS introduces new challenges, such as the need for tighter global alignment for photorealistic quality and prolonged optimization times caused by sparse data. To address these challenges, we propose LIVE-GS, an online LiDAR-Inertial Visual SLAM framework that tightly couples 3D Gaussian Splatting with LiDAR-based surfels to ensure high-precision map consistency through global geometric optimization. Particularly, to handle sparse data, our system employs a depth-invariant Gaussian initialization strategy for efficient representation and a bounded sigmoid constraint to prevent uncontrolled Gaussian growth. Experiments on public and our datasets demonstrate competitive performance in rendering quality and map-building efficiency compared with representative 3DGS SLAM baselines.
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of camera-centric 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) in SLAM—such as high computational cost, poor performance in texture-poor or lighting-varying scenes, and limited range—this work proposes LIVE-GS, an online LiDAR-Inertial-Visual SLAM framework that integrates 3DGS with LiDAR-based surfels for globally consistent mapping. The method employs a depth-invariant Gaussian initialization and a bounded sigmoid constraint to manage sparse data and prevent uncontrolled Gaussian expansion. Experimental results on public and custom datasets show that LIVE-GS achieves competitive rendering quality and faster map-building efficiency compared to existing 3DGS SLAM baselines.
为解决传统相机中心3D高斯点云（3DGS）在SLAM中存在计算量大、纹理贫乏或光照变化环境下表现不佳、作用范围有限等问题，本文提出LIVE-GS框架，将3DGS与基于激光雷达的表面点云（surfels）结合，通过全局几何优化实现高精度地图一致性。该方法采用深度不变的高斯初始化策略和有界Sigmoid约束，以高效处理稀疏数据并防止高斯点云无限制增长。实验表明，LIVE-GS在公开及自建数据集上，渲染质量与地图构建效率均优于现有3DGS SLAM基线方法。
Breaking the Bias Barrier in Concave Multi-Objective Reinforcement Learning
Authors: Swetha Ganesh, Vaneet Aggarwal
First: 2026-03-09T15:49:10+00:00 · Latest: 2026-03-09T15:49:10+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
While standard reinforcement learning optimizes a single reward signal, many applications require optimizing a nonlinear utility $f(J_1^π,\dots,J_M^π)$ over multiple objectives, where each $J_m^π$ denotes the expected discounted return of a distinct reward function. A common approach is concave scalarization, which captures important trade-offs such as fairness and risk sensitivity. However, nonlinear scalarization introduces a fundamental challenge for policy gradient methods: the gradient depends on $\partial f(J^π)$, while in practice only empirical return estimates $\hat J$ are available. Because $f$ is nonlinear, the plug-in estimator is biased ($\mathbb{E}[\partial f(\hat J)] \neq \partial f(\mathbb{E}[\hat J])$), leading to persistent gradient bias that degrades sample complexity. In this work we identify and overcome this bias barrier in concave-scalarized multi-objective reinforcement learning. We show that existing policy-gradient methods suffer an intrinsic $\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(ε^{-4})$ sample complexity due to this bias. To address this issue, we develop a Natural Policy Gradient (NPG) algorithm equipped with a multi-level Monte Carlo (MLMC) estimator that controls the bias of the scalarization gradient while maintaining low sampling cost. We prove that this approach achieves the optimal $\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(ε^{-2})$ sample complexity for computing an $ε$-optimal policy. Furthermore, we show that when the scalarization function is second-order smooth, the first-order bias cancels automatically, allowing vanilla NPG to achieve the same $\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(ε^{-2})$ rate without MLMC. Our results provide the first optimal sample complexity guarantees for concave multi-objective reinforcement learning under policy-gradient methods.
中文标题/摘要
标题：打破凹多目标强化学习中的偏见壁垒
虽然标准强化学习优化单一奖励信号，但许多应用需要在多个目标上优化非线性效用函数 $f(J_1^π,\dots,J_M^π)$，其中每个 $J_m^π$ 表示特定奖励函数的期望折扣回报。常用方法是凹标量化，可捕捉公平性和风险敏感性等重要权衡。然而，非线性标量化为策略梯度方法带来了根本性挑战：梯度依赖于 $\partial f(J^π)$，而实践中仅能获得经验回报估计值 $\hat J$。由于 $f$ 是非线性的，直接代入估计量存在偏差（$\mathbb{E}[\partial f(\hat J)] \neq \partial f(\mathbb{E}[\hat J])$），导致持续的梯度偏差，从而降低样本复杂度。 本研究识别并克服了凹标量化多目标强化学习中的这一偏见壁垒。我们证明现有策略梯度方法因该偏差而具有内在 $\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(ε^{-4})$ 样本复杂度。为解决此问题，我们开发了一种配备多级蒙特卡洛（MLMC）估计器的自然策略梯度（NPG）算法，该算法在控制标量化梯度偏差的同时保持低采样成本。我们证明该方法可实现计算 $ε$-最优策略的最优 $\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(ε^{-2})$ 样本复杂度。此外，当标量化函数为二阶光滑时，一阶偏差可自动抵消，使普通NPG无需MLMC即可达到相同 $\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(ε^{-2})$ 级别。我们的结果为策略梯度方法下的凹多目标强化学习提供了首个最优样本复杂度保证。
Summary / 总结
This paper addresses the persistent gradient bias in policy gradient methods for concave multi-objective reinforcement learning, where optimizing a nonlinear scalarization of multiple objectives introduces estimation errors due to the nonlinearity of the scalarization function. The authors identify that existing methods suffer from an intrinsic $\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(ε^{-4})$ sample complexity caused by this bias. To overcome it, they propose a Natural Policy Gradient algorithm enhanced with a multi-level Monte Carlo estimator to control the bias while keeping sampling costs low, achieving the optimal $\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(ε^{-2})$ sample complexity. Additionally, they show that under second-order smoothness of the scalarization function, the first-order bias vanishes, allowing vanilla NPG to also attain the optimal rate without MLMC.
该论文针对多目标强化学习中因非线性标量化函数导致的策略梯度方法固有偏差问题，指出现有方法因该偏差导致样本复杂度为 $\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(ε^{-4})$。作者提出一种结合多层蒙特卡洛估计器的自然策略梯度算法，有效控制标量化梯度偏差并保持采样成本低，从而实现最优的 $\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(ε^{-2})$ 样本复杂度。此外，当标量化函数满足二阶光滑性时，一阶偏差自动消失，普通自然策略梯度即可达到相同最优速率。
